#135. IHSG Anjlok 3,49%, Asing Net Sell Rp 1,52 Triliun, Ini Saham Pilihan Jumat (30/10)

📅 Oct 30, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Yudho Winarto | Editor: Yudho Winarto

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Indeks Harga Saham Gabungan (IHSG) kembali mengalami tekanan jual pada perdagangan Kamis (29/10/2026). IHSG anjlok 3,49% ke level 5.098,06 dan mengakhiri perdagangan di dekat level terendah hariannya.

Tekanan terhadap pasar saham domestik terjadi di tengah pelemahan nilai tukar rupiah yang sempat menyentuh Rp 18.185 per dolar Amerika Serikat (AS), berlanjutnya arus modal keluar atau capital outflow, serta ketidakpastian terkait potensi penurunan klasifikasi pasar Indonesia oleh Morgan Stanley Capital International (MSCI).

Baca Juga: IHSG Anjlok 3,49%, Simak Rekomendasi Saham SIDO, EXCL, dan INDY untuk Jumat (30/10)

Investor asing kembali membukukan aksi jual bersih atau net sell sebesar Rp 1,52 triliun di pasar reguler. Saham-saham perbankan berkapitalisasi besar seperti PT Bank Central Asia Tbk (BBCA), PT Bank Rakyat Indonesia Tbk (BBRI), dan PT Bank Mandiri Tbk (BMRI) menjadi sasaran tekanan jual.

Mengutip Sajian Pagi Menu Trading (SAPA MENTARI) BRI Danareksa Sekuritas, Jumat (30/10/2026), tekanan eksternal turut memengaruhi sentimen pasar domestik.

Dari Amerika Serikat, Federal Reserve (The Fed) mempertahankan suku bunga acuan di kisaran 3,75%-4,00%. Namun, keputusan tersebut tidak bulat karena dua pejabat The Fed mendukung kenaikan suku bunga sebesar 25 basis poin.

Inflasi AS yang masih tinggi juga mendorong pasar memperhitungkan kemungkinan kenaikan suku bunga pada Desember mendatang. Kondisi tersebut berpotensi memperpanjang tekanan terhadap pasar negara berkembang, termasuk Indonesia.

Di dalam negeri, pelemahan rupiah dan berlanjutnya arus modal asing keluar menjadi risiko utama bagi pergerakan IHSG.

Investor juga akan mencermati sejumlah agenda ekonomi pekan depan, termasuk rilis data perdagangan Indonesia periode September 2026 dan inflasi Oktober 2026. Selain itu, perkembangan kebijakan Bursa Efek Indonesia (BEI) dan MSCI turut menjadi perhatian pelaku pasar.

Baca Juga: Laba Semakin Besar, Tapi Harga Saham GOTO Malah Loyo

Secara teknikal, IHSG kembali melanjutkan tren bearish setelah menembus level support 5.170 dan ditutup di posisi 5.098,06.

Indikator Moving Average Convergence Divergence (MACD) masih berada di area negatif dan kembali menunjukkan pelemahan momentum.

Sementara itu, posisi IHSG yang jauh di bawah Moving Average 20 hari (MA20) di sekitar 5.646 mempertegas dominasi tren penurunan.

BRI Danareksa Sekuritas memperkirakan, IHSG berpotensi menguji level support Fibonacci berikutnya di 5.026. Jika tekanan jual berlanjut, indeks berpeluang bergerak menuju support lanjutan di 4.942.

Sentimen eksternal juga masih perlu dicermati setelah bursa saham Wall Street ditutup melemah pada perdagangan Kamis (29/10/2026).

Dow Jones Industrial Average turun 0,25% ke level 52.342,56. Indeks S&P 500 melemah 0,30% menjadi 7.720,39, sedangkan Nasdaq Composite terkoreksi 1,38% ke posisi 26.184,72.

Baca Juga: Daftar Saham PER Terendah Tertinggi LQ45 (29 Oktober 2026), Asing Net Sell Rp1,076 T

Di tengah tekanan pasar, BRI Danareksa Sekuritas memberikan sejumlah rekomendasi saham untuk perdagangan Jumat (30/10/2026), yakni JARR, SSMS, CMRY, dan TOBA.

JARR menunjukkan upaya rebound dari area support 2.100-2.200 setelah mengalami koreksi dalam pola descending channel. Secara teknikal, saham ini dinilai menarik untuk strategi beli pada rentang 2.100-2.200, dengan target harga di level resistance 2.360 dan 2.585. Rekomendasi beli: 2.100-2.200 Target resistance 1: 2.360 Target resistance 2: 2.585 Stop loss: di bawah 2.090

2. PT Sawit Sumbermas Sarana Tbk (SSMS) – BUY

SSMS menunjukkan momentum rebound setelah menguat ke level 985. Penguatan tersebut didukung peningkatan volume transaksi dan pemulihan harga dari area support 800-895. Secara teknikal, SSMS menarik untuk strategi beli pada rentang 950-985. Target resistance terdekat berada di 1.010 dan target berikutnya di 1.060. Rekomendasi beli: 950-985 Target resistance 1: 1.010 Target resistance 2: 1.060 Stop loss: di bawah 930

3. PT Cisarua Mountain Dairy Tbk (CMRY) – BUY

CMRY berpotensi melanjutkan penguatan setelah pulih dari koreksi tajam. Harga terakhir berada di level 4.250 atau naik 2,41%. Strategi yang disarankan adalah buy on break apabila harga mampu menembus level 4.250. Target penguatan berikutnya berada di 4.300 dan 4.365. Meski demikian, CMRY masih menghadapi resistance MA20 di sekitar 4.312. Strategi beli: Buy on break di atas 4.250 Target resistance 1: 4.300 Target resistance 2: 4.365 Stop loss: di bawah 4.200

4. PT TBS Energi Utama Tbk (TOBA) – SELL/AVOID

Berbeda dengan tiga saham sebelumnya, TOBA masih menunjukkan tekanan bearish. Saham ini turun 6,61% ke level 424 setelah mengalami penolakan kuat di area resistance 620, disertai lonjakan volume jual. Harga TOBA juga kembali berada di bawah MA20 di level 432. Kondisi tersebut mengindikasikan tekanan jual masih dominan.

Investor disarankan menghindari atau menjual saham TOBA dengan mencermati area support berikutnya di 340 dan 295. Harga terakhir: 424 Rekomendasi: SELL/AVOID Support berikutnya: 340 Support lanjutan: 295

#154. Laba Semakin Besar, Tapi Harga Saham GOTO Malah Loyo

📅 Oct 30, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Yuliana Hema | Editor: Adi Wikanto

KONTAN.CO.ID - Jakarta. Kinerja PT GoTo Gojek Tokopedia Tbk (GOTO) hingga kuartal III 2026 semakin mentereng karena berhasil memperbesar perolehan laba. Sayangnya, perbaikan kinerja belum diimbangi dengan performa harga saham GOTO yang belakangan masih melemah.

Melansir laporan keuangan per 30 September 2026 yang diumumkan, Kamis (29/10/2026), laba diatribusikan kepada entitas induk GOTO mencapai Rp 1,16 triliun. Berbalik dari rugi bersih sebesar Rp 775,55 miliar pada periode sama tahun 2025.

Kinerja positif GOTO juga tercermin dari sisi operasional. Emiten teknologi ini tercatat membukukan laba usaha sebesar Rp 864,17 miliar per September 2026. Di periode sama tahun 2025, GOTO mencatat rugi laba usaha Rp 222,36 miliar.

Pencapaian laba didukung pendapatan bersih GOTO yang mencapai Rp 16,54 triliun di periode Januari–September 2026, tumbuh 24,47% secara tahunan atau Year on Year (YoY) dari Rp 13,29 triliun di kuartal III 2025.

Rinciannya, jasa pengiriman berkontribusi sebesar Rp 4,73 triliun atau naik 11,39% YoY, imbalan jasa menyumbang pendapatan sebesar Rp 4,59 triliun dan pinjaman sebesar Rp 4,24 triliun atau 58,57% naik secara tahunan.

Baca Juga: ​Bos Danantara Pandu Sjahrir Akui Punya 27,5% Saham Supra Sekuritas yang Dihukum OJK

GOTO juga memperoleh pendapatan imbalan jasa e-commerce dari operasional PT Tokopedia yang kini dikendalikan oleh TikTok sebesar Rp 786,17 miliar atau 25,22% tumbuh YoY. Imbalan iklan menyumbang Rp 360,35 miliar dan pendapatan lain-lain Rp 1,82 triliun.

Lalu jumlah biasa dan beban yang ditanggung GOTO mencapai Rp 15,41 triliun di selama periode Januari–September 2026. Angka ini naik 14,04% secara tahunan dari posisi yang sama di 2025 sebesar Rp 13,51 triliun.

Dari sisi neraca, GOTO tercatat memiliki jumlah aset sebesar Rp 48,56 triliun per 30 September 2026. Masih di periode yang sama, total liabilitas dan ekuitas GOTO masing-masing mencapai Rp 19,11 triliun dan Rp 29,54  triliun.

Meski kinerja semakin bagus, harga saham GOTO malah loyo. Pada perdagangan Kamis 29 Oktober 2026, harga saham GOTO ditutup di Rp 23 turun 1 poin atau 4,17% secara harian. Selama perdagangan 30 hari terakhir, harga saham GOTO melorot 28,13%.

Tonton: Indonesia Disebut Bakal Dapat 30 Juta Barel Minyak Gratis, Benarkah Tanpa Biaya?

Dengan pencapaian tersebut, manajemen GOTO menaikkan target kinerja keuangan untuk tahun buku 2026. Emiten teknologi ini memproyeksikan adjusted Earnings Before Interest, Taxes, Depreciation, and Amortization (EBITDA) mencapai Rp 3,5 triliun hingga Rp 3,7 triliun.

Target tersebut meningkat dibandingkan proyeksi sebelumnya sebesar Rp 3,2 triliun hingga Rp 3,4 triliun.

Direktur Utama GoTo Hans Patuwo mengatakan, penyesuaian target mencerminkan kinerja perseroan yang tetap solid. Meski demikian, GoTo tetap memperhitungkan sejumlah risiko, mulai dari persaingan usaha, kenaikan biaya hingga ketidakpastian kondisi makroekonomi.

“Penyesuaian ini mencerminkan kinerja yang tetap kuat, sekaligus untuk mengakomodasi potensi regulasi ke depan terkait komisi 8%,” jelas Hans dalam keterangan resmi, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Sejalan dengan kenaikan target EBITDA grup, GOTO juga merevisi proyeksi kinerja sejumlah segmen bisnisnya.

Untuk segmen financial technology (fintech), perseroan menaikkan target adjusted EBITDA menjadi Rp 1,9 triliun hingga Rp 2 triliun dari sebelumnya Rp 1,7 triliun hingga Rp 1,8 triliun.

Sementara itu, target adjusted EBITDA segmen on demand services (ODS) diperluas menjadi Rp 1,4 triliun hingga Rp 1,6 triliun. Sebelumnya, GOTO mematok target di kisaran Rp 1,4 triliun hingga Rp 1,5 triliun.

Adapun hingga kuartal III-2026, GOTO telah membukukan adjusted EBITDA sebesar Rp 950 miliar. Secara kumulatif sepanjang Januari-September 2026, realisasinya mencapai Rp 2,87 triliun atau meningkat 115% secara tahunan (year on year/YoY).

“Kami akan terus menjaga fundamental bisnis tetap sehat. Dengan bisnis yang sehat, kami dapat terus tumbuh dan mendukung seluruh pihak dalam ekosistem kami, termasuk jutaan mitra driver,” kata Hans.

GoTo menegaskan akan tetap berfokus pada disiplin operasional dan alokasi modal untuk menjaga keberlanjutan pertumbuhan bisnis di tengah dinamika industri teknologi dan transportasi daring.   Menkeu Buka Jalan Percepatan Restitusi, Akankah Arus Kas Usaha Terbantu? © 2026 Konten oleh Kontan

#163. &#8203;Bos Danantara Pandu Sjahrir Akui Punya 27,5% Saham Supra Sekuritas yang Dihukum OJK

📅 Oct 30, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Pulina Nityakanti | Editor: Adi Wikanto

KONTAN.CO.ID - Jakarta. Chief Investment Officer (CIO) Danantara Indonesia Pandu Patria Sjahrir buka suara mengenai kepemilikan saham di PT Supra Sekuritas Indonesia yang dijatuhkan sanksi oleh Otoritas Jasa Keuangan (OJK) karena pelanggaran aturan pasar modal.

Pandu memiliki 27,5% saham Supra Sekuritas melalui PT Asha Artha Sehati. Berdasarkan laporan Bloomberg, Pandu menguasai 99,99% saham Asha Artha Sehati dan kepemilikan tersebut telah tercatat sejak akhir 2023.

Kepemilikan Pandu menjadi sorotan setelah OJK mengumumkan sanksi terhadap sejumlah pihak terkait pelanggaran di pasar modal. Salah satu entitas yang disebut dalam pengumuman OJK adalah PT SS yang tak lain PT Supra Sekuritas Indonesia.

Menanggapi sorotan tersebut, Pandu menegaskan bahwa investasi yang dimilikinya di berbagai perusahaan selama puluhan tahun tidak selalu membuatnya terlibat dalam pengelolaan perusahaan.

“Saya percaya, perusahaan-perusahaan di Indonesia perlu untuk terus naik kelas, dan salah satu kuncinya adalah lewat permodalan atau investasi,” tulis Pandu melalui akun Instagram resminya, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Baca Juga: Harga Emas Dunia dan Antam Terkoreksi, Begini Proyeksi hingga Akhir Tahun

Pandu sebut kepemilikannya bersifat pasif

Pandu menjelaskan, selama berkarier di dunia investasi, termasuk belasan tahun di perusahaan modal ventura, dirinya telah menanamkan modal di berbagai perusahaan dari sektor teknologi hingga keuangan.

Menurut dia, dalam sejumlah investasi tersebut, posisinya hanya sebagai pemegang saham minoritas dan pasif. Dengan demikian, ia mengaku tidak terlibat dalam operasional harian, jajaran direksi maupun komisaris, serta pengambilan keputusan perusahaan.

Pandu menyebut kondisi tersebut juga berlaku untuk kepemilikannya di Supra Sekuritas. “Sederhananya, setiap orang bisa saja memiliki saham sebuah bank, bukan artinya dia menjadi bagian dari manajemennya,” tulis Pandu.

Ia menegaskan, kepemilikan saham harus dibedakan dari tanggung jawab atas tindakan perusahaan. Menurut Pandu, temuan dan sanksi yang diumumkan OJK berkaitan dengan aktivitas perusahaan dan semestinya ditangani melalui proses regulasi yang berlaku.

Pandu juga menyatakan dukungannya terhadap transparansi, tata kelola, dan upaya meningkatkan kepercayaan investor di pasar modal Indonesia.

“Saya dukung OJK dan otoritas terkait menjatuhkan sanksi ke semua yang terlibat dalam praktik-praktik tidak benar di pasar modal kita, tanpa terkecuali,” ujarnya.

Tonton: TikTok Hapus 12,2 Juta Video di Indonesia, Perkuat Keamanan Brand dan Transparansi AI

OJK jatuhkan sanksi terkait kasus DSSA, BREN, dan CUAN

Sebelumnya, OJK mengumumkan sanksi administratif terhadap sejumlah pihak terkait pelanggaran di pasar modal yang melibatkan tiga emiten, yakni PT Dian Swastatika Sentosa Tbk (DSSA), PT Barito Renewables Energy Tbk (BREN), dan PT Petrindo Jaya Kreasi Tbk (CUAN).

Berdasarkan siaran pers OJK pada 21 Oktober 2026, regulator menjatuhkan denda Rp 1,5 triliun kepada PT SMT terkait pelanggaran manipulasi perdagangan saham DSSA. Dalam perkara yang sama, OJK juga membekukan izin Wakil Penjamin Emisi Efek berinisial R selama satu tahun. OJK menyebut R saat ini menjabat sebagai direktur PT SS.

Pada kasus DSSA, OJK mengenakan sanksi admistratif berupa denda atas pelanggaran ketentuan administratif yang diberikan kepada sembilan Anggota Bursa. Mereka adalah PT SSI, PT SS, PT ALS, PT BS, PT AMS, PT TSI, PT BCS, PT WSI dan PT RSI.

Sementara itu, terkait penawaran umum saham BREN, OJK menjatuhkan denda Rp 500 miliar kepada AH Ltd atas pelanggaran berupa pengaburan informasi pihak pemesan saham.

Dalam kasus penawaran umum perdana saham CUAN, regulator menjatuhkan denda Rp 100 miliar kepada TC Pte Ltd atas pelanggaran serupa.

Dengan demikian, total denda yang diumumkan OJK untuk tiga perkara tersebut mencapai Rp 2,1 triliun.

#172. Daftar Saham PER Terendah Tertinggi LQ45 (29 Oktober 2026), Asing Net Sell Rp1,076 T

📅 Oct 30, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Hasbi Maulana | Editor: Hasbi Maulana

KONTAN.CO.ID - Perdagangan di Bursa Efek Indonesia (BEI) untuk saham-saham anggota indeks LQ45 pada 29 Oktober 2026 diwarnai gelombang tekanan jual massal yang menekan seluruh saham berkapitalisasi besar di tengah nilai transaksi bursa yang tercatat sebesar Rp11,3 triliun.

Investor asing mencatat aksi jual bersih (net sell) senilai Rp1,08 triliun di tengah volume transaksi bursa yang menembus 23,28 miliar saham.

Rasio harga berbanding laba (price earning ratio/PER) menunjukkan berapa kali lipat harga saham dibanding laba bersih per sahamnya.

Rasio harga berbanding nilai buku (price to book value/PBV) membandingkan harga saham dengan nilai aset bersih perusahaan per saham.

Seluruh saham anggota indeks LQ45 dengan PER terendah kompak mengalami tekanan jual masif pada perdagangan 29 Oktober.

PT Perusahaan Gas Negara Tbk (PGAS) memimpin koreksi terdalam di kelompok ini setelah merosot 5,08%, disusul PT Aneka Tambang Tbk (ANTM) yang turun 4,64% dan PT Medco Energi Internasional Tbk (MEDC) yang melemah 4,63%.

Tekanan serupa juga menghantam emiten perbankan dan energi lainnya di tengah sentimen pelemahan pasar secara luas.

Tekanan jual serentak turut melanda kelompok saham PER tertinggi di bursa pada perdagangan 29 Oktober. PT Barito Pacific Tbk (BRPT) mengalami kejatuhan terparah dan keluar sebagai penurun terdalam di antara seluruh 20 saham gabungan setelah ambles 6,88%.

Tekanan jual berat lanjutan juga menghantam PT Indika Energy Tbk (INDY) yang merosot 6,77%, PT Merdeka Battery Materials Tbk (MBMA) yang turun 6,13%, serta PT Darma Henwa Tbk (DEWA) yang melemah 5,83%.

#181. Wall Street Ditutup Merah (29/10), Saham Alphabet Ambles 12,6% Gegara Belanja AI

📅 Oct 30, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

KONTAN.CO.ID - Bursa saham Amerika Serikat (AS) ditutup melemah pada perdagangan Kamis (29/10/2026), seiring kekhawatiran investor terhadap besarnya belanja kecerdasan buatan (artificial intelligence/AI) perusahaan teknologi kembali mencuat.

Sentimen negatif ini muncul setelah kinerja pendapatan Alphabet meleset dari ekspektasi pasar, di tengah rilis data inflasi dan pertumbuhan ekonomi AS.

Indeks Nasdaq Composite mencatat penurunan terdalam karena tekanan pada saham-saham teknologi, terutama Alphabet. Sementara itu, indeks Russell 2000 yang mengukur kinerja saham berkapitalisasi kecil justru menguat 1%.

Baca Juga: Intip Saham yang Banyak Diburu Asing Saat IHSG Anjlok 3,48% ke 5.098, Kamis (29/10)

Melansir Reuters, pada penutupan perdagangan, Dow Jones Industrial Average turun 132,54 poin atau 0,25% ke level 52.342,56. Indeks S&P 500 melemah 23,59 poin atau 0,30% menjadi 7.720,39, sedangkan Nasdaq Composite merosot 365,13 poin atau 1,37% ke posisi 26.184,72.

Saham Alphabet anjlok 12,6% setelah perusahaan induk Google tersebut melaporkan pendapatan kuartalan di bawah ekspektasi. Kekhawatiran investor juga meningkat karena perusahaan mempercepat pengeluaran kas untuk pengembangan AI.

Tekanan terhadap Alphabet turut menyeret sektor jasa komunikasi. Investor kini semakin selektif dalam menilai perusahaan teknologi yang agresif berinvestasi pada AI, terutama dalam membedakan emiten yang mampu membiayai ekspansi dari arus kas internal dan perusahaan yang bergantung pada utang.

Analis strategi investasi Baird Ross Mayfield mengatakan, pelemahan pasar yang disertai penguatan saham berkapitalisasi kecil menunjukkan bahwa kekhawatiran terhadap prospek AI belum tentu berdampak pada seluruh sektor pasar saham.

"Masih banyak pilihan investasi lain di luar perdagangan saham AI yang memiliki prospek cukup baik," ujar Mayfield.

Saham semikonduktor juga menghentikan tren kenaikan selama empat hari berturut-turut. Indeks saham sektor tersebut turun 1,4% seiring meningkatnya kehati-hatian investor terhadap kebutuhan pendanaan belanja AI yang besar.

Baca Juga: Asing Net Sell Rp 1,08 Triliun Saat IHSG Ambruk 3,48%, Cek Saham yang Banyak Dilepas

Data ekonomi dan kebijakan The Fed menjadi sorotan

Tekanan di pasar saham terjadi sehari setelah bank sentral AS, Federal Reserve (The Fed), memutuskan mempertahankan suku bunga acuannya.

The Fed mengakui tekanan harga masih tinggi dan menyatakan akan mengumpulkan lebih banyak informasi sebelum menentukan langkah kebijakan berikutnya.

Data ekonomi yang dirilis Kamis menunjukkan ekonomi AS tumbuh lebih cepat dari perkiraan pada kuartal III-2026.

Namun, pertumbuhan harga juga menunjukkan percepatan, di tengah gangguan pasokan minyak akibat konflik yang mendorong tekanan inflasi lebih luas.

Kondisi tersebut menambah tantangan bagi The Fed dalam menentukan arah suku bunga, karena pertumbuhan ekonomi yang kuat berjalan beriringan dengan tekanan inflasi yang belum mereda.

Baca Juga: Harga Emas Dunia dan Antam Terkoreksi, Begini Proyeksi hingga Akhir Tahun

Di tengah pelemahan pasar, sejumlah saham bergerak berlawanan arah mengikuti hasil kinerja keuangan masing-masing perusahaan.

Saham Caterpillar turun 9,3% meskipun produsen alat berat tersebut membukukan laba kuartalan di atas perkiraan analis. Investor mencermati keputusan perusahaan yang mempertahankan target pertumbuhannya.

Sebaliknya, saham Eli Lilly menguat 6,6% setelah perusahaan farmasi itu menaikkan proyeksi pendapatan, didukung penjualan obat penurun berat badan. Saham Ford juga melonjak 12,4% setelah pendapatan kuartal III melampaui ekspektasi pasar.

Saham Merck naik 3,8% setelah perusahaan farmasi tersebut membukukan kinerja di atas perkiraan, ditopang penjualan obat kanker Keytruda dan vaksin HPV Gardasil.

Di sektor perangkat lunak, Fortinet dan ServiceNow masing-masing anjlok lebih dari 20% setelah merilis laporan keuangan kuartalan.

Sementara itu, saham Marvell Technology merosot 13,2% menyusul laporan media mengenai pembicaraan lanjutan untuk mengakuisisi Lumentum Holdings, perusahaan yang bergerak di bidang teknologi optik untuk pusat data. Saham Lumentum justru naik 0,6%.

Tekanan berlanjut pada perdagangan setelah penutupan bursa. Saham Amazon turun 6,5% dalam perdagangan after-hours setelah perusahaan memberikan proyeksi penjualan kuartal IV yang mengecewakan.

Baca Juga: Rupiah Berpeluang Konsolidasi, Cek Proyeksi Kurs Dolar AS pada Jumat (30/10)

Mayoritas emiten S&P 500 lampaui ekspektasi

Musim laporan keuangan kuartal III-2026 telah melewati pertengahan periode. Berdasarkan data terbaru LSEG, sebanyak 253 perusahaan anggota indeks S&P 500 telah melaporkan kinerjanya, dengan 81% di antaranya membukukan hasil di atas ekspektasi analis.

LSEG memperkirakan pertumbuhan laba agregat perusahaan-perusahaan dalam indeks S&P 500 mencapai 31% secara tahunan pada kuartal III-2026.

Meski mayoritas emiten mencatatkan kinerja yang lebih baik dari perkiraan, kekhawatiran mengenai besarnya belanja AI, tekanan inflasi, dan prospek pertumbuhan pendapatan tetap menjadi faktor yang dicermati investor dalam menentukan arah pasar saham AS.

#9. Crypto giant Binance freezes $2.5bn of assets over suspected hack

📅 Oct 30, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Cryptocurrency giant Binance said on Thursday that it has frozen digital assets worth more than $2.5bn (Â£1.87bn) to prevent them from being stolen while it investigates a security issue.

The company said as part of a series of alerts online that all deposits and withdrawals are temporarily suspended while its teams conduct security checks and that the firm will "cover all losses".

"It does look like a hack on Binance," said Binance's former boss Changpeng Zhao, who remains a majority shareholder of the firm.

Zhao added that two other crypto platforms, Tether and Circle, have also frozen around $1.7bn in stolen assets.

Circle chief executive Jeremy Allaire said the firm is "closely tracking reports of a potential exploit involving Binance" and that his company has frozen more than $800m in assets linked to the incident.

Circle is the issuer and co-creator of the USDC, one of the world's largest US dollar-backed cryptocurrencies.

Tether did not immediately respond to queries from the BBC.

Binance said the amount of funds frozen may continue to increase as its teams work closely with law enforcement and other partners to trace and seize additional assets.

"We are working around the clock and will share a full post-mortem once the investigation is complete. Please rely only on official Binance channels for updates," it said, adding that users' funds are safe.

Binance is the world's largest crypto exchange by daily trading volumes.

Bitcoin dropped by around 5% following the alerts. The price of other major cryptocurrencies including Ethereum and Solana also fell sharply.

#219. Hashim: Rp10.000 T Kegiatan Ekonomi RI Tak Tercatat, Ini Sebabnya!

📅 Oct 30, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Jakarta, CNBC Indonesia - Utusan Khusus Presiden Bidang Iklim dan Energi Hashim Djojohadikusumo mengungkapkan besarnya aktivitas ekonomi Indonesia yang belum tercatat dalam produk domestik bruto (PDB), nilainya diperkirakan mencapai Rp10.000 triliun. Besarnya nilai tersebut disebabkan oleh masih banyaknya transaksi ekonomi yang tidak masuk dalam pencatatan resmi.

Hashim mengatakan, berdasarkan data Bank Dunia, sekitar 35%-40% aktivitas ekonomi Indonesia belum tercatat. Kondisi ini membuat ukuran ekonomi Indonesia yang tercermin dalam PDB resmi dinilai belum sepenuhnya menggambarkan aktivitas ekonomi sebenarnya.

"Kalau PDB kita tercatat Rp25.000 triliun, itu diakui oleh IMF, Bank Dunia dan Bank Indonesia dan pemerintah kita. Ternyata ekonomi kita jauh lebih besar. Menurut Bank Dunia, kurang lebih 35%-40% kegiatan ekonomi di Indonesia tidak tercatat," ujar Hashim dalam Rapimnas Kadin Indonesia.

Dia memperkirakan, aktivitas ekonomi yang tidak tercatat tersebut setara dengan sekitar Rp10.000 triliun.

"Dan ternyata ekonomi kita 30%-40% tidak tercatat. Itu berarti ada kurang lebih Rp10 ribu triliun yang tidak tercatat," katanya.

Hashim menjelaskan, aktivitas ekonomi yang tidak tercatat itu bukan selalu transaksi ilegal. Sebagian terjadi dalam kegiatan sehari-hari masyarakat yang tidak disertai bukti transaksi atau pencatatan perpajakan.

Dia mencontohkan jasa tukang cukur rambut dan tukang pijat yang datang ke rumah. Pembayaran dilakukan secara langsung tanpa kuitansi sehingga transaksi tersebut tidak tercatat dan tidak dikenakan pajak pertambahan nilai (PPN).

"Saya biasanya tukang rambut datang ke apartemen saya sekali sebulan. Dia charge fee, upah. Dan upah itu saya bayar tanpa kuitansi. Dan karena tanpa kuitansi, PPN 11% tidak dipungut," ujar Hashim.

Dia juga mencontohkan jasa tukang pijat yang menerima pembayaran tanpa memberikan kuitansi.

Menurut Hashim, transaksi-transaksi serupa terjadi dalam jumlah besar di masyarakat. Mulai dari jasa perorangan, warung makan, hingga salon kecantikan. Jika dikumpulkan, aktivitas ekonomi tersebut membentuk nilai yang sangat besar.

"Seberapa banyak transaksi seperti dilakukan oleh Anton dan Yanti. Kali ratusan juta dan sebagian-sebagian jumlahnya termasuk mungkin warung tegal, beauty salon, tidak ada semua transaksi itu berupa tunai," jelasnya.

Hashim menilai besarnya aktivitas ekonomi yang tidak tercatat tersebut berkaitan dengan rendahnya penerimaan negara Indonesia. Dia menyebut rasio seluruh penerimaan negara, termasuk pajak, cukai, dan penerimaan negara bukan pajak (PNBP), masih sekitar 12% terhadap PDB.

Menurutnya, angka tersebut lebih rendah dibandingkan sejumlah negara lain. Hashim membandingkan rasio penerimaan negara Indonesia dengan Kamboja yang disebutnya mencapai 18%, India 20%, dan Vietnam 23%.

Dia menilai perbedaan rasio penerimaan negara Indonesia dengan Kamboja menunjukkan besarnya potensi penerimaan yang belum tergali.

"Kalau kita bandingkan dengan Cambodia, negara lebih miskin dari Indonesia. Selisihnya antara 18% dan 12%, itu adalah 6%. 6% dari yang tercatat adalah Rp25 ribu triliun, 6% itu berapa? Rp1.500 triliun bukan?" kata Hashim.

Hashim memperkirakan Indonesia berpotensi memperoleh tambahan penerimaan negara sekitar Rp1.500 triliun setiap tahun apabila mampu meningkatkan rasio penerimaan negara hingga setara Kamboja.

Bahkan, dia menilai tambahan penerimaan tersebut dapat mengubah posisi fiskal Indonesia. Hashim membandingkannya dengan defisit APBN yang menurutnya berada di kisaran Rp500 triliun-Rp600 triliun.

"Kalau aparat kita pajak dan bea cukai bekerja semestinya, seharusnya seperti orang Cambodia kita surplus tahun ini Rp900 triliun," ujarnya.

Untuk mengatasi persoalan tersebut, Hashim mengatakan pemerintah telah menyiapkan langkah peningkatan penerimaan negara melalui pemanfaatan teknologi digital dan kecerdasan buatan atau artificial intelligence (AI).

Dia menyebut pemerintah akan mengoptimalkan pemungutan pajak melalui sistem elektronik agar transaksi ekonomi yang selama ini tidak tercatat dapat masuk ke dalam sistem perpajakan.

"Pajak lewat elektronik, IT. AI akan dipakai semua transaksi yang nanti akan dipungut sesuai dengan undang-undang berlaku," kata Hashim.

Dia juga menyebut pemerintah akan mulai menerapkan digital taxation pada 9 November. Dalam skema tersebut, bank pemerintah, bank swasta, hingga perusahaan teknologi finansial atau fintech, termasuk platform perdagangan digital, disebut akan dilibatkan dalam pemungutan dan penyetoran pajak.

"Ini akan segera dilaksanakan, tanggal 9 November, semua bank pemerintah, bank swasta, dan semua fintech, termasuk Shopee, Lazada, Goto, dan sebagainya harus ikut pungut dan harus setor pajak yang selama ini tidak dipungut," ujarnya.

Hashim berharap langkah tersebut dapat meningkatkan penerimaan negara dan memperkuat kemampuan fiskal pemerintah untuk membiayai berbagai program pembangunan.

Dia menargetkan dalam dua hingga tiga tahun ke depan rasio penerimaan negara Indonesia dapat meningkat hingga setara dengan Kamboja.

#238. Geger Pangkalan Militer AS Diam-Diam Diserang, Pasokan Pangan Rusak

📅 Oct 30, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Jakarta, CNBC Indonesia - Pentagon tengah menyelidiki insiden kerusakan misterius pada mesin pendingin di berbagai pangkalan militer Amerika Serikat (AS) pada Agustus lalu. Empat pejabat terkait menyebut insiden yang merusak pasokan makanan tersebut diduga kuat sebagai akibat dari serangan siber.

Mengutip Reuters, Jumat (30/10/2026), para penyelidik militer AS kini terus menjalin komunikasi dengan rekan-rekan mereka di Prancis. Hal ini berkaitan erat dengan serangan siber serupa yang mengganggu ratusan mesin pendingin supermarket di Prancis pada awal bulan ini. Pihak Pentagon menyebut bahwa unit Cyber Command atau pasukan siber militer AS sedang turun tangan menyelidiki kasus ini.

Departemen Investigasi Kriminal Angkatan Darat AS menyatakan Kantor Lapangan Siber mereka juga turut serta dalam penyelidikan. Mereka sedang bekerja sama dengan pihak penegak hukum Prancis untuk lebih memahami insiden pemadaman pendingin di Eropa.

"Bekerja sama erat dengan mitra antarlembaga dan penegak hukum," ungkap perwakilan Pentagon yang menolak memberikan rincian lebih lanjut.

Kerusakan pada akhir Agustus lalu itu memengaruhi setidaknya enam toko pangkalan tempat para personel militer biasa membeli makanan. Suhu unit pendingin secara sepihak dinaikkan mendadak pada malam hari yang menyebabkan tumpukan makanan layu dan mencair di pagi harinya.

Insiden ini pertama kali dilaporkan pada 28 Agustus oleh blog Signal and Silence, yang kemudian disusul pemberitaan oleh Stars and Stripes dan Daily Telegraph. Akun Facebook pangkalan Fort Huachuca di Arizona juga sempat mengeluhkan kondisi mesin pendingin mereka pada 26 Agustus.

"Masuk ke mode pencairan bunga es di mana ia memanaskan semua makanan. Percayalah pada kami ketika kami mengatakan itu busuk," paparnya.

Jika benar terbukti sebagai serangan siber, kerusakan ini menjadi peringatan keras betapa rentannya infrastruktur penyimpanan dingin. Di Prancis, serangan serupa sukses mengambil alih kontrol suhu di lebih dari 350 lokasi supermarket hingga memicu kebakaran pada beberapa unit pendingin.

Kerugian total akibat serangan di Prancis tersebut diperkirakan mencapai puluhan juta euro. Sementara itu, pihak Kedutaan Besar Prancis di Washington memilih bungkam dan menolak memberikan komentar terkait insiden ini.

Perusahaan asal Denmark, Danfoss, menyebut banyak perangkat pendingin yang diserang di Prancis sudah usang dan menggunakan peranti lunak lawas. Kondisi tersebut membuat peretas mudah membobol sistem, meskipun belum diketahui pasti apakah pangkalan militer AS juga menggunakan merek yang sama.

Sabotase fasilitas penyimpanan dingin melalui internet telah lama dipandang sebagai skenario mimpi buruk layaknya pemadaman infrastruktur listrik. Pada awal tahun ini, Inisiatif Keamanan Pangan Global (GFSI) telah secara khusus menyerukan perlunya perlindungan keamanan siber yang kuat untuk teknologi pendingin industri.

"Sistem pendingin yang diretas sama berbahayanya dengan yang rusak."

Sebelumnya, kelompok peretas juga pernah menyabotase lemari es komersial di AS dan berbagai negara lain. Perusahaan Danfoss sempat mencatat adanya serangan siber terhadap sistem pendingin supermarket di wilayah Rusia dan Israel pada tahun 2022 silam.

Pada Desember tahun lalu, jaksa federal di Los Angeles menuduh seorang wanita asal Ukraina sebagai bagian dari sindikat peretas siber. Kelompok tersebut dilaporkan melumpuhkan sistem pendingin di fasilitas pengepakan daging pada tahun 2024 yang berujung pada hancurnya lebih dari 2.000 pon daging.

CEO perusahaan keamanan siber industri Xona Systems, Bill Moore, menyoroti sistem pendingin modern yang kini makin sering terhubung ke jaringan internet. Padahal, pengontrol perangkat tersebut awalnya murni dirancang sebagai antarmuka terisolasi yang hanya dioperasikan langsung oleh teknisi di lokasi kejadian.

"Ini pasti dapat mengganggu rantai pasokan makanan," pungkas Moore.

#28. Microsoft plans to raise $25 billion in debt as it pursues AI buildout

📅 Oct 30, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Microsoft on Thursday announced plans to raise $25 billion in bonds, taking advantage of its healthy credit rating to support its infrastructure expansion.

The software maker holds a top-tier AAA rating with S&P, a credit agency. Management is targeting $170 billion in 2026 capital expenditures, as the company looks to meet heavy demand from artificial intelligence labs Anthropic and OpenAI, as well as other clients.

"Microsoft intends to use the net proceeds from the offering for general corporate purposes,  which may include, among other things, funding for working capital, capital expenditures, repurchases of capital stock, acquisitions, and repayment of existing debt," according to a statement. The offerings should close on Nov. 4, the company said.

Amazon and Alphabet have engaged the bond market to support infrastructure projects this year, and AI-focused cloud CoreWeave has also relied on the debt market to broaden its data center footprint. Investors have turned more skittish on companies with tall debt piles as central bankers raise interest rates to fight inflation.

Microsoft will access additional funding sources to complement its own cash flow generation when conditions are attractive, Amy Hood, its finance chief, said on the company's earnings call on Wednesday.

"There's a form of financing called leasing, and we've been using that one as a vehicle," she said. "We're fortunate to have access in the debt markets, given where our ratings are, and we haven't yet tapped that."

Microsoft reported positive free cash flow on Wednesday, differentiating itself from Alphabet, whose cash flow was negative for the second quarter in a row. Last week Meta said it had negative free cash flow, and on Thursday Amazon reported negative free cash flow.

Also on Wednesday, Oracle unveiled plans to raise $10 billion in new debt and said it anticipates a return to positive free cash flow in the second half of its 2028 fiscal year.

Equipment management is essential because it helps you prevent costly breakdowns and downtime.

Start by tracking your equipment usage to identify underutilized assets; this way, you can allocate resources more effectively.

Schedule regular maintenance to keep everything in good working order and compliant with regulations.

Use analytics to predict potential failures, allowing you to address problems before they escalate.

This proactive approach not only saves money but also extends the lifespan of your equipment.

To maintain equipment effectively, focus on these six requirements:

First, schedule regular inspections to catch faults early.

Second, document maintenance history for compliance and safety.

Third, use automated scheduling to minimize downtime.

Fourth, keep a centralized database for accurate tracking.

Fifth, implement predictive maintenance using real-time data to enhance lifespan.

Finally, train your staff on proper usage and maintenance practices to guarantee everyone understands their roles in keeping equipment running smoothly.

Incorporating equipment management software can transform your business operations. Start by evaluating your current equipment needs and identifying pain points, like excessive downtime or high maintenance costs. Choose software that offers real-time tracking and integrates smoothly with your existing systems. Train your team to maximize its benefits and monitor performance regularly. By taking these steps, you’ll not only enhance efficiency but also guarantee compliance and save on costs in the long run.

#60. From workshops to the catwalk - why mended clothes are going mainstream

📅 Oct 30, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Alice Brown uses felt animals to cover holes and stains on her clothes

If Alice Brown is browsing in second-hand shops and finds a jumper with a hole, that won't stop her buying it.

"I see it as challenge," says the 27-year-old forest ranger from Lincolnshire.

She is a champion of the growing fashion for eye-catching repairs.

Over the last year she has developed her own unique style, needle-felting cute animals and insects to cover moth holes, stains and other imperfections, winning her a huge following online.

"The natural place for decoration is in the centre at the front. If the hole is under the arm or on the shoulder, it forces you to think about the design," she says.

Often dubbed "visible mending" the trend is spreading from hobby crafters and community workshops, even drawing in fast-fashion brands and reaching the designer catwalks this year.

Some mends are designed to be eye-catching

At one Saturday morning workshop in Brighton, Jayne Eagle shows her students how to attach a patch of fabric behind a tear so the cat-face design peeps through.

Then she instructs them in stitching techniques inspired by the traditional Japanese methods Boro and Sashiko. Instead of matching the thread colour to the garment, they make the mend highly decorative using geometric patterns in large white stitching.

She also offers classes in darning using contrasting vibrant colours that celebrate, rather than disguise, the repair.

Jayne Eagle sees repair as an "act of rebellion" against fast fashion

One student, Nicola, has already used her newfound skills to stitch over sunscreen marks on a T-shirt "making a feature of it" and is now delighted that people "stop seeing the stains and just see the decoration".

Another student, Lisa, says her mother's generation would never have wanted the mends to show. "Now part of it is being proud of what you've done," she says.

Lisa bought a second-hand jacket online, but snipped the fabric with the scissors when she was opening the package. She managed to patch it in a way that "looks intentional".

"I was actually pointing it out to people," she laughs.

Lisa says she's so proud of her mends she wants people to notice them

In the past mending was primarily about saving money, by wearing clothes for longer, rather than buying new. But visible mending is about celebrating that choice.

The trend took off about six years ago, Jayne says, and has boomed since.

An era of "throwaway" fast fashion from the 1980s onwards undermined the tradition of looking after and mending your clothes and people lost the skills, she says.

The revival of interest feels to her like a backlash against overproduction in the fashion industry and the damage it causes: the pollution, carbon emissions, water use, and poor labour conditions.

So perhaps it is surprising just how popular mended style is proving among retailers, with brands from Ralph Lauren to ultra-fast-fashion firm Shein currently selling stitched and patched garments, albeit in both of those cases brand new off the peg.

Other High Street chains are adopting a more authentic approach, offering in-house repairs and workshops.

"We want to give customers practical ways to extend the life of the clothes they already love, rather than simply replacing them," says Uniqlo's head of sustainability, Maria Ledous.

Uniqlo offers decorative mending using Sashiko techniques, in keeping with the brand's Japanese heritage

Primark, often seen as a fast-fashion retailer due to its lower prices, has also joined the trend.

"We don't think that affordability and durability should be opposites," says Vicki Swain, the brand's longevity lead.

Outdoor brand Finisterre says around a fifth of the mends it provides for customers are designed to be eye-catching, while premium artisan clothing store Toast says around a third of customers go for that option.

Part of the "magic" is that customers can bring in a garment and "choose what they'd like it to become next", says Toast chief executive Suzie de Rohan Willner.

Toast has a team of 14 in-house "repair artists" providing a range of creative mends

Toast's repair services are free, but off-the-peg "renewed" pieces fetch higher prices than the originals to reflect their unique character.

Levi's, which boasts probably the longest tradition of being fixed up by its fans using anything from rhinestones to fringed cuffs, is rolling out a workshop programme this year.

The firm says its research shows younger customers in particular want to repair more but don't know how.

For now, Levi's' workshops focus on traditional mending skills, such as sewing on a button and hemming, rather than visible mends, and at a recent launch event in London, that seemed to be what its Gen Z participants were looking for.

"I'm from East London. If you have holes in your clothes people are like 'Ha ha! You're poor!'," says 16-year-old school student Bianca.

At a workshop run by Levi's, Nicholas (r) says he prefers mending not to show, while Bianca (centre) says people still see it as a sign of poverty

Theo, 19, says he'd rather learn how to mend without it showing.

"I would want [the jeans] to look how I originally bought them," he says.

He has a side hustle selling clothes at car boot sales and thinks traditional repair skills would make it more lucrative.

His friend Nicholas, also 19, says most people want to look "fresh".

"No-one wants to look like they came out of a dumpster," he says.

Still, Paul Dillinger, Levi's head of global design innovation, thinks more creative repairs will win them over as they pick up more skills.

"No-one has time for that perfect 'straight from the shop' thing. That's not cool.

"People crave agency," he says. "Mending is a demonstration of humanity: it's not a mustard stain, it's a memory of a great day in the ballpark."

#287. Gawat! Negara Eropa Ini 'Diserang' Asing, 750 Ribu Warga Jadi Korban

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Jakarta, CNBC Indonesia - Moldova baru saja memanggil Dewan Keamanan Nasional untuk menggelar pertemuan khusus usai sistem informasi pemerintahannya kebobolan. Serangan siber skala besar ini secara membabi buta membidik jutaan data pribadi milik warganya.

Mengutip Manila Times, Kamis (29/10/2026), serangan siber tersebut terjadi antara tanggal 19 hingga 23 Oktober lalu. Insiden peretasan ini dikonfirmasi langsung oleh Badan Pemerintahan Elektronik Moldova (AGE).

Sekitar satu juta nomor identitas pribadi menjadi target dalam upaya ekstraksi data ilegal ini. Pihak AGE mengungkapkan bahwa sekitar 750.000 kumpulan data telah berhasil digasak oleh peretas. Sebagai catatan, total populasi negara Moldova saat ini hanya berkisar di angka 2,4 juta jiwa.

Pihak kepresidenan menyatakan bahwa Presiden Maia Sandu telah mengadakan pertemuan Dewan Keamanan Nasional untuk beberapa hari mendatang. Langkah ini diambil guna menilai dampak kerusakan dari serangan dan menentukan langkah tanggapan yang tepat.

Perdana Menteri Moldova Vasile Tofan pada hari Rabu (28/10/2026) sebelumnya juga telah menyinggung insiden tersebut dalam sebuah rapat pemerintah. Ia mencurigai adanya campur tangan pihak luar dalam kasus ini.

"Ada indikasi kampanye yang lebih luas yang melibatkan aktor negara asing," ucap Tofan.

Kendati demikian, ia tidak merinci negara mana saja yang dicurigai sebagai dalang di balik serangan siber tersebut. Ia hanya menegaskan bahwa proses investigasi sedang berjalan secara menyeluruh.

"Pihak berwenang terkait sedang menyelidiki, termasuk keterlibatan mereka dalam insiden baru-baru ini," tegasnya.

Sebagai buntut insiden ini, Tofan secara resmi telah memecat Direktur AGE, Nicoleta Colomeet. Ia meminta institusi tersebut untuk memberikan penekanan yang jauh lebih besar pada keamanan layanan digital ke depannya.

Sementara itu, pihak AGE merinci bahwa data yang berhasil dicuri meliputi nama, tanggal lahir, status asuransi, hingga informasi kesehatan. Informasi keuangan krusial seperti nomor rekening bank internasional (IBAN) juga ikut bocor.

"Risiko utamanya adalah penggunaan data dalam upaya penipuan dan manipulasi daring."

#42. Amazon CEO sees AI spending 'meaningfully' higher in 2027

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Amazon is already spending a record amount on AI data centers. Next year will be even bigger.

The world's biggest cloud-computing company reported third-quarter results on Thursday and reiterated its plan for $220 billion in capital expenditure for 2026.

Then, CEO Andy Jassy shared his outlook for next year, saying Amazon expects "to spend meaningfully more in 2027."

Amazon’s spending has become the central question hanging over the company. It's building massive data centers and buying expensive chips and other gear to meet surging demand for AI.

That's pushed its free cash flow into negative territory, with third-quarter results showing a hole of $42.4 billion for the trailing twelve months.

Amazon spent $75.9 billion in cash capital expenditures during the third quarter alone, CFO Brian Olsavsky said, primarily on AWS and AI infrastructure.

The results did little to ease investors' concern about the spending. Amazon's third-quarter revenue and earnings came in below Wall Street expectations, and its shares fell roughly 6% in after-hours trading.

"The cloud unit's improving operating margin was one welcome signal, but it may not be sufficient to alleviate concerns about the sharp spike in capex spending and corresponding freefall of its free cash flow," Sky Canaves, a principal analyst at eMarketer, said in a statement.

Amazon has argued that AI infrastructure takes years to build before it can begin generating revenue, and that AI chip servers typically break even in less than three years once deployed. But the scale of the outlay has raised the stakes for Amazon's cloud business to sustain rapid growth as competitors make similarly enormous bets.

Add BI in Google so our reporting is easier to find when you’re searching for what matters.

On Thursday, Jassy reiterated his confidence in the company's AI business model and outlook, saying there's a "clear line of sight to attractive financial returns."

The time it takes Amazon to turn newly delivered servers into revenue-generating capacity has also come under scrutiny.

Last week, Business Insider exclusively reported that Amazon has begun receiving billions of dollars in Nvidia server racks before all of the gear can be deployed at their final data-center sites, as power shortages and construction delays hold back new capacity.

Jassy argued Thursday that Amazon has visibility into the demand supporting its huge AI investments. Customers that need the largest AI deployments reserve capacity well ahead of time, he said, in blocks ranging from hundreds of megawatts to, in some cases, as much as a gigawatt.

"We have very good visibility into 2027 and 2028 and some of '29 too with respect to what customers want in those really big chunks of capacity," Jassy said.

AWS revenue rose 39.3% year over year to $46 billion in the third quarter, its fastest growth in nearly five years. Jassy said he expects AWS revenue to grow more than 40% in both 2027 and 2028, a projection that requires the company to keep rapidly adding capacity.

Jassy described the spending as a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity rather than an optional expansion. It would be more convenient, he said, if Amazon could spread the investment over 25 years. But the speed of the AI market does not allow that.

"We can fund this plan," he said. "It will forever change our revenue. It will forever change our free cash flow."

#19. Google value plunges by $500bn as AI fears rattle Wall Street

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Google CEO Sundar Pichai has backed the company's massive spending on AI.

Google parent Alphabet lost just over $500bn (Â£374bn) in value on Thursday after it rattled Wall Street with its last update of the year on its business operations.

Google spooked investors so badly that its stock hit a six-month low, after it revealed its artificial intelligence (AI) summaries on search were affecting ad revenue.

Microsoft and Amazon also updated investors this week, and joined Google in plans to keep spending astronomical sums of cash in an ongoing AI race.

But investors appeared dismayed at the impacts these plans are having on the ability of each to keep growing their core lines of business.

At first glance, the quarterly results of Alphabet, the parent company of Google, were not at all bad.

Total revenue grew 25% compared to last year, hitting $127.6bn. It was buoyed by the ongoing success of Google Cloud, where revenue grew by 103% to almost $31bn.

However, profits at the company fell by a considerable 25% during the quarter. And for the second time in its history as a public company, it showed negative free cash flow, this time in excess of $9bn. That's almost twice the loss of cash Alphabet showed just three months ago.

Cash flow represents the money a company retains after paying for operations and investments, meaning Alphabet is now spending more money than it is bringing in. That new level of spending is almost entirely dedicated to Google's AI investments.

By the end of Thursday, the company's total stock market value had fallen to $3.46 trillion from $3.97 trillion the day before.

Google's omnipresent search engine now defaults to AI summaries of a person's query, and that hit the number of advertisements it was able to sell during the quarter.

The sale of ads that appear alongside Google Search results is the vast majority of Alphabet's business.

Tracy Woo, a principal analyst at Forrester, said Alphabet's disappointing search business results "highlights the challenge of funding massive AI investments while maintaining the strength of established businesses."

Although Phillip Schindler, Google's chief business officer, said on Wednesday that he felt "very good about our trajectory" with an AI-focused version of the search engine, the company's stock fell by almost 13% on Thursday, its second lowest showing this year.

Amazon's stock also took a hit on Thursday after it shared results that disappointed investors. Shares of the company fell 6% in after hours stock trading.

Alongside quarterly sales growth of 11%, hitting $201bn, the company shared a staggering cash flow figure of negative $42bn.

That number shows that Amazon is not only spending more money than it makes, but that spending has increased six-fold over the span of three months.

Chief executive Andy Jassy claimed the business was spending in part because it had the chance at, not one, but two businesses that could end up bringing in $1 trillion for Amazon the future.

Jassy did not specify what two Amazon businesses those may be. But during a call with financial analysts he repeatedly mentioned the financial opportunity presented by Amazon making and selling its own computer chips, as well as its AI developer platform Bedrock, which offers paying users access to most major AI models and tools.

"One of the lessons we've learned over 20-plus years is that there is not one tool to rule the world," Jassy said. "People always assume whatever is in the lead early on will be the only thing that people use - it's never true."

As Jassy was asked several times to explain the company's spending, he admitted it "would be more convenient" if it were spread over the next 25 years. "That's not the world we live in," he added.

"We can fund this plan, and I think it will forever change the size, the revenue, the free cash flow of Amazon."

Wall Street reacted less enthusiastically to Microsoft's update, with shares falling by less than 1% on Thursday.

The company, which is also one of OpenAI's largest investors, spent around $5bn less on investments dedicated to AI. It also revealed earlier this week plans to cut thousands of workers, which will further reduce the company's costs.

Meanwhile, its revenue grew 19% from a year earlier, hitting $92.3bn for the quarter. Its crucial cloud business grew by 30% to $63bn.

Chief executive Satya Nadella said the use of Copilot, Microsoft's AI chatbot and tool, has seen "automated tasks nearly double since July" as daily users grew by 500% from a year ago.

Usually lower spending and growing sales earns a positive reaction from investors.

But Woo said Wall Street is trying to look farther into the future when it comes to which businesses will truly make good on their big promises around AI.

"Microsoft's results reinforce that enterprise demand for AI remains strong, but they also highlight a much bigger question facing the technology industry, where AI value will ultimately be captured," Woo said.

#52. Smartbird's CEO talks about converting a shoe company into an AI player

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Before Smartbird was powering large language models, it was making shoes.

The CEO of the AI infrastructure company, Nadia Carlsten, spoke about the transition with chief correspondent Ben Bergman on Thursday at Business Insider's The Long Play.

Its roots are the sustainable sneaker company Allbirds, which skyrocketed in the late 2010s. The shoes were particularly popular among the Silicon Valley crowd. Allbirds went public in 2021 before the market turned on many direct-to-consumer businesses. The stock quickly fell, and it sold to American Exchange Group in March for $39 million.

"The short answer is the shoe business wasn't doing that well," Carlsten said.

The company then sold off its footwear business and shed most of its workforce, rebranding as Smartbird. The AI pivot skyrocketed the stock and was briefly up 800%, though it has since come back down. Carlsten, who became CEO in June, said she had never worn those wool shoes.

"The goal of the company was to do something valuable and meaningful. And for me, I knew I wanted to do AI infrastructure," she said.

After looking at the emerging AI infrastructure sector, she believed with the company's transformation, it was the "right place at the right time" for her to join.

In the five months since she took over, Carlsten said the experience can be described in one word: exciting.

"Never in my life thought that I would be the CEO of a former shoe company and managing what is essentially a startup." Since taking on the role, she has focused on building out a new team, scouring new technologies for the company to look into, and producing a new pipeline of customers.

Add BI in Google so our reporting is easier to find when you’re searching for what matters.

While the stock price has gone down since the initial pivot-induced spike, Carlsten does not regret taking on the challenge. She is betting on the company's long-term potential.

"The stock market is going to do what the stock market does," she said. "What I care about is making sure that we're putting the company on the right path, and that we're able to build it quickly enough and well enough that we have a sustainable business that investors will love. So what happens in the short term? Not that important. What happens in the long term is very important."

When asked why the company decided to reinvent itself in the public eye instead of beginning as a private start-up, Carlsten countered with a question: "Why not?" she said, "It will come back up."

Having to reinvent the company from scratch can be challenging, and the public's presence is clear, she said. Yet, Carlsten said reinvention is inevitable for companies.

"This is a very public reinvention of the company, and people are watching," she said. "I think every company needs to reinvent itself every now and then."

When asked why the company is fully remote, Carlsten said most of the work at Smartbird is done virtually or at data centers, meaning an office would not be of much use.

"We spend a lot of time actually building things, which I actually love. So if my people are sitting in an office, something's wrong," she said.

Carlsten said that although her competitors are building similar AI infrastructure, the way Smartbird is going about it is unique.

"For us, it's about the right infrastructure for the right company at the right time," she said. "Our business model allows us to work more closely with customers, but it also is a lot less capital intensive."

Smartbird is looking to work with companies that have yet to explore implementing AI in their systems, she said. As more companies outside the tech realm look to adopt AI into their businesses, they have the choice to build infrastructure themselves or outsource it, and Smartbird wants to be the one to do it for them, she said.

"We'll do the infrastructure part. We'll let you focus on the actual business," she said.

The most common misconception Carlsten said she has encountered in the business is that it's all about GPUs. She said the company does a lot more than just run GPUs.

"It's really about having a holistic system that a customer can use to do AI," she said.

Another rumbling that's taken over the tech company discourse is whether there's an AI bubble and if it will pop. Carlsten said that depends on how people go about defining the bubble.

Excitement in the sector means funding might be going into companies that may not prosper in the future, but this is something that happens with the arrival of new technologies, she said.

"Every time there is a transformational technology, this happens," she said. "Even if we assume that there is a bubble, that doesn't mean that the technology is not good. It also doesn't mean that the adoption of the technology is going to go away."

#72. Barry Diller has a plan for monetizing legacy media in the AI age

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Media titan Barry Diller, the chairman of publishing empire IAC, has built and rebuilt his career more than most executives, from network television to Hollywood studios to the earliest internet empires.

As part of Business Insider's The Long Play, Diller talked to Peter Kafka, Business Insider's chief correspondent covering media and technology, about what successful businesses require: good timing, nerve, and the willingness to walk away from what's working.

During this conversation, titled "The Art of the Comeback: A Masterclass on Resilience," Diller shares his perspectives on AI adoption. He also talks about why his MGM Resorts deal fell apart and how he's leveraging legacy print publications like Southern Living to create new revenue streams.

The 84-year-old media and tech exec told Kafka he wants to keep working as long as he can be engaged and useful.

#1. Binance 'potential security issue' sees $2.54 billion frozen by the likes of Circle, Tether

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Several crypto firms, including Circle Internet Group and Tether, on Thursday froze $2.54 billion of funds tied to a potential hack on cryptocurrency exchange Binance.

The world's largest crypto exchange by daily trading volumes didn't explicitly confirm that it has suffered an exploit.

"Our teams are aware of reports of a potential security issue and are actively investigating," a Binance spokesperson said. "Rest assured that user funds are completely safe." Binance also said on X that Binance Alpha trading had been temporarily suspended, and that "the current verified total frozen is $2.54 billion."

Binance founder and former CEO Changpeng Zhao said in a post on X that user funds were safe and that he expected all customer funds to be fully covered. Zhao, who no longer runs the exchange, also said the incident appeared to be a hack.

Circle CEO Jeremy Allaire said Thursday evening in a social media post that the company had frozen more than 800 million in USD Coin associated with the incident. USDT stablecoin issuer Tether CEO Paolo Ardoino also said in on X that his firm had frozen more than 1.5 billion of its stablecoin that may have been related to a potential breach.

Later, Binance co-founder Yi He said in a statement that the XRP and RLUSD-tied firm Ripple helped halt the movement of $200 million of funds linked to the possible attack.

But those frozen funds could represent only a fraction of the ones impacted. Crypto data provider Arkham Intelligence linked nearly $6 billion in digital assets — including bitcoin, ether, solana and various stablecoins — to 55 wallet addresses it has linked to the potential exploit.

Arkham identifies, tracks and flags crypto wallets by analyzing publicly available blockchain data. It also solicits tips from its community members.

Exploits are not uncommon in the crypto space. In 2025, Bybit suffered roughly $1.5 billion in losses due to a hack. More recently, Bitget lost more than $350 million worth of digital assets in a cyber theft incident. Over the past several years, bad actors have also drained billions of dollars in crypto from decentralized exchanges.

Bitcoin — the most widely followed cryptocurrency in the world — slid more than 3% following the executives' statements, breaking below $80,000. Ether fell nearly 4%, while solana shed about 3%.

Shares of Circle also tumbled about 5% in after-hours trading on Thursday.

#224. FL Technics Kantongi Sertifikasi EASA, Buka Peluang Garap Pasar MRO Eropa

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Dina Mirayanti Hutauruk | Editor: Dina Hutauruk

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. FL Technics Indonesia mengantongi sertifikasi EASA Part-145 dari European Union Aviation Safety Agency (EASA) untuk fasilitas perawatan pesawatnya di Bali. Sertifikasi ini membuka peluang perusahaan memperluas layanan perawatan, perbaikan, dan overhaul (MRO) pesawat ke pasar Eropa.

Pengakuan tersebut menegaskan bahwa fasilitas FL Technics Indonesia di Bali telah memenuhi standar keselamatan dan kualitas perawatan pesawat yang ditetapkan regulator penerbangan Uni Eropa. Sertifikasi ini juga memperkuat posisi perusahaan dalam persaingan industri MRO internasional.

Dengan sertifikasi tersebut, FL Technics Indonesia dapat melayani base maintenance dan line maintenance bagi maskapai komersial serta perusahaan penyewaan pesawat (lessor) asal Eropa. Layanan mencakup pesawat berbadan sempit (narrowbody), antara lain keluarga Airbus A320 CEO dan NEO serta Boeing 737 Classic, Next Generation, dan MAX.

Chairman FL Technics Indonesia Martynas Grigas mengatakan, perusahaan akan segera memulai pekerjaan perawatan untuk operator penerbangan asal Uni Eropa. Kedatangan armada berikutnya juga telah dijadwalkan pada November 2026.

Baca Juga: FL Technics Gandeng GE Aerospace Perkuat Layanan Avionik

“Sertifikasi tersebut menjadi langkah penting untuk memperluas jangkauan bisnis perusahaan, tidak hanya di pasar domestik dan regional, tetapi juga ke Asia, Timur Tengah, hingga Australia,” kata Martynas dalam keterangannya, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Sebelum memperoleh sertifikasi EASA untuk fasilitas Bali, FL Technics Indonesia telah melayani maskapai dari berbagai kawasan, termasuk Amerika, Asia, Afrika, dan Australia melalui fasilitasnya di Jakarta dan Denpasar.

Sertifikasi EASA diperoleh setelah fasilitas tersebut menjalani serangkaian audit teknis. Pemeriksaan meliputi kelayakan infrastruktur hanggar, sistem perkakas, serta kompetensi dan kesiapan tenaga teknik.

Fasilitas di kawasan Bandara Internasional I Gusti Ngurah Rai, Denpasar, berdiri di atas lahan seluas 17.000 meter persegi (m2). Kompleks ini memiliki tiga hanggar dengan tiga aircraft bays serta fasilitas pengecatan pesawat (paint booth) seluas 2.000 m2.

Baca Juga: Bidik Pasar ASEAN, FL Technics Operasikan Fasilitas Pengecatan Pesawat di Bali

Infrastruktur tersebut menjadi modal perusahaan untuk menawarkan layanan perawatan pesawat dengan waktu pengerjaan yang efisien, sekaligus memenuhi standar keselamatan penerbangan internasional.

FL Technics Indonesia merupakan bagian dari Avia Solutions Group, grup penyedia layanan penerbangan yang memiliki jaringan kantor dan fasilitas produksi di berbagai negara. Perusahaan juga mengoperasikan kompleks hanggar dan kantor di Bandara Internasional Soekarno-Hatta, Jakarta.

#83. The CEO behind Grand Theft Auto has an 8-part longevity routine for shredded abs and sharp decision-making. Here's why peptides aren't part of it.

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Media mogul Strauss Zelnick spends his days hyper-focused on making the most creative, innovative, best video games in the world.

As the CEO and Chairman of video game-maker Take-Two Interactive, he's readying to release Grand Theft Auto 6 next month.

At home though, he doesn't play games. He prefers to spend his time working out and staying healthy for a long time.

At Business Insider's "The Long Play" event on Thursday, Zelnick said his formula for healthy aging is both simple and effective.

While he calls biohacker Bryan Johnson, the man behind the "Don't Die" movement, a friend, he said trying to live forever is a pretty silly strategy.

"There's almost no evidence that anything anyone is doing will increase your lifespan," he told Business Insider's Senior Leadership & Workplace Correspondent, Sarah Needleman. "How many of us really want to increase our lifespan from like 96 to 101? Look, that is not on my bucket list, thank you very much."

Strauss Zelnick is a speaker at Business Insider's The Long Play event on Thursday. Follow along with our coverage here.

Instead, Zelnick, who wrote a book on "Becoming Ageless" in 2018, focuses on the same core behaviors for "taking care of yourself" that longevity experts around the world swear by for maintaining health and happiness for the long haul.

"Smoking is the factor most highly correlated with a short, unhealthy, and frankly, unhappy life," Zelnick said. "Don't smoke."

These lead to "a bad old age, or no old age," he said.

Try a Level Up action pack.

Start small, stack wins, and fully own your first hour in 21 days.

Add BI in Google so our reporting is easier to find when you’re searching for what matters.

Zelnick said: "You don't have to do what I do," with 6 a.m. wakeup calls for group training sessions, or twice-a-day workouts.

"I do what I do because I love it, it's my passion," he said. "If you walk 30 minutes a day, four or five days a week — just walk — you're like 80% of the way there."

Zelnick says he doesn't have an "insanely healthy diet," but he tries to maintain some balance. "I eat a lot of vegetables and salads and protein — I also like sweets, and I do eat carbs."

Zelnick says he treats this as a non-negotiable for his business.

"If you're getting less than six hours, you're making bad decisions," he said.

Longevity experts stress this is key for mental health, and establishing a sense of purpose in the world.

Zelnick said it's critical to get support from "people who love you."

Zelnick says don't waste your time with all the overhyped, unproven health hacks.

"All the other stuff, like, peptides? A: No," he said. "We have an FDA for a reason. Don't take something that is not double-blind tested."

And, he said, be careful with some of the internet's most famous longevity gurus. He name-checked controversial Harvard professor David Sinclair, who has made bold promises about the anti-aging effects of unproven supplements, including resveratrol, a plant polyphenol found in grapes and red wine, and NMN, which is a precursor to NAD, the vital coenzyme that's in every cell of our body.

"A medical doctor actually is a degree: M-D, an MD degree," Zelnick said. "Not a David Sinclair degree, he is not actually a doctor."

#218. Rupiah Berpeluang Konsolidasi, Cek Proyeksi Kurs Dolar AS pada Jumat (30/10)

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Muhammad Fatih | Editor: Handoyo

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Nilai tukar rupiah diperkirakan masih bergerak fluktuatif pada perdagangan Jumat (30/10/2026), meski mencatatkan penguatan pada penutupan perdagangan hari ini. Pergerakan mata uang Garuda selanjutnya akan dipengaruhi oleh sentimen global, arah kebijakan suku bunga Amerika Serikat (AS), serta efektivitas intervensi Bank Indonesia (BI).

Berdasarkan data Bloomberg pada Kamis (29/10/2026), rupiah di pasar spot ditutup pada level Rp 18.137 per dolar AS atau menguat 0,26%. Sementara itu, kurs referensi Jakarta Interbank Spot Dollar Rate (JISDOR) Bank Indonesia menunjukkan rupiah terapresiasi 0,30% ke posisi Rp 18.128 per dolar AS, dari Rp 18.183 per dolar AS pada hari sebelumnya.

Ekonom Universitas Airlangga Rahma Gafmi menjelaskan, penguatan rupiah pada Kamis (29/10/2026) ditopang oleh keputusan Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC) yang menahan suku bunga acuan. Keputusan tersebut meredakan tekanan terhadap dolar AS dan memberikan ruang bagi pasar keuangan regional untuk bernapas dalam jangka pendek.

Selain itu, konsistensi Bank Indonesia dalam menjalankan bauran instrumen intervensi valuta asing (valas) dinilai efektif menjaga stabilitas rupiah di tengah fluktuasi harga energi. Meski demikian, pergerakan rupiah masih dibayangi sentimen negatif berupa aksi jual bersih investor asing di pasar saham serta penyesuaian indeks portofolio MSCI.

Baca Juga: Target IPO BEI 2027 Sebanyak 25 Perusahaan, Kepercayaan Investor Masih Jadi Tantangan

Menjelang perdagangan Jumat (30/10/2026), arah pergerakan rupiah akan kembali ditentukan oleh dinamika ekonomi global dan perkembangan indikator fundamental domestik.

Dari sisi eksternal, pelaku pasar tidak hanya mencermati suku bunga The Fed yang berada di level 3,75%–4,00%, tetapi juga menunggu petunjuk lanjutan (forward guidance) mengenai arah inflasi AS. Perkembangan tersebut akan menjadi salah satu faktor penentu pergerakan indeks dolar AS (DXY).

"Pergerakan tingkat imbal hasil obligasi pemerintah Amerika Serikat terus dicermati pelaku pasar untuk melihat potensi capital outflow atau inflow dari pasar keuangan domestik (SBN dan saham)," ungkap Rahma kepada Kontan, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Selain kebijakan moneter AS, tingginya harga minyak mentah dunia berpotensi menambah tekanan terhadap neraca perdagangan Indonesia. Kondisi tersebut juga dapat meningkatkan kebutuhan valuta asing bagi perusahaan-perusahaan energi nasional.

Sementara dari sisi domestik, investor akan mencermati efektivitas triple intervention BI melalui pasar spot, Domestic Non-Deliverable Forward (DNDF), dan Surat Berharga Negara (SBN). Langkah tersebut menjadi salah satu instrumen untuk menjaga pergerakan rupiah tetap sesuai dengan fundamental ekonomi, terutama ketika nilai tukar mendekati level psikologis tertentu.

Ketahanan indikator makroekonomi, termasuk pertumbuhan kredit pada segmen investasi dan modal kerja, juga menjadi perhatian investor. Hal ini terutama karena aktivitas kredit tersebut masih perlu dicermati di tengah melemahnya daya beli masyarakat kelas menengah.

Baca Juga: Wall Street Dibuka Melemah, Tertekan Tingginya Rencana Belanja AI

"Menjelang akhir bulan Oktober 2026, aksi korporasi serta penyesuaian portofolio (rebalancing) oleh investor institusional domestik maupun asing turut mewarnai volume transaksi harian di pasar valas," tambah Rahma. Proyeksi Rupiah Jumat (30/10/2026)

Secara teknikal, Rahma memperkirakan rupiah akan bergerak fluktuatif, tetapi cenderung terkonsolidasi pada perdagangan Jumat (30/10/2026). Volatilitas pasar dan dinamika likuiditas menjelang akhir pekan menjadi sejumlah faktor yang memengaruhi pergerakan mata uang domestik.

Ia memproyeksikan rupiah bergerak dalam rentang Rp 18.100 hingga Rp 18.230 per dolar AS pada perdagangan besok. Pergerakan tersebut akan sangat bergantung pada respons pasar terhadap sentimen setelah keputusan The Fed serta keberlanjutan intervensi Bank Indonesia dalam menjaga stabilitas nilai tukar.

#129. 5 Proven Tips to Increase Store Sales Fast

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

If you want to boost your store sales quickly, start by knowing your customers. Use data analytics to understand their preferences and tailor your marketing efforts. Next, create a welcoming atmosphere in your store, making it easy to navigate. Don’t forget to leverage technology for personalized experiences and implement compelling promotions to entice purchases. Finally, streamline your checkout process to make transactions quick and easy. These steps can transform your sales strategy, but there’s more you can do to maximize success.

To boost your store sales, it’s essential to know your customers well. Start by identifying your ideal customer profile. This helps tailor your offerings to meet their specific needs, leading to a potential 30% increase in engagement and conversion rates.

Next, analyze customer feedback and purchasing patterns. Use this data to craft personalized marketing strategies that build loyalty.

Consider conducting regular surveys to understand customer challenges better; 70% of respondents are willing to provide feedback if incentivized.

Don’t forget to engage with your customers on social media. This interaction fosters community, making 60% of consumers more likely to buy from brands they feel connected to.

Lastly, utilize customer data analytics for improved inventory management. Ensuring popular items are consistently available can reduce stockouts by up to 25%, which is vital for learning how to increase foot traffic in a retail store.

Creating an inviting atmosphere in your store can greatly enhance the customer experience and boost sales. To get more foot traffic in your store, start with warm lighting and engaging displays that draw customers in. Soothing music can also create a comfortable environment, encouraging longer visits.

Next, optimize your store layout. Arrange products strategically to guide customers toward high-margin items, and use clear signage for easy navigation.

Effective visual merchandising is key—eye-catching displays can spark impulse buys, so make sure your products are well-organized and appealing.

Personalize customer service by training your staff to actively listen and provide tailored recommendations, such as product demonstrations.

Lastly, streamline checkout processes with multiple payment options to reduce wait times. This not only enhances the shopping experience but encourages return visits, ultimately leading to increased sales.

Enhancing the in-store experience doesn’t stop at ambiance; technology plays a vital role in personalizing customer interactions.

Start by implementing a Salesforce Customer Relationship Management (CRM) system to capture and analyze customer interactions. This data allows you to tailor marketing strategies that resonate with individual preferences.

Utilize data analytics tools to identify shopping patterns, enabling you to offer personalized recommendations and promotions that can boost engagement and sales by up to 30%.

Consider developing a mobile app that offers personalized discounts based on purchase history, encouraging loyalty and repeat visits.

Use real-time inventory management systems to alert customers about product availability and suggest alternatives, improving satisfaction.

Finally, integrate AI chatbots on your website for instant, personalized customer support.

These steps not only enhance the shopping experience but also show customers you understand their needs, which is key to driving traffic to your store.

Promotions and incentives can drive significant sales growth, so it’s essential to craft offers that truly engage your customers. Start with limited-time offers, like flash sales, to create urgency; this can lead to a 50% increase in impulse purchases.

Consider bundled discounts, as they can boost average transaction value by up to 30% when customers see greater value in combined products.

Seasonal promotions tailored to holidays can also increase sales by 20-30%.

Don’t forget loyalty rewards; implementing a points-based program can encourage repeated purchases, with 83% of consumers influenced by membership.

Finally, offer exclusive deals to loyalty members, providing them with early access to sales. This not only enhances retention but can also generate word-of-mouth referrals.

To boost your store’s sales, streamlining the checkout process is essential, especially since customers value speed and convenience.

Here are three effective strategies you can implement today:

The 3-3-3 rule in sales involves asking three open-ended questions, providing three solutions, and outlining three benefits.

First, engage your customer by asking questions that uncover their needs.

Next, suggest tailored solutions based on their responses.

Finally, clearly state the benefits of your solutions, showing how they meet the customer’s challenges.

This approach promotes deeper engagement, builds trust, and can lead to higher conversion rates in your sales process.

The five C’s of sales are Customer, Cost, Communication, Convenience, and Consistency.

First, identify your ideal customer to tailor your offerings.

Next, focus on value instead of just cost to attract buyers.

Communicate clearly and authentically to build trust.

Make shopping convenient by providing multiple payment options and a smooth process.

Finally, guarantee your brand messaging and service quality are consistent across all platforms to reinforce customer loyalty and encourage repeat purchases.

The 7 C’s in sales are Customer, Cost, Convenience, Communication, Consistency, Connection, and Credibility.

Start by understanding your customer’s needs to tailor your offerings. Emphasize the value of your products instead of just the price.

Make purchasing easy with flexible options. Communicate clearly to build trust, guarantee consistent experiences across all touchpoints, create emotional ties with your customers, and establish credibility through reliable service.

Each aspect strengthens customer engagement and loyalty.

The 5 P’s of successful selling are Product, Price, Place, Promotion, and People.

First, guarantee your product meets customer needs.

Next, set competitive prices that reflect value.

Then, optimize your store layout for easy navigation.

Use targeted promotions to create urgency and drive foot traffic.

Finally, invest in training your staff to improve customer service.

By implementing these five strategies, you can quickly boost your store sales. Start by gathering customer data to enhance engagement, then improve your in-store atmosphere for a better shopping experience. Use technology to personalize interactions, create appealing promotions to attract buyers, and streamline your checkout process for efficiency. Focus on these actionable steps, and you’ll see positive changes in your sales figures. Take action now, and watch your business thrive!
