#230. Target IPO BEI 2027 Sebanyak 25 Perusahaan, Kepercayaan Investor Masih Jadi Tantangan

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Pulina Nityakanti | Editor: Handoyo

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Pemulihan kepercayaan investor menjadi pekerjaan rumah utama pasar modal Indonesia untuk mendorong kembali aktivitas penawaran umum perdana saham atau initial public offering (IPO).

Meski Bursa Efek Indonesia (BEI) menargetkan pencatatan 905 efek pada 2027, meningkat 13,1% dibandingkan target dalam Rencana Kerja dan Anggaran Tahunan (RKAT) 2026 revisi sebanyak 800 efek. Target tersebut terdiri atas 25 pencatatan saham baru, 175 emisi obligasi dan sukuk, serta 705 efek lainnya, termasuk waran terstruktur dan produk investasi kolektif.

Sementara itu, target IPO pada 2026 telah direvisi menjadi 15 perusahaan, dari sebelumnya 50 perusahaan. Penurunan target tersebut mencapai sekitar 70%.

Hingga akhir Oktober 2026, baru tujuh emiten yang mencatatkan sahamnya di Bursa Efek Indonesia. Dengan demikian, masih diperlukan delapan emiten tambahan untuk memenuhi target revisi IPO tahun ini.

Direktur Utama BEI Jeffrey Hendrik mengatakan, pihaknya berharap kondisi pasar modal membaik sehingga target pencatatan efek pada 2027 dapat tercapai.

Di saat bersamaan, BEI melihat pemerintah memiliki peran aktif dalam menggerakkan perekonomian, salah satunya melalui pembangunan kawasan industri.

Baca Juga: Wall Street Dibuka Melemah, Tertekan Tingginya Rencana Belanja AI

“Hal itu tentu akan membutuhkan pembiayaan. Salah satu potensi pembiayaannya melalui pasar modal. Ini salah satu potensi yang kami lihat,” ujarnya kepada wartawan di Gedung BEI, Rabu (28/10/2026).

Selain pembangunan kawasan industri, restrukturisasi badan usaha milik negara (BUMN) yang tengah dilakukan oleh BPI Danantara juga dinilai berpotensi menjadi katalis bagi aktivitas IPO pada tahun depan.

Namun, Jeffrey belum merinci jumlah perusahaan pelat merah yang berpotensi melantai di Bursa melalui aksi tersebut. Target IPO 2027 Dinilai Masih Kurang Realistis

Pengamat pasar modal sekaligus Direktur Avere Investama Teguh Hidayat menilai, target IPO BEI masih menghadapi tantangan untuk dapat direalisasikan.

Menurut dia, target IPO 2026 sulit tercapai karena waktu yang tersisa hingga akhir tahun semakin terbatas. Sementara itu, target 2027 juga belum sepenuhnya realistis mengingat tingginya ketidakpastian pasar, terutama terkait persoalan klasifikasi pasar Indonesia oleh Morgan Stanley Capital International (MSCI).

Pada paruh pertama 2027, ketidakpastian diperkirakan masih tinggi hingga Juni, yang menjadi tenggat keputusan MSCI mengenai klasifikasi pasar Indonesia.

“Apa pun keputusannya, pasar saham Indonesia baru akan normal setelah Juni 2027. Baru kita bisa melihat kembali target IPO ke depan,” ujarnya kepada Kontan, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Sementara itu, pengamat pasar modal dari Universitas Indonesia Budi Frensidy menilai peluang pemulihan aktivitas IPO pada 2027 masih terbuka, tetapi prosesnya tidak akan berlangsung secara instan.

Menurut dia, prospek IPO akan sangat bergantung pada stabilitas ekonomi, arah suku bunga, arus dana asing, serta tingkat kepercayaan investor terhadap pasar modal Indonesia.

Rencana IPO BUMN melalui Danantara juga berpotensi menjadi katalis positif. Namun, perusahaan yang ditawarkan kepada publik harus memiliki fundamental dan tata kelola yang baik, serta valuasi yang wajar.

Baca Juga: BEI Luncurkan Indeks Baru IDX Commodity 20, Ini Daftar Sahamnya

“Target 25 IPO di 2027 cukup realistis, tetapi yang lebih penting bukan jumlah perusahaan yang IPO, melainkan kualitas dan besarnya dana yang berhasil dihimpun,” ujarnya kepada Kontan, Kamis (29/10).

Analis pasar modal Alfred Nainggolan juga melihat peluang perbaikan tren IPO pada 2027. Menurut dia, koreksi dalam yang terjadi pada Indeks Harga Saham Gabungan (IHSG) saat ini dapat membuka peluang bagi aktivitas pasar modal untuk kembali tumbuh.

Meski demikian, risiko eksternal masih perlu diperhatikan, terutama konflik geopolitik yang berpotensi memengaruhi sentimen investor.

“Masih ada tantangan dari konflik geopolitik. Namun, pasar sudah mulai menunjukkan resiliensi, karena sudah terbiasa dengan eskalasi konflik Timur Tengah,” ujarnya kepada Kontan, Kamis (29/10/2026). Kepercayaan Investor dan Kualitas Emiten Jadi Kunci

Managing Director PT Samuel Sekuritas Indonesia Harry Su menilai, tidak tercapainya target IPO dapat membatasi pertumbuhan pendapatan BEI dari pencatatan saham baru sekaligus mengurangi pilihan investasi bagi investor. Namun, kondisi tersebut tidak secara otomatis menekan pergerakan IHSG.

Untuk mendorong pencapaian target IPO, BEI perlu meningkatkan kepastian proses pencatatan saham, memperkuat pendampingan bagi calon emiten, serta meningkatkan perlindungan investor tanpa mengabaikan kualitas perusahaan yang masuk ke Bursa.

Baca Juga: Harga Emas Dunia dan Antam Ikut Melemah Saat IHSG Anjlok, Cek Pemicunya

“Pencatatan IPO bukan jaminan keuntungan, sehingga hindari membeli hanya karena antusiasme saat pencatatan,” ungkapnya, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Harry mengatakan, di tengah lesunya aktivitas IPO, perusahaan masih memiliki sejumlah alternatif pendanaan. Pilihan tersebut antara lain pinjaman perbankan, penerbitan obligasi, serta masuknya investor strategis melalui mekanisme private placement.

Di sisi lain, Alfred menilai potensi kenaikan suku bunga bank sentral pada akhir 2026 dapat membuat perusahaan lebih berhati-hati dalam menentukan sumber pendanaan ekspansi hingga 2027. Perusahaan perlu mempertimbangkan kembali biaya pendanaan yang bersumber dari pinjaman maupun penerbitan surat utang.

“Pemerintah juga harus bisa menciptakan iklim yang lebih kondusif, mengingat kepercayaan pelaku pasar ini penting untuk meningkatkan ekspansi bisnis,” tuturnya.

Teguh Hidayat memperkirakan aksi backdoor listing berpotensi semakin marak di tengah terbatasnya aktivitas IPO. Melalui mekanisme ini, perusahaan dapat memperoleh akses pencatatan di Bursa melalui akuisisi atau merger dengan perusahaan yang telah tercatat.

Selain itu, persoalan kepercayaan pasar terhadap pemerintah dinilai semakin penting untuk diperhatikan, terutama di tengah sejumlah langkah yang dilakukan Danantara dan pejabat pengurusnya.

Menurut Teguh, investor ritel tidak memiliki banyak pilihan untuk mengubah kondisi pasar dalam jangka pendek. Meski demikian, investor yang masih memiliki dana untuk berinvestasi di pasar saham dapat mempertimbangkan akumulasi saham perusahaan dengan fundamental yang baik.

Budi Frensidy juga menilai perusahaan kemungkinan akan lebih mengandalkan pinjaman bank, penerbitan obligasi, laba ditahan, atau investor strategis untuk membiayai ekspansi.

Namun, pendanaan melalui utang memiliki keterbatasan karena dapat meningkatkan beban bunga dan risiko keuangan perusahaan.

Dengan kondisi tersebut, ekspansi bisnis tidak harus dihentikan. Perusahaan hanya perlu lebih selektif dalam memilih proyek dan mempertimbangkan penundaan investasi yang menawarkan tingkat pengembalian kurang menarik.

Baca Juga: Semen Indonesia (SMGR) Berencana Buyback Saham, Ini Tujuannya

“Kondisi ini bisa berlanjut sampai kepercayaan investor dan prospek ekonomi membaik,” tuturnya. BEI Diminta Prioritaskan Pemulihan Kepercayaan Pasar

Budi menilai prioritas organisasi regulator mandiri atau self-regulatory organization (SRO) di pasar modal saat ini seharusnya berfokus pada pemulihan kepercayaan investor. Upaya tersebut dapat dilakukan melalui peningkatan transparansi, penegakan hukum, serta perbaikan kualitas emiten.

Menurut dia, BEI tidak perlu terlalu berorientasi pada jumlah perusahaan yang melakukan IPO. Lebih baik jumlah IPO lebih sedikit, tetapi perusahaan yang masuk memiliki kualitas bisnis dan kapitalisasi pasar besar, dibandingkan banyak perusahaan yang sahamnya kemudian tidak likuid atau berkinerja buruk.

“Pemerintah juga perlu menjaga stabilitas kebijakan ekonomi dan memberikan kepastian berusaha,” ungkapnya.

Selain perbaikan dari sisi regulator dan pemerintah, investor ritel juga perlu meningkatkan kehati-hatian dalam menilai peluang investasi saham perdana. Tidak semua IPO dapat memberikan keuntungan dalam waktu singkat.

Investor perlu mencermati valuasi perusahaan, prospek laba, arus kas, penggunaan dana hasil IPO, rekam jejak pemegang saham pengendali, hingga kualitas penjamin emisi efek.

“Terutama untuk IPO BUMN, jangan berasumsi bahwa dukungan pemerintah otomatis menjamin keuntungan investasi,” katanya.

#215. Fondasi Finansial Generasi Muda Harus Diperkuat Sejak Dini

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Dina Mirayanti Hutauruk | Editor: Dina Hutauruk

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Literasi keuangan menjadi bekal penting bagi generasi muda untuk mengelola uang, memahami risiko, dan mengambil keputusan finansial secara bijak. Penguatan literasi ini turut mendukung peningkatan kualitas sumber daya manusia yang unggul dan berdaya saing.

Penguatan literasi keuangan tidak hanya menjadi tanggung jawab pemerintah dan regulator, tetapi juga membutuhkan peran aktif pelaku usaha di sektor keuangan. Melalui edukasi yang berkelanjutan, pelaku industri dapat membantu masyarakat memahami produk keuangan, mengenali risiko, serta mengambil keputusan finansial secara bijak dan bertanggung jawab.

Pengamat BUMN Toto Pranoto menilai, keterlibatan dunia usaha dalam edukasi keuangan dapat meningkatkan pengetahuan dan keterampilan masyarakat dalam mengelola keuangan. Perusahaan jasa keuangan, termasuk sektor nonbank, berperan membantu masyarakat memahami manfaat dan risiko berbagai produk keuangan.

“Perusahaan yang bergerak di sektor jasa keuangan nonbank memiliki peluang untuk berkontribusi dalam meningkatkan literasi keuangan publik. Dengan demikian, masyarakat dapat memperoleh pemahaman yang lebih baik mengenai layanan keuangan, termasuk manfaat dan risiko yang menyertainya,” ujar Toto dalam keterangannya, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Baca Juga: Bank Mandiri Akan Melepas 20% Saham Inhealth ke IFG Life, Nilainya Rp 570 Miliar

Menurutnya, penguatan literasi keuangan perlu menyesuaikan perkembangan teknologi dan kebutuhan industri. Edukasi sejak dini penting untuk membentuk kebiasaan finansial yang sehat sekaligus meningkatkan pemahaman generasi muda terhadap risiko keuangan di era digital.

Berdasarkan Survei Nasional Literasi dan Inklusi Keuangan (SNLIK) OJK 2024, tingkat literasi keuangan kelompok usia 15–17 tahun baru mencapai 51,70%, di bawah rata-rata nasional sebesar 65,43%. Sementara itu, tingkat inklusi keuangan kelompok tersebut mencapai 85,10%. Kesenjangan ini menunjukkan pentingnya mengimbangi akses layanan keuangan dengan pemahaman yang memadai mengenai manfaat, risiko, dan penggunaannya.

Salah satu upaya menjawab tantangan tersebut dilakukan Indonesia Financial Group (IFG melalui program IFG Goes to School yang berkolaborasi dengan Sekolah Alam Bogor dan Jaringan Sekolah Alam Nusantara. Menggunakan metode game-based learning, program ini membekali siswa dengan pemahaman tentang pengelolaan keuangan, risiko finansial di era digital, serta pentingnya proteksi dan manajemen risiko.

Baca Juga: IFG Life Perkuat Layanan dan Perluas Akses Proteksi

Selain melalui edukasi di sekolah, IFG memperluas literasi keuangan melalui kegiatan publik yang menekankan pentingnya perlindungan risiko. Salah satunya melalui kegiatan tahunan untuk memperingati Hari Asuransi, yang bertujuan meningkatkan kesadaran masyarakat terhadap peran asuransi dalam perencanaan keuangan dan perlindungan dari berbagai risiko.

IFG juga berpartisipasi dalam Jogja Financial Festival 2026 untuk memperluas edukasi mengenai pengelolaan keuangan dan pentingnya perencanaan keuangan jangka panjang. Sekretaris Perusahaan IFG Denny S. Adji mengatakan, literasi keuangan menjadi salah satu faktor penting dalam memperkuat ketahanan ekonomi masyarakat sekaligus mendukung keberlanjutan industri jasa keuangan nasional.

“IFG bersama anggota holding terus memperkuat komitmen dalam meningkatkan literasi dan inklusi keuangan masyarakat, khususnya pada sektor asuransi, penjaminan, dan investasi. Kami meyakini bahwa pemahaman yang baik mengenai pengelolaan keuangan dan perlindungan risiko dapat membantu masyarakat mengambil keputusan keuangan secara lebih bijak sekaligus memperkuat ketahanan ekonomi,” ujar Denny.

Ke depan, sinergi pemerintah, lembaga pendidikan, dunia usaha, dan industri jasa keuangan diperlukan untuk memperluas jangkauan edukasi. Penguatan literasi sejak dini diharapkan membekali generasi muda dengan kemampuan mengelola keuangan secara bertanggung jawab dan menghadapi risiko ekonomi di masa depan.

#249. Apjatel Minta Ada Dialog soal Kabel Udara, Soroti Kepastian Usaha Operator

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Ahmad Febrian | Editor: Ahmad Febrian

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA.  Penataan kabel udara menjadi salah satu tantangan pemerintah daerah memperbaiki estetika kota sekaligus menjaga keandalan infrastruktur telekomunikasi.

Proses pemindahan kabel ke bawah tanah membutuhkan kesiapan jalur pengganti serta koordinasi antara pemerintah daerah dan operator agar tidak mengganggu layanan kepada masyarakat. Maka, Asosiasi Penyelenggara Jaringan Telekomunikasi (Apjatel) meminta pemerintanh daerah embuka forum dialog dengan operator untuk membahas rencana penataan kabel udara. Termasuk kesiapan infrastruktur dan tahapan pemindahan jaringan. Salah satunya Pemerintah Kota Bandung.

Ketua Umum Apjatel Jerry Siregar mengatakan, dialog diperlukan untuk membahas kesiapan infrastruktur pengganti, mekanisme pemindahan jaringan, serta ketentuan sewa yang berlaku.  Menurut dia, penataan perlu mempertimbangkan kondisi teknis di lapangan dan keberlangsungan layanan telekomunikasi.

“Kami percaya kebijakan yang diambil tetap berpijak pada keseimbangan antara kepentingan penataan kota dan pelestarian estetika, namun tidak mengabaikan keberlangsungan layanan telekomunikasi kepada masyarakat serta kepastian hukum bagi para penyelenggara jaringan telekomunikasi,” ujar Jerry, Kamis (29/10).

Apjatel merujuk pada rapat koordinasi implementasi konsep sewa Barang Milik Daerah (BMD) berdasarkan Peraturan Menteri Dalam Negeri Nomor 7 Tahun 2024 yang digelar Kementerian Dalam Negeri pada 20 Januari 2026. Rapat tersebut dipimpin Wakil Menteri Dalam Negeri Bima Arya Sugiarto bersama Wakil Menteri Komunikasi dan Digital Nezar Patria.

Menurut Apjatel, rapat tersebut menyepakati bahwa pemerintah daerah tidak melakukan pemotongan kabel serat optik secara sepihak dengan alasan retribusi atau sewa.

“Tidak ada pemotongan kabel fiber optik yang dilakukan secara sepihak oleh Pemerintah Daerah dengan alasan retribusi atau sewa,” kata Jerry mengutip poin kesepakatan rapat tersebut.

Apjatel menilai, pemutusan kabel oleh Pemkot Bandung pada 2 Juni 2026 perlu ditinjau dengan mengacu pada kesepakatan tersebut. Notula rapat Kemendagri, menurut asosiasi, juga mengamanatkan pembentukan tim lintas pemangku kepentingan untuk menyusun surat edaran bersama, menjaga keberlangsungan layanan publik, dan memastikan skema penataan sesuai regulasi.

Baca Juga: Menakar Peluang dan Tantangan Penguatan Pasar Gas Bumi Domestik

Dari sisi aturan, Apjatel menyoroti Pasal 128B ayat (7) Permendagri Nomor 7 Tahun 2024 yang mengatur faktor penyesuai tarif sewa sebesar 0% untuk infrastruktur telekomunikasi apabila objek sewa berupa tanah belum tersedia Sarana Jaringan Utilitas Terpadu (SJUT).

Apjatel menyebut, ketersediaan SJUT yang memenuhi standar teknis dan kebutuhan operator di Kota Bandung belum merata. Karena itu, kesiapan infrastruktur pengganti menjadi salah satu hal yang perlu dibahas sebelum pemindahan jaringan dilakukan.

Asosiasi tersebut juga merujuk Peraturan Pemerintah Nomor 46 Tahun 2021 tentang Pos, Telekomunikasi, dan Penyiaran yang mengatur peran pemerintah dalam memfasilitasi kepastian berusaha.

Selain itu, Apjatel mengacu pada ketentuan Undang-Undang Nomor 36 Tahun 1999 tentang Telekomunikasi terkait larangan tindakan yang dapat mengakibatkan gangguan penyelenggaraan telekomunikasi.

Selain persoalan regulasi, pemindahan kabel juga menghadapi kendala teknis. Berdasarkan evaluasi Apjatel bersama operator, sejumlah jalur ducting dan subduct yang menjadi infrastruktur pengganti masih tersumbat, kapasitasnya terbatas, dan standar teknis migrasi antarinstansi belum seragam.

Kondisi tersebut membuat pemindahan jaringan membutuhkan evaluasi dan koordinasi lebih lanjut. Apjatel menilai, gangguan dalam proses migrasi berpotensi memengaruhi layanan internet rumah tangga, layanan pemerintahan digital, fasilitas kesehatan yang menggunakan telemedicine, perbankan, sistem pembayaran digital, hingga operasional usaha mikro, kecil, dan menengah (UMKM).

Untuk mengantisipasi risiko tersebut, Apjatel meminta Pemkot Bandung membuka forum dialog dengan asosiasi dan operator telekomunikasi sebelum menetapkan batas waktu penertiban jaringan.

“Pemkot Bandung perlu membuka dialog dan mengajak Apjatel serta para anggota operator telekomunikasi untuk duduk bersama dalam forum dialog terbuka dan inklusif guna membahas secara komprehensif rencana penataan jaringan,” ungkap Jerry.

Forum tersebut diharapkan dapat membahas kapasitas ducting, penyelesaian draf perjanjian kerja sama (PKS), penyusunan jadwal migrasi secara bertahap, serta penetapan tarif sewa yang transparan dan sesuai dengan ketentuan perundang-undangan.

Ringkas pengulangan soal forum dialogPertajam lead dengan dampak bisnis

#103. Celebrity deaths in 2026: Remembering the famous people we lost this year

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Below, we look back at the celebrities we lost in 2026, listed in alphabetical order.

Scott Adams worked at a telephone company in the 1980s. The experience inspired his beloved comic strip, "Dilbert."

By the end of the decade, the character and his satirical office humor began appearing in comic strips.

At its peak, "Dilbert" was syndicated in over 2,000 newspapers. A cartoon series debuted in 1999, running for two seasons.

Adams gained notoriety when he began writing blog posts praising Donald Trump during his 2016 presidential run. In 2023, "Dilbert" was pulled from newspapers across the US following racist comments made by Adams.

Adams died on January 13 from metastatic prostate cancer.

The lead singer of 3 Doors Down, Brad Arnold launched the group into fame with the 2000 rock anthem "Kryptonite," which peaked at No. 3 on the Billboard Hot 100 that year.

The success led to other hits like "Loser," "Here Without You," and "When I'm Gone." Arnold's vocal range and lyrics focused on love and love lost, which fueled the group to stardom.

Arnold died on February 7 from a type of kidney cancer.

A hip-hop pioneer during the 1970s and 1980s, Lance Taylor, known professionally as Afrika Bambaataa, first gained attention as a DJ in the Bronx.

He signed with Tommy Boy Records in 1981 and released landmark songs like "Jazzy Sensation" and "Planet Rock." He would go on to influence the likes of Queen Latifah and A Tribe Called Quest throughout his career.

Bambaataa founded the hip-hop awareness group, Universal Zulu Nation, in the 1970s to steer kids away from gang life. Bambaataa stepped down as the head of the organization in 2016 after accusations of sexual abuse.

He died on April 9 due to prostate cancer.

One of the icons responsible for bringing hip-hop to the mainstream, Rob Bass crafted the iconic track "It Takes Two" alongside DJ E-Z Rock.

The track, released in 1988, reached No. 3 on the Billboard Hot Dance/Club Songs chart and later went certified platinum. In 1989, it was voted the greatest single of all time by Spin magazine.

Nicholas Brendon was a television actor, best known for playing Xander Harris in all seven seasons of "Buffy the Vampire Slayer."

He also starred in "Criminal Minds" and "Kitchen Confidential."

Brendon died in his sleep on March 20 due to natural causes, according to his family's statement.

In 2023, Brendon suffered a heart attack and was diagnosed with a congenital heart defect. He also had cauda equina syndrome, which led to several spinal surgeries."While it's no secret that Nicholas had struggles in the past, he was on medications and treatment to manage his diagnosis, and he was optimistic about the future at the time of his passing," the statement to The Hollywood Reporter read. "Our family asks for privacy during this time as we grieve his loss and celebrate the life of a man who lived with intensity, imagination, and heart."

Two-time Grammy winner Peabo Bryson is best known for his classic love ballads.

Bryson began releasing music in the late 1970s, but one of his first major hits was opposite Roberta Flack on the 1983 duet, "Tonight, I Celebrate My Love." He continued hit-making a year later with the smash "If Ever You're in My Arms Again," and in 1991, he topped the R&B charts with "Can You Stop the Rain."

Bryson found global stardom with his renditions of two Disney classics. In 1991, he released the pop version of "Beauty and the Beast," alongside Céline Dion, and a year later, he duetted with Regina Belle on "A Whole New World" from the 1992 release of "Aladdin."

Bryson died on June 2. No cause was given.

An 11-time Emmy winner and co-creator of "Cheers," director James Burrows helped shape modern TV comedy through his work on iconic sitcoms including "Will & Grace," "Friends," and "Frasier."

Known for his comedic timing and elaborate blocking, Burrows got his start directing episodes of "The Mary Tyler Moore Show." Throughout his career, he directed episodes of "Frasier" (winning an Emmy for the pilot episode), "Will & Grace," "Friends," and "The Big Bang Theory." He directed over 50 pilot episodes.

Burrows died on June 19. No cause was given.

Two-time NASCAR Cup Series champion Busch was considered one of the greatest racers of all time.

Busch was hospitalized on May 21, before two upcoming races. Hours later, he was pronounced dead.

Busch died from severe pneumonia that progressed into sepsis.

Robert Carradine was best known for his roles as Lizzie McGuire's dad and as the iconic geek Lewis Skolnick in the 1980s franchise "Revenge of the Nerds."

As a member of Hollywood's famed Carradine family, Robert was destined for an onscreen career.

He received one of his first screen credits starring in his brother David Carradine's popular 1970s TV series "Kung Fu" and went on to land roles in everything from Samuel Fuller's World War II classic "The Big Red One" to the "Lizzie McGuire" series.

Carradine's family shared statements with Deadline that emphasized being open about his struggles with mental illness.

"We are bereft at the loss of this beautiful soul and want to acknowledge Bobby's valiant struggle against his nearly two-decade battle with Bipolar Disorder," Carradine's family said. "We hope his journey can shine a light and encourage addressing the stigma that attaches to mental illness."

Carradine died by suicide on February 23.

If you or someone you know is experiencing depression or has had thoughts of harming themself or taking their own life, get help. In the US, call or text 988 to reach the Suicide & Crisis Lifeline. Help is also available through the Crisis Text Line — just text "HOME" to 741741. The International Association for Suicide Prevention offers resources for those outside the US.

Most famous for his role as Harold in the 1971 cult-classic "Harold and Maude," Bud Cort became the epitome of 1970s counterculture thanks to his baby-faced looks, wit, and sophisticated persona.

The dark comedy starred Cort as Harold, a man in his 20s who is obsessed with suicide until he meets 79-year-old eccentric Maude (Ruth Gordon).

Before that, Cort scored bit parts in Robert Altman's "M*A*S*H*" (1970) and "Brewster McCloud" (1970).

After "Harold and Maude," Cort worked steadily for the rest of his life, occasionally showing up in hit movies like Michael Mann's "Heat" (1995) and Wes Anderson's "The Life Aquatic with Steve Zissou" (2004).

Cort died on February 11 following a long illness.

Curry is best known for playing Dr. Frank-N-Furter in the cult-classic musical "The Rocky Horror Picture Show," after originating the role in the original stage production.

Over six decades, Curry carved out a niche playing outlandish villains like Rooster Hannigan in the 1982 film "Annie" and Pennywise in the 1990 miniseries "It." Other notable roles include Wadsworth the Butler in "Clue" (1985) and Mr. Hector in "Home Alone 2: Escape from New York."

Curry suffered a major stroke in 2012, which required brain surgery. He said in 2025 that he was "still recovering."

Curry died at his Los Angeles home on August 26. No cause was given.

Long before becoming one of TV's most recognizable heartthrobs, Eric Dane was building his career with guest roles on series like "Saved by the Bell" and "Married… with Children."

He later gained wider attention with appearances on "Charmed" before landing his breakout role as Dr. Mark "McSteamy" Sloan on "Grey's Anatomy." He most recently starred in HBO's "Euphoria," where he played Cal Jacobs, the father of Jacob Elordi's character, Nate Jacobs.

Dane has two children with his wife, Rebecca Gayheart. In April 2025, the actor said that he was diagnosed with amyotrophic lateral sclerosis, or ALS.

Dane died on February 19 at age 53, his publicist confirmed in a statement to Business Insider.

"He spent his final days surrounded by dear friends, his devoted wife, and his two beautiful daughters, Billie and Georgia, who were the center of his world," his publicist said.

A legend in the music world, Davis was responsible for elevating pop music to unthinkable heights by discovering talents like Aretha Franklin, Barry Manilow, Bruce Springsteen, Billy Joel, Whitney Houston, and Alicia Keys, to name a few.

Over his 50-year career, along with being an executive behind millions of album sales, he also won five Grammys, including two for Album of the Year ("The Bodyguard" soundtrack and "Supernatural" by Santana).

Davis died on June 22. No cause was given.

Mike Ditka was the first tight end ever inducted into the Pro Football Hall of Fame. As a coach for the Chicago Bears, he led the team to its only Super Bowl win to date in 1986.

Ditka's success turned him into a celebrity who showed up in commercials, TV shows, and movies in cameo roles.

One of his most famous was starring opposite Will Ferrell in the 2005 comedy "Kicking and Screaming."

His persona became the long-running "Saturday Night Live" sketch called "Bill Swerski's Superfans," in which cast members dressed up as Ditka and spoke about their love for "Da Bears."

Ditka died on October 9; no cause was given.

Robert Duvall starred in a plethora of timeless classics, including "The Godfather," "The Godfather Part II," "Apocalypse Now," "THX 1138," "The Natural," "Days of Thunder," and "The Apostle."

Duvall will be best remembered for starring in three of director Francis Ford Coppola's iconic films: playing Tom Hagen in the first two "Godfather" movies and Lieutenant Colonel Bill Kilgore in "Apocalypse Now," in which he delivers one of the most memorable lines in movie history: "I love the smell of napalm in the morning."

Duvall was lauded for his acting talent, from not saying a word while playing Boo Radley in his first big screen role in the 1962 classic "To Kill a Mockingbird" to his portrayal of a country singer dealing with alcoholism in 1983's "Tender Mercies," which earned him an Oscar.

He died on his ranch in Virginia on February 15. No cause was given.

This Irish actress was a fixture on the stage and screen for six decades. But she's most known for showing up in the "Home Alone" sequel.

As The Pigeon Lady in 1992's "Home Alone 2: Lost in New York," Fricker has become a fixture for families during the holiday season for decades (and in 2020 became the subject of a popular meme).

But Fricker's acting talents go beyond playing a spooky wanderer of Central Park.

In 1990, she became the first Irish actress to win an Academy Award, as she was given the best supporting actress Oscar that year for her performance in "My Left Foot."

Fricker died on July 16; no cause was given.

Fuhrman cemented his place in popular culture as one of the lead LAPD detectives on the 1995 murder case of O.J. Simpson's ex-wife, Nicole Brown Simpson, and her friend Ronald Goldman.

Fuhrman found the famed bloody glove on the Simpson estate, and during the trial, he gained infamy when Simpson's defense team used his past racist language to discredit him as a witness.

Fuhrman retired from the LAPD after the Simpson trial and moved to Idaho, where he wrote true crime books.

For over six decades, one name has been synonymous with women's high fashion: Valentino.

The Italian designer launched his fashion business in Rome in 1960 and never looked back. Soon, every star wanted to be seen in his creations. They've been worn by icons like Elizabeth Taylor, Joan Collins, Jackie Kennedy, Princess Diana, Sharon Stone, and more.

Though Garavani retired from designing in 2008, his label is still a red carpet staple, as is his famous shade of bright red dubbed "Valentino Red."

Garavani died on January 19. No cause was given.

No one defined a bully for a generation more than Gibb's hulking fraternity character Ogre in the "Revenge of the Nerds" movies of the 1980s.

Since the original raunchy comedy was released in 1984, Gibb's intimidating size and loud yells of "NERDS!" have become as memorable as the pocket protectors worn by the movie's geeks.

Gibb went on to star opposite Jean-Claude Van Damme in his classic 1988 action movie "Bloodsport." He also landed guest star roles through his career on shows like "Quantum Leap," "Magnum P.I.," "MacGyver," "Night Court," and "Cheers."

Gibb died on May 12 following health complications.

Gilbert was the guitarist and backing vocalist for the punk band New Found Glory.

The band's hit albums include 2002's "Sticks And Stones" and 2004's "Catalyst." Both showed up in the top 10 on the Billboard 200 chart.

Gilbert died on September 20; no cause was given, but Gilbert told People in April he was battling stage 4 adrenal cancer.

Irish singer-songwriter Hansard was the frontman of the rock band The Frames and one-half of the band The Swell Season. But he was perhaps best known for writing and starring in the hit 2007 indie film "Once," in which he and his Swell Season bandmate Markéta Irglová play two struggling musicians who fall in love. The duo won an Oscar for best original song for the film's standout track, "Falling Slowly."

Hansard also embarked on a solo career; his 2015 album, "Didn't He Ramble," was nominated for a Grammy for best folk album.

Hansard died on July 29 in a motorcycle crash in Dublin.

For those who grew up in the '90s, Dennis Haskins was your favorite high school principal, Mr. Belding.

Starring in the hit TV series "Saved By The Bell," Haskins' Belding character traveled the halls trying to be a step ahead of the hijinks inflicted by Zack Morris (Mark-Paul Gosselaar) and his friends. But unlike many school principals in pop culture who were mean and seemed to hate kids, Haskins played his authority role with a playful, good-natured style.

He was on the show's full four-season run from 1989 to 1993, and also starred in the spin-off, "Saved By The Bell: The New Class," from 1993 to 2000.

Haskins' other credits include "The Bold and the Beautiful," "Mad Men," "It's Always Sunny in Philadelphia," and "New Girl."

Haskins died on September 27; no cause was given.

Rising up through the UK stage scene in the 1980s, Head found fame when he was cast as Rupert Giles, who became a father figure to Sarah Michelle Gellar's Buffy Summers in all seven seasons of "Buffy the Vampire Slayer."

Most recently, he played the bitter former owner of AFC Richmond on "Ted Lasso."

Head's death was announced by his family on June 5. They stated the cause was "complications due to pneumonia."

British painter David Hockney was renowned for his striking portraits and figurative pop art. Many of his best-known paintings, including 1966's "The Splash" and 1972's "Portrait of an Artist (Pool with Two Figures)," were inspired by the bright colors and sun-dappled pools of Los Angeles.

Hockney moved to Los Angeles in the late '60s, and his work became synonymous with the warmth and leisure of Southern California. He was also one of the few popular artists at the time to depict openly gay scenes, and Hockney was outspoken about anti-gay censorship.

Throughout his career, Hockney split time between California, England, and France. He died at his home in London on June 12 at age 88, his publicist confirmed to The New York Times. No cause of death was given.

The Rev. Jesse Jackson was a leader of the Civil Rights Movement, a minister, and an activist icon who twice ran for president.

Born on October 8, 1941, in Greenville, South Carolina, Jackson experienced Jim Crow segregation on public buses and at school firsthand. It would shape the rest of his life.

His fight for civil rights began in the 1960s, when he helped organize protests and demonstrations across the US and worked closely alongside civil rights icon Dr. Martin Luther King Jr.

His decadeslong career as a leading civil rights activist included support for modern national movements, such as the push for voting rights, the fight against racism, and a higher minimum wage.

Jackson ran for president twice, both times as a democrat. He placed third for the party's nomination in 1984 and second in 1988. This marked the most successful presidential runs of any Black candidate prior to Barack Obama.

Jackson announced in 2017 that he'd been diagnosed with Parkinson's disease. In November 2025, Jackson was treated in a Chicago hospital after complications from progressive supranuclear palsy (PSP), a rare neurodegenerative condition.

As the boyish Russian crewman Pavel Chekov on "Star Trek," Walter Koenig left an indelible mark in the minds of sci-fi fans for generations as one of the key crew members of Captain Kirk's ship, the USS Enterprise.

First appearing in the show's second season, Koenig played Chekov in 36 of the 50 episodes that aired in 1967-68 and 1968-69. He also starred in the first seven releases of the movie franchise, from 1979 through 1991.

Koenig died on October 25 of natural causes.

Kusama was a famed Japanese pop artist who often used polka-dot motifs in her work, initially rising to prominence in Japan in the 1950s.

In 1959, Kusama presented her first solo exhibition in New York: a collection of abstract paintings that an ARTnews review praised as "strong, advanced in concept and realized."

Kusama later branched into sculptures, performances, and immersive installations. She continued to expand her influence and relevance into the social media era, when her mirrored "Infinity Room" installations became Instagram-friendly attractions that inspired many copycats.

Kusama died on August 14 in Tokyo. She was 97.

As one of Hollywood's most renowned costume designers, Bob Mackie's eye set the style for decades.

Known as the Sultan of Sequins, the Rajah of Rhinestones, or the Guru of Glitter, a signature of Mackie's work was its use of sparkle, which made everyone from Cher to Elton John to Dolly Parton to Sabrina Carpenter shine on stage.

He also was the costume designer on "The Carol Burnett Show" for all eleven seasons through the 1970s. He won nine Emmys, a Tony Award, and was nominated for three Academy Awards for his work.

Mackie died on September 14; no cause was given.

Mitte was best known for playing the son of Bryan Cranston's Walter White in the hit series "Breaking Bad."

Mitte, who was diagnosed with cerebral palsy when he was 3 years old, became a disability rights activist after finding fame with "Breaking Bad."

His other credits include the 2021 movie "Triumph," in which he played a high school student with cerebral palsy who tries out for the wrestling team. He also starred in the 2024 thriller "Love Me Dead" opposite Dove Cameron.

In the 1990s, you couldn't escape the piercing eyes of Patrick Muldoon.

He played Austin Reed on "Days of Our Lives" for most of the decade, and when he wasn't on the soap, he was over on "Melrose Place" playing the villain role of Richard Hart.

If being on two iconic shows wasn't enough, he also showed up on "Saved by the Bell" and had a role in the cult classic movie "Starship Troopers."

Muldoon died on April 19 after a heart attack.

With a career spanning more than five decades and including countless memorable performances in movies and television, Sam Neill was one of the most versatile actors of his generation,

The New Zealand actor starred opposite Nicole Kidman in the 1989 thriller "Dead Calm," played the loyal first officer opposite Sean Connery in 1990's "The Hunt for Red October," played the lead in John Carpenter's 1994 horror "In the Mouth of Madness," and most recently starred in the hit series "Peaky Blinders."

But Neill is best known for playing the brave and all-dinosaur-knowing Dr. Alan Grant in the "Jurassic Park" franchise.

Neill died on July 13; no cause was given. Neill was battling a rare and aggressive form of blood cancer, but was cancer-free at the time of his death, according to a post on his Instagram account announcing his passing.

Standing at a towering 6' 5", Tom Noonan was born to play the bad guy. And he did just that, starring as the villain in movies like "Manhunter," "RoboCop 2," and "Last Action Hero."

But this actor had more to show off. He showcased his comedy chops as Rodney Dangerfield's degenerate gambling friend in the 1983 comedy "Easy Money," and set up the heist for Robert De Niro in Michael Mann's 1995 classic "Heat."

His knack for storytelling also translated behind the camera. Noonan wrote, directed, and starred in the drama "What Happened Was," which won the grand prize at the 1994 Sundance Film Festival.

Noonan died on February 14; no cause was given.

Few action stars have Chuck Norris' credentials.

Before he ever stepped in front of a camera, he was a martial arts champion and a black belt in Karate, judo, Brazilian Jiu-Jitsu, Taekwondo, and Tang Soo Do.

His talents would become known the world over in 1972, when he faced off against Bruce Lee in "The Way of the Dragon."

Through the 1980s, Norris became one of the biggest action stars in the world thanks to movies like "A Force of One," "The Octagon," and the "Delta Force" and "Missing in Action" franchises.

In the 1990s, he took over the small screen, playing the lead in the "Walker, Texas Ranger" TV show, which aired in over 100 countries during its run from 1993 to 2001.

Norris was hospitalized on March 19 in Hawaii and died the following day, according to his family. No cause was given.

O'Hara, who was best known for her roles in "Beetlejuice" and "Home Alone," got her start in the Canadian comedy scene, appearing in the now-legendary cast of "SCTV."

It would mark the beginning of a fruitful partnership with fellow actor Eugene Levy, who became her frequent costar and collaborator in memorable appearances in Christopher Guest films like "A Mighty Wind" and "Best in Show" and television series like "Schitt's Creek," which earned her an Emmy Award for outstanding lead actress in 2020.

O'Hara's representative confirmed her death to Business Insider on January 30. The Los Angeles County Department of Public Health confirmed in a death certificate viewed by People that O'Hara died of a pulmonary embolism, with rectal cancer listed as an underlying cause.

Panettiere began acting as a child, landing one of her earliest major roles as Sarah Roberts on the ABC soap opera "One Life to Live," on which she appeared from 1994 to 1997.

As her career progressed, she became best known for portraying Claire Bennet on "Heroes" and Juliette Barnes on "Nashville."

Over the years, Panettiere had spoken openly about her struggles with alcoholism and postpartum depression. She detailed those experiences in her memoir, "This Is Me: A Reckoning," which was released in May.

Panettiere died on August 16. A final autopsy report by the Greenville County Coroner's Office ruled her death an accident caused by the toxic effects of fentanyl and other drugs.

Parton released her debut album, "Hello, I'm Dolly," in 1967 and went on to pen country hits like "I Will Always Love You" and "Jolene," both of which were released as singles in 1974.

In addition to her music, Parton was beloved for her flamboyant style and her philanthropy. She founded the Dollywood Foundation in 1988, a charitable organization focused on education and community initiatives in her home state of Tennessee. She continued to fund scholarship programs and donate to hospitals, public health initiatives, and disaster relief programs throughout her life.

Parton is known as the best-selling female country music artist of all time. She was inducted into the Country Music Hall of Fame in 1999, the Songwriters Hall of Fame in 2001, and the Rock & Roll Hall of Fame in 2022.

Parton's 49th and final solo studio album, "Rockstar," was released in 2023. The tracklist included both original material and covers of classic rock songs, featuring an array of guests like Paul McCartney, Ringo Starr, Sting, Joan Jett, and Parton's goddaughter, Miley Cyrus.

News broke on August 25 that Parton died following unspecified health issues. She was 80.

This character actor found fame appearing in a string of mob movies in the 1990s, like "Goodfellas," "Carlito's Way," and "Men of Respect." But Pastore truly became a star when he was cast in the hit HBO series "The Sopranos."

As Salvatore "Big Pussy" Bonpensiero, Pastore was part of the main cast for the first two seasons of the show, playing Tony Soprano's mob enforcer-turned-FBI informant. Bonpensiero's shocking death was one of the most famous in the show's history and proved that no one was safe from getting whacked.

Pastore died on August 1. No cause was given.

Perrine found herself cast alongside the biggest stars of the 1970s.

She played the love interest opposite Jeff Bridges in 1973's "The Last American Hero" and Robert Redford in 1979's "The Electric Horseman." She earned an Oscar nomination for starring alongside Dustin Hoffman in 1974's "Lenny."

For most, she'll be remembered best for sharing the screen with Gene Hackman as the secretary to his evil Lex Luthor in 1978's "Superman." She also starred in the sequel. As Ms. Teschmacher, her loyalty to Luthor is questioned when she rescues Superman (Christopher Reeve) after he's chained with Kryptonite.

Perrine died in her home in Beverly Hills on March 23. She was diagnosed with Parkinson's disease in 2015.

A prolific character actor, Riehle has over 400 screen credits, appearing in everything from "Casino" to "Bridesmaids" over his career.

He's best known for playing the worker in "Office Space" who, after being laid off, wins a large payout from a lawsuit after a suicide attempt.

Other credits include "The Fugitive," "Fear and Loathing in Las Vegas," and "Transformers: Age of Extinction."

Riehle died on October 15; no cause was given.

One of the most influential jazz musicians in history, Rollins recorded more than 60 albums over his seven-decade career.

His live album, "A Night at the Village Vanguard," is considered one of the greatest jazz albums ever made.

Rollins died on May 25; no cause was given.

An Oscar and Emmy winner, Eva Marie Saint appeared in classic movies in Hollywood history.

She made her film debut starring opposite Marlon Brando in Elia Kazan's 1954 classic "On the Waterfront," which won her the Oscar for best supporting actress. In 1959, she heated up the screen with Cary Grant in Alfred Hitchcock's defining thriller "North by Northwest."

In 1990, she won an Emmy for her work in the miniseries "People Like Us."

Saint died on October 6 of natural causes.

Saint James was a fixture in television from the late 1960s through the 1980s, starring in "The Name of the Game," "McMillan & Wife," and "Kate & Allie."

She was Emmy-nominated eight times and won an Emmy for lead actress in 1985 for "Kate & Allie."

Saint James' movie credits include "Outlaw Blues" (1977), "Love at First Bite" (1979), and "How to Beat the High Cost of Living (1980).

Saint James died on October 26 after being diagnosed with pancreatic cancer.

Sedaka is responsible for some of the biggest pop songs of the 1960s and 1970s.

The Brooklyn native wrote over 500 songs, including classic hits like "Laughter in the Rain," "Breaking Up Is Hard to Do," "Bad Blood" and the Captain & Tennille song, "Love Will Keep Us Together."

Sedaka died on February 27; no cause was given.

Before there was Rotten Tomatoes or even Siskel & Ebert, movie lovers got their recommendations from Gene Shalit.

The iconic movie critic could be seen sporting his signature look of glasses, bushy hair, and big mustache as a staple on the "Today" show from 1973 to 2010. If he wasn't interviewing the biggest movie stars, he was reviewing movies with incredible puns ("'Ishtar' is Ish-terrible.")

His larger-than-life presence left a mark on pop culture: he was parodied as a Muppet and on countless episodes of "SCTV" and "Saturday Night Live."

Shalit died on June 12; no cause was given.

Sheik found success in the late 1990s with his hit song "Barely Breathing." He later pivoted from alt-rock to a career on Broadway, composing the music for the Tony-winning musical "Spring Awakening." His other stage credits include "American Psycho" (2013) and "The Secret Life of Bees" (2019).

Sheik died on September 17 after a cardiac arrest.

In her 15-year tenure as a co-host on "Showtime at the Apollo," Kiki Shepard was considered royalty at Harlem's famed Apollo Theater.

Known in the building as "Apollo Queen of Fashion" thanks to her dazzling outfits, Shepherd was a fixture on the show from 1987 to 2002.

Throughout her career, Shepard also appeared on TV shows like "Baywatch," "A Different World," "NYPD Blue," and "Grey's Anatomy."

Shepard died on March 16 after suffering a heart attack.

A beloved actor in Hollywood for over six decades, Tom Skerritt appeared in some legendary movies and television.

Robert Altman gave Skerritt his big break when he cast him as the womanizing and sarcastic surgeon Captain "Duke" Forrest in the classic 1970 movie "M*A*S*H." Skerritt followed it up with a string of hits, including Altman's "Thieves Like Us" (1974), "The Turning Point" (1977), and a hilarious cameo in Cheech & Chong's classic debut movie "Up in Smoke" (1978).

Then, Skerritt went sci-fi when Ridley Scott cast him as Captain Arthur Dallas in 1979's "Alien." Skerritt also scored a memorable role as Tom Cruise's flight instructor "Viper" in 1986's "Top Gun."

On the small screen, Skerritt earned an Emmy for playing Sheriff Jimmy Brock on the hit series "Picket Fences," which ran from 1992 to 1996.

Skerritt died on October 21 of natural causes.

An icon in the women's liberation movement of the 1970s, Steinem dedicated most of her life to fighting sexism.

Starting her career as a journalist, she became a columnist for the newly founded New York Magazine in 1968. She cofounded Ms. magazine in 1971, which focused on women's issues. She also helped launch the Women's Action Alliance, which focused on tackling sexism.

Steinem was awarded the Presidential Medal of Freedom in 2013.

Steinem died on September 2, according to a post on her social media. No cause was given.

In a career spanning over 60 years on stage and screen, Stewart is responsible for some of the most memorable characters in the biggest franchises.

After getting his start with the Royal Shakespeare Company in the late 1960s, he found acclaim on the stage, performing Shakespearean classics like "Antony and Cleopatra" and "Hamlet," both of which earned him Laurence Olivier Awards.

Hollywood soon came calling, leading him to star in epics like "Excalibur" (1981) and David Lynch's "Dune" (1984). But he would get his big break in 1987 when he was cast as Captain Jean-Luc Picard in "Star Trek: The Next Generation." Fans quickly gravitated to this new captain of the USS Enterprise, and the show ran until 1994. He also reprised the Picard role in four movies in the franchise: "Star Trek Generations" (1994), "Star Trek: First Contact" (1996), "Star Trek: Insurrection" (1998), and "Star Trek: Nemesis" (2002), as well as in the TV series "Star Trek: Picard" (2020-2023).

In 2000, he joined the comic-book world when he was cast as mutant Professor Charles Xavier, founder of the X-Men in the Marvel Comics. Stewart portrayed Xavier in several more "X-Men" films, and will reprise the role in the forthcoming MCU film "Avengers: Doomsday," out this December.

Stewart died on October 28 after suffering from Alzheimer's and Lewy Body Dementia.

Hungarian filmmaker Béla Tarr became a legend in the arthouse world for his unflinching work featuring several-minute-long takes.

With movies like "Family Nest" (1979) and "The Turin Horse" (2011), Tarr showcased his slow cinema style while focusing on marginalized characters living bleak lives. Tarr pushed the envelope with his style when he made "Sátántangó" in 1994, which had a run time of 450 minutes.

His work influenced several arthouse filmmakers, including Jim Jarmusch and Gus Van Sant's "Death Trilogy" ("Gerry," "Elephant," and "Last Days").

Tarr died on January 6 following a battle with a "long and serious illness."

You might not recognize the name, but if you're a movie fan, you know the face.

Tolkan's tough-guy persona made a mark on movie history in films like "Top Gun" and the "Back to the Future" franchise.

In "Top Gun," he played Commander Tom "Stinger" Jardian, who famously gives Tom Cruise's character a tongue lashing, saying, "Your ego is writing checks your body can't cash."

In "Back to the Future" and its sequels, he played Principal Strickland, who had zero tolerance for "slackers."

Other credits include "Serpico," "WarGames," "Masters of the Universe," and "Dick Tracy."

Tolkan died on March 26; no cause was given.

Ted Turner changed the television landscape thanks to his maverick maneuvers to harness the 24-hour news cycle.

After taking over his father's advertising company in the 1960s at the age of 24, Turner embarked on a path to change the media landscape by buying up radio and TV stations around the South. He even bought sports teams including the Atlanta Braves MLB team and the Atlanta Hawks NBA team.

All of that programming would eventually become part of TBS, a "super station" that showed old movies, reruns, and Braves and Hawks games.

Turner then set his sights on the news. He founded CNN, the first 24-hour news channel, in June of 1980.

Over the decades, Turner gained celebrity status, particularly after marrying Jane Fonda, a union that lasted from 1991 to 2001. In 1991, he was named Time's "Man of the Year." He sold his networks to Time Warner for $7.5 billion in 1996.

Turner died on May 6; no cause was given.

Welsh singer Bonnie Tyler became one of the biggest artists of the 1980s thanks to her legendary power ballads.

Tyler was behind hits like "Total Eclipse of the Heart" and "Holding Out for a Hero," which was also featured on the 1984 "Footloose" soundtrack. Both songs were chart-toppers that have since become two of the best-selling singles of all time.

Over her career, Tyler was nominated for three Grammys and was appointed a Member of the Order of the British Empire (MBE) by Queen Elizabeth II in 2022.

Tyler died on July 8 in Portugal, where she was being treated for an illness, according to a statement posted on her social media.

Van Der Beek was beloved all over the world for his role as the titular teen Dawson Leery on the WB series "Dawson's Creek."

The series, which ran from 1998 to 2002, turned Van Der Beek into a star and a teen heartthrob. While "Dawson's Creek" was still airing, he'd cement his stardom playing the lead role of high school quarterback "Mox" Moxon in the 1999 coming-of-age drama "Varsity Blues."

Van Der Beek found steady work the rest of his career in movies like "Texas Rangers" and "The Rules of Attraction, and memorably played a fictional version of himself on the TV series "Don't Trust the B---- in Apartment 23," and performed on "Dancing with the Stars" in 2019.

Van Der Beek learned he had stage 3 colon cancer in 2023, and publicly announced his diagnosis in late 2024.

Van Der Beek previously told Business Insider that the news came as a shock, given the subtleness of his symptoms and his efforts to stay as healthy as possible.

"I was doing everything," he said. "I was doing sauna, cold plunge, weightlifting, Pilates. I would dance and also do football training."

Weir was one of the founding members of the Grateful Dead.

The singer-songwriter wrote and/or sang on songs like "Sugar Magnolia," "Playing in the Band," "Truckin,'" "I Need a Miracle," and "The Other One."

After the Grateful Dead disbanded in 1995, Weir went on to form several other bands, including Kingfish, Bobby and the Midnites, RatDog, The Other Ones, and, alongside John Mayer, Dead & Company.

In the summer of 2025, Weir was diagnosed with cancer.

Willis was the lead singer of the iconic disco band the Village People.

Along with being the lead vocalist and dressing as the police officer (and sometimes naval officer), Willis was also the cowriter on many of the band's hits, including "In the Navy," "Macho Man," and the song that will keep the Village People in pop culture lore until the end of time, "Y.M.C.A."

Willis left the band to embark on a solo career in 1979, then re-formed the Village People in 2017.

Willis died on June 30 following a "short but aggressive illness," according to the Village People Facebook page.

For over five decades, Wiseman's brand of non-fiction storytelling put a spotlight on everyday people.

From high schoolers to hospital workers to Air Force cadets training for nuclear war, Wiseman's close to 50-film catalogue is an unvarnished reflection of America over the last half century.

Wiseman died on February 16. No cause was given.

#239. Wall Street Dibuka Melemah, Tertekan Tingginya Rencana Belanja AI

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Avanty Nurdiana | Editor: Avanty Nurdiana

KONTAN.CO.ID - NEW YORK. Pasar saham Amerika Serikat (AS) dibuka melemah pada Kamis (29/10/2026) setelah laporan keuangan Alphabet kembali memicu kekhawatiran mengenai melonjaknya biaya kecerdasan buatan (AI). Investor juga mencermati data inflasi terbaru setelah Federal Reserve memutuskan untuk mempertahankan suku bunga.

Pada saat pembukaan perdagangan, indeks Dow Jones Industrial Average turun 389,0 poin atau 0,74% menjadi 52.086,13.

Indeks S&P 500 melemah 44,4 poin atau 0,57% menjadi 7.699,61, sedangkan indeks Nasdaq Composite turun 348,0 poin atau 1,31% menjadi 26.201,823.

Baca Juga: BEI Luncurkan Indeks Baru IDX Commodity 20, Ini Daftar Sahamnya

Saham Alphabet turun 11,5% dalam perdagangan sebelum pembukaan pasar. Jika penurunan tersebut bertahan, perusahaan itu berpotensi kehilangan lebih dari US$ 450 miliar dalam nilai pasar setelah melaporkan hasil iklan mesin pencari yang mengecewakan dan lonjakan pengeluaran kas akibat percepatan belanja terkait AI.

Penurunan saham perusahaan induk Google tersebut menyumbang sebagian besar dari potensi penghapusan nilai pasar lebih dari US$ 660 miliar pada indeks Nasdaq-100 jika pelemahan saat ini bertahan.

“Dampak positif AI harus tetap bertahan selama bertahun-tahun ke depan. Jika tidak, investor Alphabet dan perusahaan penyedia layanan komputasi awan berskala besar lainnya pada akhirnya akan menilai ulang valuasi saham mereka ketika laba bersih mulai memudar,” kata Anders Bylund, analis teknologi di The Motley Fool.

Sebaliknya, saham Microsoft naik 1,8% setelah perusahaan mencatat arus kas yang jauh melampaui perkiraan dan memproyeksikan pertumbuhan bisnis komputasi awan yang kuat. Namun, kenaikan saham tersebut menyusut setelah sejumlah laporan media menyebutkan bahwa Microsoft berencana menghimpun dana sedikitnya US$ 25 miliar melalui penerbitan obligasi berperingkat layak investasi pertamanya di AS dalam lebih dari sembilan tahun.

Antusiasme investor terhadap perdagangan saham terkait AI mulai mereda bulan ini akibat kekhawatiran bahwa belanja yang dibiayai utang akan semakin sulit dibenarkan ketika biaya pinjaman meningkat. Pengungkapan Anthropic pada Senin mengenai pengajuan penawaran umum perdana saham (IPO) di AS sempat memberikan kelegaan, tetapi kekhawatiran tersebut kembali muncul.

Baca Juga: Harga Emas Dunia dan Antam Ikut Melemah Saat IHSG Anjlok, Cek Pemicunya

Sementara itu, data yang dirilis pada Kamis menunjukkan Indeks Harga Pengeluaran Konsumsi Pribadi (PCE) meningkat sesuai dengan perkiraan para ekonom yang disurvei Reuters. Data terpisah menunjukkan ekonomi AS tumbuh lebih cepat dari perkiraan pada kuartal III.

“Kombinasi pertumbuhan ekonomi yang masih sangat kuat dan tangguh, bersama dengan inflasi dan harga-harga yang semakin menjauh dari target The Fed, memperkuat perkiraan bahwa keputusan pada Oktober merupakan penundaan sementara, bukan penghentian kenaikan suku bunga oleh The Fed,” kata Angelo Kourkafas, ahli strategi investasi global senior di Edward Jones.

Pada Rabu, The Fed mempertahankan suku bunga sesuai perkiraan. Bank sentral AS itu memilih menunda perubahan biaya pinjaman hingga setelah pemilihan paruh waktu AS pekan depan.

#233. Strategi Universal BPR Jaga Momentum Pertumbuhan

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Dina Mirayanti Hutauruk | Editor: Dina Hutauruk

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. PT BPR Universal atau Universal BPR menggelar Universal Bonanza 2026 untuk memperkuat hubungan dengan nasabah sekaligus meningkatkan literasi keuangan masyarakat. Kegiatan ini juga menjadi sarana mendorong kebiasaan menabung dan pengelolaan keuangan yang lebih terencana.

Komisaris Utama Universal BPR Kaman Siboro mengatakan, kebiasaan menabung penting untuk membangun ketahanan finansial, baik dalam menghadapi kebutuhan mendesak maupun merencanakan kebutuhan masa depan. Acara ini menghadirkan ekonom dan akademisi Rhenald Kasali yang membahas adaptasi terhadap perubahan ekonomi, perkembangan teknologi, serta dampak kecerdasan buatan (AI) terhadap masyarakat dan dunia usaha. Ia menekankan pentingnya meningkatkan produktivitas, mengembangkan keterampilan, dan mempersiapkan ketahanan finansial.

“Banyak orang berpikir bahwa uang hanya untuk investasi, padahal kita juga harus mempersiapkan diri menghadapi berbagai risiko. Orang yang bijak bukan hanya pandai melipatgandakan pendapatan, tetapi juga mampu mempertahankan kekayaan agar keluarga memiliki ketenangan di masa depan,” ujar Rhenald.

Adi mengapresiasi inisiatif Universal BPR dalam memberikan nilai tambah kepada nasabah melalui program tersebut. Menurut dia, Universal Bonanza merupakan salah satu upaya bank dalam meningkatkan pelayanan kepada nasabah.

Hadiah yang diundi meliputi satu unit Honda Brio, tiga unit Honda Vario, lima unit Smart TV LG 50 inci, tiga unit iPhone 17, serta voucher tabungan. Voucher senilai Rp 2 juta diberikan kepada 28 nasabah, Rp 5 juta kepada tujuh nasabah, dan Rp 10 juta kepada tiga nasabah. Hadiah utama berupa Honda Brio dimenangkan oleh nasabah Kantor Cabang Universal BPR BSD.

Hingga September 2026, Universal BPR mencatatkan total aset sekitar Rp 2,4 triliun dan dana pihak ketiga (DPK) sekitar Rp 1,9 triliun. Pada periode yang sama, perseroan membukukan laba bersih Rp 18,3 miliar dengan rasio kredit bermasalah atau non-performing loan (NPL) net sebesar 1,89%.

Dari sisi penyaluran kredit, lebih dari 86% portofolio kredit perseroan dialokasikan untuk kegiatan produktif, termasuk modal kerja dan pengembangan usaha. Universal BPR menyatakan akan terus menjaga keseimbangan antara pertumbuhan bisnis, kualitas aset, dan penerapan prinsip kehati-hatian.

Ke depan, perseroan berencana mengembangkan produk dan layanan digital melalui Universal Mobile Plus. Layanan ini direncanakan mencakup pembayaran menggunakan QRIS, pembukaan rekening secara daring, serta pembukaan dan pengelolaan deposito melalui kanal digital.

Di bidang pembiayaan, Universal BPR juga menyiapkan pengembangan Kredit Usaha Inklusif (KUI) untuk memperluas akses pendanaan bagi masyarakat dan pelaku usaha. Inisiatif ini melengkapi penguatan kerja sama antar-BPR dalam mendukung pembiayaan sektor produktif.

#241. Perkuatan Daya Saing, OJK Luncurkan Peta Jalan Keuangan Berkelanjutan PPDP 2026-2030

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Ade Priyatin | Editor: Ignatia Maria Sri Sayekti

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Otoritas Jasa Keuangan (OJK) senantiasa memperkuat ketahanan dan daya saing industri perasuransian, penjaminan, dan dana pensiun (PPDP) untuk menghadapi risiko perubahan iklim dan dinamika ekonomi global.

Sejalan dengan itu, OJK meluncurkan Peta Jalan Pengembangan Keuangan Berkelanjutan Sektor PPDP 2026-2030. Peta Jalan ini akan menjadi arah strategis bagi regulator dan industri dalam meningkatkan pengelolaan risiko berkelanjutan sekaligus memperkuat kontribusi PPDP terhadap pertumbuhan ekonomi nasional.

Kepala Eksekutif Pengawasan Perasuransian, Penjaminan, dan Dana Pensiun OJK, Ogi Prastomiyono mengatakan sektor PPDP memiliki peran strategis untuk mendukung transisi menuju ekonomi berkelanjutan.

"Kontribusi PPDP dalam transisi ekonomi bukan hanya membiayai transisi, tetapi juga melindungi proses transisi tersebut. Inilah posisi strategis PPDP dalam agenda keberlanjutan: investasi dan perlindungan," ujarnya dalam keterangan resmi, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Adapun peran strategis PPDP adalah sebagai investor institusional yang mengelola dana jangka menengah dan panjang, maupun sebagai penyerap risiko yang memberikan perlindungan pada masyarakat dan dunia usaha.

Baca Juga: OJK Sebut Sektor PPDP Punya Posisi Strategis Dalam Transisi Ekonomi

Selain itu, keberlanjutan yang dimaksud adalah berkaitan dengan tanggung jawab terhadap lingkungan serta memperkuat ketahanan ekonomi dan menjaga stabilitas sistem keuangan.

Lebih lanjut, prinsip keuangan berkelanjutan juga harus tetap memperhatikan prinsip kehati-hatian, termasuk kesesuaian profil risiko dan imbal hasil investasi, karakteristik kewajiban, likuiditas, dan perlindungan kepentingan pemegang polis dan peserta.

Adapun peta perjalanan tersebut bertumpu pada empat pilar strategis, yaitu penguatan pengaturan dan pembangunan keuangan berkelanjutan untuk mendukung penerapan prinsip keberlanjutan sesuai karakteristik sektor PPDP. Kemudian, peningkatan kapasitas sumber daya manusia industri PPDP guna memperkuat kompetensi dan kesiapan industri.

Di sisi lain, penguatan kolaborasi dan koordinasi lintas sektor. Hal ini dilakukan untuk meningkatkan sinergi antara regulator, industri, dan pemangku kepentingan.

Selanjutnya adalah penguatan pengawasan atas penerapan keuangan berkelanjutan untuk memastikan implementasi pertumbuhan dan penguatan pengawasan pada 2030.

Baca Juga: OJK Proyeksikan Sektor PPDP Tumbuh Lebih Moderat hingga Akhir Tahun 2026

Untuk melaksanakan peta jalan tersebut, OJK menerapkan tiga fase utama yaitu penguatan fondasi pada 2026-2027, peningkatan kolaborasi pada 2028-2029, dan percepatan pertumbuhan dan penguatan pengawasan pada 2030.

Melalui pertahapan ini, akan memberikan ruang bagi industri untuk memperkuat kesiapan internal dan mengintegrasikan prinsip keberlanjutan dalam strategi bisnis, pengelolaan investasi, manajemen risiko, dan pengembangan produk dan layanan secara proporsional sesuai karakter masing-masing industri.

Harapannya, industri PPDP akan makin tangguh dalam menghadapi berbagai risiko dan mampu memanfaatkan peluang ekonomi berkelanjutan sekaligus bisa memberikan kontribusi yang lebih besar terhadap pertumbuhan ekonomi nasional.

#105. Employee engagement in U.S. hits lowest point in 15 years

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Worker engagement in the U.S. has dropped below 30%, to its lowest level since 2011, Gallup reports.

Engagement dropped two points from last quarter, equivalent to roughly 3.2 million fewer engaged workers. The share of “actively disengaged” workers rose from 17% to 18%.

The largest decline was among fully remote workers, whose engagement fell five points. The drop was sharpest among remote and hybrid employees at organizations that have implemented artificial intelligence. Meanwhile, engagement among on-site workers fell only about one point.

The results “raise questions about whether communication and manager support are keeping pace with AI integration,” the report states, noting that the percentage of employees who strongly agreed they knew what was expected of them at work fell three points, to 46%. 

The percentage who strongly agreed that their supervisor or someone at work cares about them as a person also fell three points, from 40% to 37%.

While Gallup’s July survey found engagement to be considerably higher at organizations that have adopted AI, this advantage narrowed considerably in the latest edition, at least for remote and hybrid workers.

In Q2 2026, employee engagement at AI-integrated organizations was noticeably higher, outperforming that of industry peers by eight points among hybrid workers and five points among fully remote workers. By Q3, however, these advantages shrank to just two points.

The report suggests a lack of manager support may be to blame, noting that “active manager support” had the strongest correlation with engagement among the various “AI-related factors” measured in the survey.  

This may be especially true for hybrid and remote employees, “who may have fewer opportunities for informal, in-person communication.”

Gallup began tracking employee engagement in 2000, finding that only 26% of U.S. workers were engaged. Engagement peaked at 36% in 2020 before dropping to 31% in 2024 and 29% this quarter.

Gallup measures engagement using a composite of 12 characteristics, among them “role clarity,” “resource availability,” and “recognition for good work.”

#113. Inside giant homebuilder D.R. Horton’s bet on robotic homebuilding

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Want more housing market stories from Lance Lambert’s ResiClub in your inbox? Subscribe to the ResiClub newsletter.

D.R. Horton, the nation’s largest homebuilder by volume, has signed a multiyear commercial agreement with Promise Robotics to use the startup’s robotic factory capacity on more than 1,250 homes. The deal, announced October 13, makes D.R. Horton the first major U.S. builder to sign on to Promise Robotics’s factory-as-a-service model.

On a typical jobsite, framing crews build walls by hand, cutting studs, nailing them together, and standing them up on the foundation. That labor-intensive, weather-dependent work is where builders feel labor shortages most. Promise Robotics moves much of the framing into a factory, where robots assemble wall panels from the builder’s existing plans. The panels are then trucked to the site and set in place, which is far faster than framing from scratch.

D.R. Horton closed 84,863 homes in fiscal 2025, so the around 1,250 homes planned with Promise Robotics amount to about 1.5% of a single year’s volume. To start, this looks more like a measured test than a wholesale shift in how D.R. Horton builds.

In metro Phoenix, where Promise Robotics tells ResiClub all the 1,250 homes will be built and located, it’s a different story: 1,250 homes is more than half a year of D.R. Horton’s recent annual combined closings across Arizona’s Maricopa, Pinal, and Pima counties. The structure of the deal matters, too. D.R. Horton isn’t buying robots or building a factory. It’s renting automated production capacity from a third party that owns the plants, the software, and the robotics—and that says it can now replicate the model market by market, starting with a factory in Gilbert, Arizona.

The deal

Under the agreement, D.R. Horton will tap the Promise Robotics production platform to help build 1,250-plus homes. The companies did not disclose financial terms, a delivery timeline, or which Phoenix communities the homes will go to.

Brad Conlon, D.R. Horton’s senior vice president of strategic growth and innovation, framed the deal around three operating goals: faster cycle times, more production capacity, and more consistency and predictability. He called the Promise Robotics platform a new capability for pursuing those goals.

How Factory-as-a-Service works

The pitch from Promise Robotics is that builders shouldn’t have to own a factory to get factory-style production. Under its factory-as-a-service model, Promise Robotics owns and runs the plants, robotics, and software, and builders buy output.

The workflow runs in four steps:

  1. Design translation: The Promise Robotics AI ingests a builder’s existing home plans, so the builder doesn’t have to redesign product for the factory.
  2. Production planning: The software generates production plans and machine instructions for the robots.
  3. Robotic manufacturing: Off-the-shelf industrial robots produce building components on the factory line.
  4. Site installation: Promise Robotics coordinates delivery and installation of those components on the jobsite.

The company says the integration is the differentiator: Rather than automating one task, such as nailing or cutting, it ties design, factory, and jobsite into a single production system. Earlier coverage of the company describes its output as framing components such as wall panels, though the D.R. Horton announcement doesn’t specify which components are in scope.

That asset-light structure matters for builders. Off-site construction has a long history of builders and startups sinking capital into factories that then sat underutilized when local demand cooled. Promise Robotics CEO Ramtin Attar made the same point back in 2023, when the startup raised a $15 million Series A: Builders are reluctant to invest in costly factories and then spend years on trial and error.

From Edmonton to Calgary to Phoenix

Speaking to ResiClub, Attar laid out how Promise Robotics sees its path from Canadian pilot to U.S. scale:

“Promise is building the production infrastructure homebuilding scales on. Builders are increasingly looking to secure industrial production capacity through multiyear commitments because they need to deliver more homes with faster, more predictable cycle times. But building and operationalizing that capacity takes significant capital, time, and expertise. Our platform removes those barriers: It turns a builder’s designs into production instructions, orchestrates production, and gets better with every build. We proved the model in Edmonton and scaled it in Calgary, where we’ve been in continuous production since September 2025. Now we’re replicating that model in Phoenix, creating the template for every market that follows.”

The Canadian track record is still short. Promise Robotics, founded by Attar, a former Autodesk R&D executive, and Canadian homebuilder Reza Nasseri, ran its first production line in Edmonton, Alberta. It then opened a 60,000-square-foot plant in an existing Calgary warehouse, which it has called a flagship hub and a model to replicate in U.S. markets. The Calgary plant was backed in part by a federal contribution from Prairies Economic Development Canada.

Calgary’s anchor customer is the Alberta division of Mattamy Homes, which signed its own multiyear agreement with Promise Robotics to supply single-family, semidetached, and multifamily homes in two master-planned communities. The D.R. Horton deal follows the same template: a multiyear capacity commitment from a large builder, tied to a Promise-run plant.

Phoenix is the test of whether that template travels to the United States. It’s Promise Robotics’s first U.S. production market, a different labor pool, supply chain, and building code environment, and a market where the company will need to win business from builders who have their own trade bases and framing crews.

Why a builder would rent capacity instead of building it

For a production builder, the appeal is less about robots than about cycle time and cost per home. Faster, more predictable builds mean inventory turns faster and less capital sits tied up in homes under construction.

Those levers matter more when margins are under pressure. Among the 15 largest homebuilders tracked by ResiClub, 13 are seeing year-over-year gross margin compression. In that environment, any tool that shaves weeks or labor hours off a build is worth testing.

The factory-as-a-service structure also limits the downside. D.R. Horton takes no factory on its balance sheet and carries no fixed overhead if local demand softens. If the Promise Robotics plants don’t deliver on schedule or on cost, the builder can fall back on its existing trade partners. Promise Robotics carries the capital and operating risk; D.R. Horton carries execution risk on 1,250 homes.

Attar’s comment points to a broader shift he says Promise Robotics is seeing: builders locking in industrial production capacity through multiyear commitments. Two such deals, Mattamy in Alberta and now D.R. Horton, are early evidence for that claim, but not yet a trend.

#258. Pasokan Nikel Berlebih, Empat Raksasa China Berencana Pangkas Produksi di Indonesia

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Penulis: Chelsea Anastasia, Chelsea Anastasia | Editor: Avanty Nurdiana

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Asosiasi Penambang Nikel Indonesia (APNI) angkat bicara mengenai rencana empat perusahaan besar asal China memangkas produksinya di Indonesia.

Diberitakan sebelumnya, sebanyak empat perusahaan nikel asal China berencana mengurangi hingga 30% produksinya di Indonesia, antara lain Tsingsham Holding Group, GEM, Lygend Resources & Technology dan Zheijiang Huayou Cobalt.

Sekretaris Umum APNI Meidy Katrin Lengkey menjelaskan, rencana pengurangan produksi tak terlepas dari kondisi kelebihan pasokan yang menekan permintaan pasar. Menurutnya, penyesuaian produksi diperlukan ketika pasokan tidak sebanding dengan permintaan.

Baca Juga: Menakar Peluang dan Tantangan Penguatan Pasar Gas Bumi Domestik

"Domestik saat ini ada pengurangan karena lagi kondisi over (pasokan). Pengurangan itu bisa dipengaruhi oleh produksi, kemudian oleh beberapa daerah market itu mengurangi permintaan juga," kata Meidy saat ditemui di Kantor Direktorat Jenderal Ketenagalistrikan Kementerian Energi dan Sumber Daya Mineral (ESDM), Jakarta, Kamis (29/10).

Ia menambahkan, tekanan juga terlihat dari tingginya persediaan nikel di bursa komoditas internasional, termasuk Shanghai Futures Exchange dan London Metal Exchange (LME). Di sisi lain, permintaan dari pasar akhir pun tengah melemah.

"Shanghai Futures Exchange lagi overstock, LME lagi overstock. Lalu market-nya sendiri, end market-nya, itu lagi berkurang," ujarnya.

Selain kondisi pasar, kenaikan biaya produksi turut menjadi pertimbangan perusahaan untuk mengurangi produksi. Meidy menyebut, biaya produksi saat ini lebih tinggi dibandingkan harga pasar.

"Pengurangan itu juga dipengaruhi oleh cost produksi yang saat ini sedang lebih tinggi dibandingkan harga market," jelasnya.

Meski demikian, APNI belum memperoleh informasi dtail mengenai besaran pengurangan produksi yang akan dilakukan oleh masing-masing perusahaan. Rencana tersebut masih dalam proses. "Belum, masih (on going)," imbuh Meidy.

Menurutnya, penyesuaian produksi diperlukan untuk menjaga keseimbangan pasokan dan permintaan. Jika produksi tetap tinggi ketika permintaan melemah, perusahaan berisiko menghadapi kesulitan menjual hasil produksinya.

"Nanti kalau produksi lalu nggak ada yang beli, gimana. Tentu (alasan pemangkasan produksi) ada faktor internal, kedua karena global, ketiga karena cost produksi," katanya.

Baca Juga: Kecerdasan Buatan & Teknologi Digital Dorong Inovasi di Sektor Pendidikan dan Budaya

Di sisi lain, pengurangan produksi smelter berpotensi menekan penyerapan bijih nikel dari penambang domestik. Ia mengungkap indikasi penurunan serapan tersebut mulai terlihat belakangan ini.

Rata-rata penyerapan bijih nikel yang sebelumnya mencapai sekitar 25 juta ton per bulan pada tahun ini, katanya, turun menjadi sekitar 20 juta ton per bulan dalam dua bulan terakhir.

"Untuk dua bulan ini agak kelihatan ada penurunan, karena rata-rata tahun ini kan 25 juta (ton) per bulan. Dua bulan terakhir ini mungkin ada pengurangan, mungkin menjadi 20 jutaan (ton)," imbuh Meidy.
