#116. How a co-owner of the New York Giants is using AI to build a women's sports powerhouse with Gotham FC

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

New York Giants and Gotham FC co-owner Carolyn Tisch Blodgett is betting on AI to transform women's sports into a major moneymaker.

"If AI can make you more efficient, that totally changes where women's sports can go," Tisch Blodgett said at Business Insider's The Long Play event on Thursday afternoon.

Tisch Blodgett, who's the lead owner of Gotham FC in the National Women's Soccer League, sees AI as a way to reduce the costs of running a sports business without cutting corners.

Follow along with our coverage of Business Insider's The Long Play

"There's a lot of parts of a sports team that are just expensive to operate. It's expensive to operate a stadium, it's expensive to operate a ticketing team, expensive to operate a marketing team," Tisch Blodgett said.

Those struggles are heightened for women's sports, which historically haven't gotten the same level of investment as men's sports.

"The revenue was not high enough to really justify building out an entire business team, and the business model just didn't really work," Tisch Blodgett said of women's sports.

Tisch Blodgett made a name for herself by starting sports-focused venture capital firm Next 3, which has invested in Gotham FC, the Los Angeles Golf Club in golf league TGL, Premier Lacrosse League, and lottery ticket site Jackpot.com.

The Gotham FC owner has had a front-row seat to running sports since she was a kid: Her family bought half of the NFL's New York Giants in 1991. The Tisch family runs Loews Corporation, a hotel and insurance conglomerate worth about $22.9 billion.

Under the Tisch's ownership, the Giants have won two Super Bowls. Tisch Blodgett has already matched that title count in the three years she's owned Gotham FC — though building a winner hasn't been easy.

Add BI in Google so our reporting is easier to find when you’re searching for what matters.

Tisch Blodgett felt firsthand the challenges that women's sports teams face after taking over Gotham FC in 2023. The New York-based team had talent, as it went on to bring home its first title months later.

But success on the pitch hadn't translated to a winning business. Gotham FC didn't have its own business office when Tisch Blodgett took over, and players shared a practice field with a club team of 16-year-old boys.

While Gotham FC had "a great product on the field," Tisch Blodgett said, "attendance was really low" due to a lack of awareness.

"It was clear that the people that came really liked it, but it was very small," Tisch Blodgett said of Gotham FC's fanbase in 2023.

Tisch Blodgett has since flipped the script. This July, Gotham FC sold out Citi Field in Queens, drawing over 42,000 fans in what she said was the biggest women's sports event ever in New York City.

Gotham FC announced in July that it would call Queens home starting in 2028 — a major upgrade from its current stadium in Harrison, New Jersey, which is difficult to reach from New York City by public transit.

One key to Gotham FC's success has been gathering feedback. Tisch Blodgett, who led marketing for fitness giant Peloton before starting Next 3, said learning from fans "makes the whole business smarter" and has been crucial to the soccer club's growth.

"Fan feedback is incredibly important to me — it's how we run our organization," Tisch Blodgett said. "After every game, we send out a survey to every person that attended, essentially saying, 'Did you like it? Would you recommend it to a friend?'"

Gotham FC's Net Promoter Score — which measures how likely customers are to recommend the brand to a friend — rivals Apple, Tisch Blodgett said.

"It makes every person in the organization understand what we're building towards in a much more efficient way," Tisch Blodgett said.

#65. Amazon AWS posts 39% revenue growth as cloud rivals also accelerate

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Amazon's cloud-computing unit reported more rapid third-quarter growth than analysts had projected.

Revenue came in at $45.98 billion, up 39% year over year and above the $45.29 billion consensus among analysts polled by StreetAccount, according to a statement. Growth in the second quarter came in at about 37%.

"Our core compute and storage revenue grew at their fastest rates in several years," Amazon CEO Andy Jassy said on a conference call with analysts. The AWS growth rate was the highest since the fourth quarter of 2021.

Amazon remains the top cloud by revenue, but growth has also sped up at rivals Alphabet and Microsoft. On Wednesday Alphabet said the Google Cloud business, which contains infrastructure and Google Workspace subscriptions, more than doubled in the third quarter.

All the major clouds have been racing to build data centers that can accommodate high artificial intelligence demand. During the quarter, AWS announced plans to expand its portfolio of Nvidia graphics processing units that can handle AI workloads.

AWS also acquired data analysis startup DuckLabs and signed deals with Meta and OpenAI, according to the statement.

The AWS segment generated $17.77 billion in third-quarter operating income, up 55% and more than the $16.97 billion StreetAccount consensus.

The unit now accounts for almost 23% of Amazon's revenue, a record.

On Wednesday, Microsoft published its first earnings report that contained revenue from its Azure cloud in dollars. Azure is 72% the size of AWS, up from 58% two years ago.

AWS came before Azure and the Google Cloud Platform, with its first services appearing in 2006.

"With our continued pace of innovation, new core and AI functionality, enterprises wanting to run their core and AI workloads in AWS, and the amount of capacity that we're adding each year, we believe that AWS can grow north of 40% in each of 2027 and in 2028, and be a trillion-dollar revenue business in the next five to 10 years," Jassy said.

Google has been pumping up its cloud revenue by making its tensor processing units for AI available outside its own facilities. Now Amazon will adopt the strategy with its Trainium chips for training models, starting in late 2027.

"With Trainium demand so strong, customers continue encouraging us to make it available outside of AWS. We intend to do so late in 2027," Jassy said.

#123. Sam Altman has ‘chronic disappointment’ with MBA students. Here’s his advice for young job seekers

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Sam Altman knows how to play the startup game. Beyond co-founding two companies—the social network Loopt, at just 20 years old, and OpenAI, which now stands as one of the highest valued unicorns of all time—he’s helped launch dozens of other startups as the former president of Y Combinator.

That background has led Altman to his view of the current startup landscape: “chronic disappointment.”

In an interview with Stanford’s Graduate School of Business, Altman sounded off on how he feels prospective founders are falling behind in the era of artificial intelligence.

“My chronic disappointment with MBA students is that they always want to go do the thing that was cool five years ago rather than try something new,” he said. “I think this is a great time to go do the new thing.”

What would succumbing to the cool factor look like? Altman says now would be a bad time to “start another copy of OpenAI,” but that “there are many companies that can be built on top of technology that OpenAI has put out into the world that will be much, I hope, bigger and more important than OpenAI.”

Dodging the doomerism

Though Altman has admitted that AI could lead to the apocalypse, he’s quick to shut down a different kind of doomsday: that AI will decimate the job market. 

“I don’t think there’s going to be, like, a job apocalypse in the way that many people talk about,” Altman said. As with finding success in starting a new tech company, he simply encourages job seekers to adapt, rather than stay stuck in the past.

“It is totally true that huge numbers of tasks that are done today by people will be done better, cheaper, faster, completely differently by AI. And it’s also true that wide swaths of current jobs—the way they’re done today, at least—will be entirely done by AI,” Altman continued. 

But that degree of change in any given position’s day-to-day operations is an opportunity, he argues, not a setback.

“If you are heading into the world right now to go work on something, I think you want to be starting your career at a time of the highest degree of change, and you want the most open playing field,” he said. “I think people with high potential and drive and ideas and sort of a new way of looking at things and new skills do relatively the best when the ground is shaking the most.”

The school of startups

Altman may have hot takes on MBAs, but he never received a degree himself, having dropped out of Stanford University after two years of studying computer science in order to found Loopt. But that didn’t stop him from getting what he considers to be an MBA’s worth of education.

“I was sad to drop out of school because I loved school, and I was also worried I just was not going to keep learning stuff,” Altman said. “And one of the coolest things for me about doing a startup is, it was—and still is—the highest rate of learning environment I had ever been in.”

Addressing the room of Stanford students, Altman passed on a piece of wisdom he received as a young startup founder: “You will get an entire MBA in one month of doing a startup.”

“I don’t have an MBA, so I can’t say whether or not that’s true. But I did learn a lot very quickly about business and product and customers and sales and all of this other stuff that has been very important to the rest of my career, that I think I just wouldn’t have been able to learn in school,” he added.

#259. Data Historis Klaim Mobil Listrik Jadi Tantangan,Ini Cara Great Eastern Menyiasatinya

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Ferry Saputra | Editor: Ignatia Maria Sri Sayekti

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Meningkatnya penjualan mobil listrik tak serta-merta membuat perusahaan asuransi gencar memberikan perlindungan untuk segmen tersebut. Adapun salah satu pertimbangannya karena data historis klaim mobil listrik yang terbilang masih minim.

Asal tahu saja, berdasarkan data Gabungan Industri Kendaraan Bermotor Indonesia (Gaikindo), penjualan wholesales mobil listrik mencapai 117.218 unit sepanjang Januari 2026 hingga September 2026. Jumlah tersebut meningkat 112,25%, dibandingkan periode yang sama tahun lalu sebanyak 55.225 unit.

PT Great Eastern General Insurance Indonesia (GEGI) memahami bahwa mobil listrik merupakan segmen yang relatif baru, sehingga historis data klaimnya belum seperti mobil konvensional. Namun, Marketing Director Great Eastern General Insurance Indonesia Linggawati Tok menerangkan kondisi itu tidak serta-merta membuat perusahaan asuransi menerapkan tarif yang jauh lebih tinggi atau mengurangi manfaat perlindungan untuk segmen mobil listrik.

Linggawati menyampaikan perusahaan asuransi umumnya menyiasati tantangan tersebut melalui pendekatan underwriting yang lain, seperti memanfaatkan data pengalaman pasar global, serta melakukan pemantauan pengalaman klaim secara berkala.

Baca Juga: Great Eastern General Sebut Program PLTS 100 GWp Bisa Menjadi Peluang Besar

"Adapun penyesuaian premi, deductible, maupun syarat pertanggungan akan ditentukan berdasarkan profil risiko masing-masing kendaraan dan pengalaman klaim yang berkembang," ungkapnya kepada Kontan, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Ke depannya, Linggawati meyakini akurasi penetapan tarif juga akan makin baik, seiring bertambahnya populasi mobil listrik dan data yang tersedia.

Lebih lanjut, Linggawati mengatakan saat ini, mobil listrik belum dapat dikategorikan sebagai segmen berisiko tinggi secara umum, walaupun sering terjadi kecelakaan yang cukup fatal disebabkan kurang disiplin pengemudi. Selain itu, terdapat juga beberapa karakteristik risiko yang berbeda dibandingkan kendaraan berbahan bakar bensin.

Linggawati mengungkapkan salah satu risiko yang menjadi perhatian utama terkait mobil listrik adalah nilai komponen baterai yang relatif tinggi, kebutuhan perbaikan yang memerlukan teknisi khusus, serta ketersediaan suku cadang tertentu. Meski demikian, kendaraan listrik juga dilengkapi berbagai teknologi keselamatan modern yang berpotensi mengurangi frekuensi kecelakaan.

Baca Juga: Ini Strategi Great Eastern General Untuk Mendorong Kinerja Asuransi Perjalanan

"Oleh karena itu, fokus perusahaan asuransi bukan semata-mata pada tingkat risiko yang lebih tinggi atau lebih rendah, melainkan pada pemahaman yang lebih baik terhadap profil risiko yang berbeda," tuturnya.

Seiring berkembangnya ekosistem kendaraan listrik di Indonesia, Linggawati mengatakan risiko-risiko tersebut diperkirakan akan makin terkendali.

Terkait kinerja, Linggawati mengatakan permintaan perlindungan asuransi untuk kendaraan listrik juga menunjukkan tren yang positif, sejalan dengan penjualan kendaraan listrik di Indonesia. Dia bilang jumlah polis kendaraan listrik yang diasuransikan di Great Eastern General naik sebesar 126% Year on Year (YoY) per Agustus 2026.

#268. Init-6 Investasi di Netra Runtime, Startup AI RI yang Klaim Empat Kali Lebih Cepat

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Secillia Hafifah | Editor: Avanty Nurdiana

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Perusahaan modal ventura Init-6 memperkenalkan Netra Runtime sebagai portofolio investasi terbarunya. Investasi ini sejalan dengan meningkatnya kebutuhan perusahaan terhadap infrastruktur kecerdasan buatan (AI) yang lebih cepat dan efisien untuk menekan biaya operasional.

Init-6 merupakan perusahaan modal ventura yang berfokus pada perusahaan tahap awal (early stage company) dan didirikan oleh dua pendiri Bukalapak, Achmad Zaky dan Nugroho Herucahyono.

Sementara itu, Netra Runtime merupakan perusahaan infrastruktur inferensi AI asal Indonesia yang membantu perusahaan menjalankan model AI melalui GPU milik sendiri maupun layanan cloud.

Baca Juga: Mencermati Prospek Saham BBNI Usai Mencetak Kinerja Solid di Kuartal III

Founding Partner Init-6 Nugroho Herucahyono mengatakan, perkembangan AI membuat efisiensi pemrosesan menjadi semakin penting.

AI kini tidak hanya digunakan untuk menjawab pertanyaan, tetapi juga menulis kode, membaca dokumen dalam jumlah besar, hingga menjalankan tugas secara terus-menerus sebagai agen.

“Di dunia seperti ini kecepatan itu bukan cuma masalah kenyamanan, tapi juga masalah biaya,” ujar Nugroho dalam media briefing di University Club Jakarta, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Menurut Nugroho, perusahaan dapat menjalankan model AI terbuka (open-weight model) di server sendiri untuk mengendalikan infrastruktur dan biaya.

Namun, menjalankan model tersebut dengan performa tinggi masih menjadi tantangan karena mesin inferensi open source umumnya tertinggal dari teknologi yang dikembangkan perusahaan AI besar.

Netra hadir untuk menjawab kebutuhan tersebut. Berdasarkan hasil benchmark yang disampaikan perusahaan, Netra mampu menghasilkan empat kali lebih banyak token per detik dibandingkan mesin inferensi open source terkemuka dengan GPU yang sama.

“Gampangnya begini, GPU itu kayak dapur, inference engine itu kayak kokinya. Jadi dengan dapur yang sama, Netra itu bisa menghasilkan 4 kali lebih banyak hidangan dibandingkan inference engine open source yang lain,” kata Nugroho.

Efisiensi tersebut berpotensi memangkas waktu pemrosesan dari satu jam menjadi 15 menit. Bagi perusahaan yang memiliki GPU, peningkatan pemanfaatan perangkat juga dapat mempercepat pengembalian investasi.

Netra menawarkan dua skema layanan, yakni penyebaran privat (private deployment) menggunakan infrastruktur GPU milik pelanggan dan layanan Netra Cloud melalui API terkelola dengan skema pembayaran berdasarkan penggunaan token. Teknologinya mendukung GPU AMD dan NVIDIA, sementara layanan API kompatibel dengan OpenAI.

Baca Juga: Init-6 Venture Telah Biayai Empat Perusahaan Startup hingga Saat Ini, Ini Bisnisnya

“Pertanyaan bagi perusahaan bukan lagi sekadar model mana yang paling pintar dan paling cepat, tetapi berapa biaya untuk menghasilkan suatu pekerjaan. Netra menjawab kebutuhan ini dengan menjadi infrastruktur inferensi berkinerja tinggi untuk GPU AMD dan NVIDIA,” ujar Nugroho.

Bagi Init-6, investasi ini juga menjadi bagian dari upaya mendorong perusahaan teknologi Indonesia agar tidak hanya menggunakan model AI dan aplikasi berbasis API dari luar negeri, tetapi turut membangun infrastruktur yang menentukan performa dan biaya pemrosesan AI.

Dalam jangka pendek, Init-6 berharap Netra dapat membuktikan performanya untuk mendukung pelanggan enterprise. Dalam jangka panjang, Init-6 menargetkan Netra menjadi infrastruktur AI andalan perusahaan di Indonesia dan pasar global.

“Kami ingin Netra ini menjadi bukti bahwa anak muda Indonesia bisa menghasilkan teknologi AI di seluruh dunia,” kata Nugroho.

#119. Reddit shares sink on weak sales guidance and plans login requirement test as Google search traffic shrinks

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reddit plans to require all users to create official accounts to access the social media platform, marking a major departure for the site known as "the front page of the internet."

The company revealed the change Thursday as part of its third-quarter earnings, in which it beat on the top and bottom lines, but provided weak fourth-quarter sales guidance. The company's shares dropped over 15% after its numbers were released.

Here's how the company did compared with LSEG estimates:

Reddit CEO Steve Huffman told CNBC in an interview that the company's shrinking search-referral traffic from Google went "down meaningfully in this quarter" due to the "shift to AI mode and AI overviews." The traffic decline played a big role in Reddit's planned efforts to require people to become logged-in users.

A previous Google search update designed to surface "authentic" content from internet forums helped Reddit grow its user base over the past few years, but the search giant's continued push on AI overviews and related chatting features has negatively impacted referral traffic.

"I think fundamentally that relationship between Google and the rest of the internet is evolving, and so for us, this means a continued focus on our core users that come to Reddit directly," Huffman said.

Previous Google algorithm changes would "kind of come and go," Huffman said, but the company's AI efforts represent "a fundamental shift in how the Google product works, from being a traffic driver to whatever's going on now."

The CEO didn't provide any specific timing on when Reddit plans for the platform registration change, saying only that implementing it through the site would involve a lot of complexity. The company will begin testing in the fourth quarter outside of the U.S.

He said that Reddit is still determining what parts of the service logged-out users will be able to see, and will iron out the details as part of its upcoming testing.

Reddit said that daily active uniques, or DAUq, rose 12% year-over-year to 130.3 million in the third quarter, trailing Wall Street estimates of 133.7 million. U.S.-specific DAUq dropped 2% year-over-year to 50.7 million, which was below analyst projections of 53.5 million.

Huffman said that "search referral traffic is down meaningfully in this quarter" and that the trend will continue in the fourth quarter.

He said that Reddit is continuing its effort to make its platform "a daily habit" for users. "So that's where our focus is: growing those app users and logged-in users," Huffman said.

Reddit, which derives the bulk of its overall sales from online advertising, is able to monetize logged-ln users better than logged-out users, because those registered users are more active and valuable to advertisers itching to deliver more targeted ads.

"The vast majority of our revenue comes from our app users and logged-in users are direct traffic, and both of those are up Q. over Q. and so that's where our focus is," Huffman said.

The company's third-quarter sales grew 53% year-over-year, marking an end to the eight-straight quarters of revenue growth eclipsing 60% that it reported in July as part of its second-quarter earnings.

Reddit said that fourth-quarter sales will come in a range of $1 billion to $1.03 billion, which was below Wall Street estimates of $1.05 billion.

"It's still a strong guide," Huffman said about its revenue guidance. "We are still growing at an industry leading rate, and that's despite the headwinds from search referrals."

Reddit's third-quarter average revenue per user, or ARPU, came in at $6.87, which was higher than the $6.50 that analysts were expecting.

The company's "other revenue" segment, which includes its data-licensing business, jumped 35% year-over-year to $48 million in the third quarter. Reddit's two biggest data-licensing partners are OpenAI and Google.

Huffman said that Reddit remains "in negotiations" with Google over its data-licensing agreement, and said that it is "only going to do a deal that makes sense for Reddit," if it chooses to continue the partnership.

"I think there's a lot of strategic considerations that goes into that," Huffman said.

Huffman called the required registration plan a "big change" for himself, considering he "grew up on the open Internet."

"Reddit is the last platform to provide basically unfettered logged-out access, and that's largely because of me, I've been holding that line," Huffman, a Reddit co-founder, said. "I think users will probably go through the same understanding that I have, which is that it's a sign of the times. It's an evolution of how these platforms work."

Huffman later expanded on the significance of AI's impact on Reddit's user growth in an earnings call with analysts, and explained that the company plans to continue spending on marketing efforts intended to offset search-referral declines. "This is a consequential time for Reddit, and this period does remind me of about 11 years ago when I came back to the company," Huffman told analysts, referring to when he rejoined Reddit in 2015 following it's then-chief Ellen Pao stepping down. "We had a lot of conversations then about what is Reddit and what is Reddit for, and what is our place in this ecosystem. And we've been having those conversations at Reddit recently." "Reddit's mission is to create community and belonging for everyone in the world, and while there's overlap between Reddit and other players in the ecosystem, whether we're talking about social media or AI search, fundamentally, Reddit is communities and conversations," Huffman said.

#18. Intel stock plunges 21% as chipmaker blames PC slowdown for weak guidance

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Intel shares plummeted more than 20% in extended trading on Thursday after the chipmaker issued a weak fourth-quarter forecast, citing continued softness in the global PC market.

Here's how the company did in the third quarter, compared with estimates from analysts polled by LSEG:

Revenue climbed 21% from $13.7 billion a year earlier, the company said in a press release.

However, revenue in Intel's client computing and physical AI group fell 5% from the same quarter a year ago to $8.1 billion, missing estimates, even as data center and AI revenue jumped 78% to $7.3 billion.

Global PC shipments declined 4% in the second quarter from a year earlier due to soaring costs for memory and other components, according to Counterpoint Research.

For the current quarter, Intel said it expects adjusted earnings per share of 25 cents on revenue between $15 billion and $16 billion. Analysts were expecting revenue of $17.1 billion and EPS of 43 cents, according to LSEG.

"We see the PC slowdown continuing at least through the first half of next year," Intel finance chief David Zinsner, said in the release. "We will continue making disciplined investments in capacity and working capital to increase supply and support future growth."

Intel's forecast reflects the effects of an explosion at a fab in Israel that killed two workers and seriously injured a third earlier this month. The incident will cost the company about $600 million in lost revenue over two quarters, Zinsner said on Thursday's earnings. CEO Lip-Bu Tan said the explosion, which he called an "unfortunate and tragic accident," caused an "extended shutdown" at the plant, impacting shipments of 10-nanometer chips.

Intel's stock has been on an upward tear, up about 150% so far this year prior to the earnings report, although it's down roughly 23% in October over growing concern over the size of the artificial intelligence buildout.

"We will remain disciplined as we invest, and we will not spend $1 of capital unless we have confidence in the health of our products and process technologies, and clear evidence of customer demand," Zinsner said on the call.

Nvidia, which has become the world's most valuable company thanks to its dominance in graphics processing units, or GPUs, is scheduled to report results in mid-November. Advanced Micro Devices, a maker of GPUs and central processing units, or CPUs, reports next week.

Known best for its industry-leading CPUs, Intel has been a beneficiary of the rise in agentic AI. While chatbots and large models rely heavily on graphics processors from Nvidia and AMD, agentic applications such as Meta's Muse and OpenAI's Dots lean on CPUs to help orchestrate tasks and data from a computer operating in the cloud.

"Despite near term headwinds affecting the broader PC industry, we continue to see robust demand for CPUs and more broadly, for all our computing platforms in the long term," Tan said in the press release.

Intel's foundry business, meanwhile, has struggled to find its footing.

Intel is the only U.S.-based manufacturer of advanced logic chips, and is now in high-volume production of its most advanced 18A chip out of its new fab in Arizona, providing an alternative to Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. Its next fab in Arizona is expected to begin operations by the end of 2028.

For now, Intel has no major external customers for its new chips.

"We're going to have a couple of customers ready to announce the next two quarters," Tan said on the call, adding that Intel is making "tremendous progress" on its coming 14A chips.

The Trump administration took a 10% stake in Intel last year after the company had fallen behind on its 7- and 10-nanometer technologies. Nvidia and Softbank also invested billions in Intel last year.

Tan has taken a more cautious approach to foundry spending than his predecessor, Pat Gelsinger, who launched an ambitious revival of the foundry business.

Last week, Intel said crews have installed the first structural truss at its huge Ohio fab that was originally expected to deliver chips in 2025.

In September, reports surfaced that the world's leading maker of high-bandwidth memory, SK Hynix may team up with Intel in Ohio to lease some foundry space to make memory chips at the new site. Neither company would confirm any plans.

"We continue to evaluate unique and highly differentiated opportunities to accelerate our Ohio build out, and I hope to share more on this in the near future" Tan said on the call.

Intel completed a $23 billion equity offering in August. Tech giants have spent trillions of dollars in recent years to meet insatiable demand for AI and the infrastructure needed to support it.

Tan said he's "disappointed that we were not able to meet strong customer demand in this quarter."

#128. Rivian third-quarter earnings beat estimates as software revenue, R2 deliveries rise

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Rivian Automotive beat Wall Street's third-quarter earnings expectations as it ramped up production of its new R2 entry-level SUV that the company has promised investors will lead it to profitability.

Here's how Rivian performed in the period ended Sept. 30, compared with average estimates compiled by LSEG:

Shares of Rivian were up roughly 7% during afterhours trading Thursday, after hitting a new 52-week low of $11.38 earlier in the day. The stock closed at $11.45 a share, down 1.9%

The electric vehicle maker also revised its 2026 guidance to mark expected improvements in its vehicle deliveries, pre-tax adjusted earnings and capital expenditures.

The new guidance calls for a pre-tax adjusted loss of between $1.75 billion and $1.85 billion — a $100 million improvement at the midpoint from its prior guidance — vehicle deliveries of between 67,000 and 70,000 units, up from at least 65,000; and capital expenditures of between $1.4 billion and $1.5 billion, $300 million less at the midpoint.

Rivian attributed the improvement in its pre-tax adjusted earnings guidance to better-than-expected revenue related to its sales of regulatory credits, an IEEPA tariff refund receivable in the third quarter and its expected increase in vehicle deliveries.

The R2 — the first deliveries of which arrived to customers in June — has become Rivian's best-selling model and was responsible for "much of the gain quarter-over-quarter," the company said.

Rivian delivered a record 19,248 units from July through September. The company does not disclose deliveries by vehicle.

The EV maker remains unprofitable overall but continued to narrow its losses during the quarter. The company reported a net loss attributable to common stockholders of $913 million, compared with a loss of $1.17 billion during the third quarter of 2025.

Rivian's gross profit, which is closely monitored by investors, was $176 million during the third quarter, up from $24 million a year earlier. Its adjusted earnings before interest, taxes, depreciation and amortization was a loss of $435 million, a $167 million improvement from the same period last year.

The company reported that its software and services segment continues to grow, generating $605 million in revenue and a gross profit of $256 million, a 42% margin. That compares to its automotive operations, which recorded nearly $1.55 billion in revenue and a gross loss of $80 million.

Also on Thursday, Rivian reiterated that it expects the R2, a smaller and less expensive sibling to its current luxury R1S SUV, to have a positive gross margin by the end of the year. CEO RJ Scaringe has said the company needs to scale beyond current R2 production plans of 160,000 units in order to achieve overall profitability.

"That ramp-up is critical for us getting to positive gross margin on R2 and that ultimately will contribute to positive gross margin for the business," Scaringe told CNBC's Phil LeBeau during an interview Thursday. "We're going to see that through the end of the quarter and the unit economics will continue to grow … into Q1 of next year as well."

Rivian on Thursday said its cash, cash equivalents and short-term investments balance was $5.5 billion, up from $5.3 billion to end the second quarter.

#285. Init-6 Venture Telah Biayai Empat Perusahaan Startup hingga Saat Ini, Ini Bisnisnya

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Ade Priyatin | Editor: Avanty Nurdiana

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Perusahaan modal ventura, Init-6 Venture mengungkapkan telah menyalurkan investasi kepada empat startup sepanjang tahun berjalan 2026.

Dari keempat perusahaan tersebut, Init-6 Venture telah mendanai startup d sektor consumer, business-to-business (B2b), dan artificial intelligent (AI).

Founding Partner Init-6 Nugroho Herucahyono jumlah investasi tersebut dipengaruhi kondisi makroekonomi serta daya beli masyarakat yang masih lemah sehingga pendirian startup lebih ditahan.

Baca Juga: Kredit Bank Besar Tumbuh hingga September 2026, Korporasi Jadi Motor Utama

Namun, perusahaan tetap optimistis terhadap peluang investasi modal ventura ke depannya. Optimisme ini tetap dibarengi dengan upaya mencermati perkembangan ekonomi seraya tetap mencari startup yang memiliki prospek bisnis sesuai dengan kriteria investasinya.

"Kami selalu optimistis, tapi semua memang faktornya banyak. Faktor utamanya justru dari makro, dari ekonomi Indonesia sendiri seperti apa," ujarnya saat ditemui setelah acara Media Briefing Init-6 di Jakarta Selatan, Kamis (29/10/2026).

Dari segi nilai investasi, ia menyebut kisaran investasi pada startup tersebut mulai dari sekitar US$ 200.000 hingga US$ 2 juta, dengan fokus pada perusahaan tahap awal atau early stage.

Sementara itu, sektor AI dinilai masih memiliki peluang yang menarik untuk ke depannya. Sayangnya, tantangan yang dihadapi industri modal ventura saat akan masuk ke ini adalah ketersediaan startup pada sektor AI.

Di sisi lain, untuk ke depannya Init-6 Venture mengaku tidak menerapkan target jumlah investasi secara khusus. Ia mengatakan, keputusan pendanaan bergantung pada peluang dan kualitas perusahaan yang dinilai layak untuk didukung.

#142. Instagram head Adam Mosseri warns creators that AI backlash is only going to get worse

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Instagram head Adam Mosseri said he expects anxiety and resentment about artificial intelligence to intensify over the next few years—and that social media platforms may be limited in their ability to control it.

“[The anti-AI backlash] is going to continue to get worse,” he said in a recent appearance on the Colin & Samir podcast. “I think we’re going to see more frustration and anger . . . some of it founded, some of it unfounded.”

While AI anxiety is present across many industries, Mosseri thinks it is most acute in creative fields. Among other issues, creators worry about retaining ownership of their content, whether it can be used to train AI models, and job displacement.

Concerns are especially strong among creators and influencers dependent on social media for income, who now must contend with copycat accounts and AI-generated personas. Mosseri said he has recently received many complaints about AI characters stealing stand-up comedians’ jokes.

He noted that policing this kind of activity can be incredibly difficult. 

Although Meta has long required disclosures for certain AI-generated content, and Instagram announced in August that it would restrict the reach of accounts featuring AI-generated people if they fail to self-identify, rooting out delinquents can be tricky. AI-identification technology is less than perfect and often produces false positives.

“One of the ways to really get a lot of people angry is to label real creators as AI creators by mistake,” Mosseri said.

Instagram is still perfecting how to rank AI-generated posts in users’ feeds and currently ranks AI-generated influencers no differently than humans, he added. 

Although such ranking issues are common whenever a new type of content explodes in popularity, this means human influencers are currently competing on a level playing field with virtual personalities that don’t need salaries, photo shoots, travel, or time off—and that are already eroding users’ trust in the influencer economy. (Mosseri did not comment on the podcast about whether he thinks AI influencers would take sponsorship deals away from human influencers or otherwise impact their incomes.)

Mosseri described understanding public opinion about AI as a crucial aspect of his job, noting that many young people strongly dislike the technology and that they are critical to his company’s future. 

“What I think [the backlash against AI] comes down to, when you really have a heart-to-heart with someone, is a fear of being left behind,” he said. “I want us to embrace [AI] in ways that empower people, so they don’t feel like they’re left behind.”

#273. Kecerdasan Buatan & Teknologi Digital Dorong Inovasi di Sektor Pendidikan dan Budaya

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Ahmad Febrian | Editor: Ahmad Febrian

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Pemanfaatan kecerdasan buatan (AI) dan teknologi digital membuka peluang pengembangan solusi di sektor pendidikan, pelestarian budaya, hingga pengelolaan lingkungan. Pengembangan teknologi ini juga membutuhkan pemahaman terhadap aspek sosial dan perilaku manusia agar inovasi sesuai dengan kebutuhan masyarakat.

Faculty of Humanities Binus University mengembangkan pendekatan tersebut melalui riset digital humanities yang menghubungkan teknologi dengan berbagai bidang ilmu. Fokus kajiannya mencakup pemanfaatan AI, psikologi digital, pendidikan, seni dan budaya, hingga ekonomi politik teknologi.

Dekan Faculty of Humanities Binus University, Elisa Carolina Marion mengatakan, perkembangan teknologi perlu diimbangi pemahaman terhadap kebutuhan manusia dan lingkungan.

"Di tengah perkembangan teknologi yang semakin pesat, inovasi tidak hanya berorientasi pada kemajuan teknologi itu sendiri, tetapi juga pada manusia dan lingkungan,” ujar Elisa.

Baca Juga: Okupansi Gudang Modern Jabodetabek Tembus 97%, Ruang Kosong Makin Terbatas

Penerapan digital humanities terlihat dalam sejumlah proyek. Mulai dari aplikasi puisi dan literasi digital, eksplorasi budaya Nusantara berbasis AI, tur museum virtual, hingga platform pembelajaran bahasa.

General Chair CoDHES 2026, Miranda Paulina Tahalele mengatakan, pengetahuan lokal Indonesia dapat menjadi sumber perspektif untuk pengembangan riset lintas disiplin.

"Kami ingin membuka ruang dialog dan kolaborasi agar pengetahuan tersebut dapat terus dikembangkan melalui penelitian lintas disiplin dan pemanfaatan teknologi digital,” kata Miranda.

Pengembangan digital humanities menjadi bagian dari riset Faculty of Humanities BINUS University selama lima tahun terakhir. Pendekatan ini menghubungkan teknologi digital dengan kajian kemanusiaan, termasuk pendidikan, budaya, dan lingkungan.

#145. Barry Diller is 84 and says he has no plans to retire: 'I have a motor inside me.'

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

He may have run Paramount, built the Fox television network, and just pulled a buyout offer for MGM Resorts, but media and tech executive Barry Diller says he's not done yet.

He has a "motor inside" that just won't stop, Diller said Thursday at Business Insider's The Long Play in New York.

"I'm still curious," he told Peter Kafka, BI's chief correspondent. "If I can be engaged and hopefully be useful, then OK."

"At some point, someone will tell me in some masterful way, either it's over or, in another way, it will be over," he said, alluding to the final bow. "I will either pay attention, or not, and they will drag me out to the heap."

Follow along with our coverage from Business Insider's The Long Play

Diller's desire to keep leading companies means staying on top of major technological changes, such as AI.

"I am, at any point, ready to change my mind based on events of that moment," he said. "I don't think whatever the past process is should educate that," he said.

At People Inc., Diller has overseen multiple strategy shifts. The company is evolving from print to a digital business, and like other publishers, it's had to adjust to significant Google traffic declines.

On Thursday, People Inc. laid off 195 workers, according to a memo from its CEO, Neil Vogel, which Business Insider viewed. The company cut workers from its "print and legacy web businesses," while adding hundreds of staffers in other business areas that it hopes to grow, the memo said.

Diller told Kafka that People's layoffs were a sign that its print business was in a "steady kind of decline," and that it was shifting its workforce to focus on new business priorities.

Add BI in Google so our reporting is easier to find when you’re searching for what matters.

Diller's time isn't solely focused on business: He's also worked on various public-interest projects, such as developing New York's High Line and Little Island alongside his wife, Diane von Fürstenberg.

On Thursday, he teased a coming project for Los Angeles, too, but wouldn't reveal details. Despite taking joy in seeing satisfied attendees at Little Island on the Hudson River, Diller said he's not focused on his public legacy.

"I don't do legacy," he told Kafka. "If you say legacy, it kind of means all you're doing is looking backward, and that's just boring to me."

#300. Kredit Bank Besar Tumbuh hingga September 2026, Korporasi Jadi Motor Utama

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Selvi Mayasari | Editor: Handoyo

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Segmen korporasi menjadi pendorong utama pertumbuhan kredit perbankan nasional hingga September 2026. Sejumlah bank besar mencatatkan peningkatan penyaluran pembiayaan kepada dunia usaha, seiring kebutuhan modal kerja dan investasi yang terus berkembang.

Berdasarkan data Otoritas Jasa Keuangan (OJK), pertumbuhan kredit perbankan pada Agustus 2026 mencapai 13,65% secara tahunan atau year on year (YoY), dengan nilai outstanding sebesar Rp 9.178 triliun. Angka tersebut meningkat dibandingkan pertumbuhan pada Juli 2026 sebesar 13,58% YoY.

Berdasarkan jenis penggunaan, kredit investasi mencatatkan pertumbuhan tertinggi sebesar 25,11% YoY, diikuti kredit modal kerja sebesar 11,45% YoY dan kredit konsumsi sebesar 5,07% YoY.

Dari kategori debitur, kredit korporasi tumbuh paling tinggi sebesar 22,18% YoY. Sementara itu, kredit usaha mikro, kecil, dan menengah (UMKM) masih tumbuh positif sebesar 2,32% YoY. Berdasarkan kepemilikan, kredit bank badan usaha milik negara (BUMN) mencatatkan pertumbuhan tertinggi sebesar 16,41% YoY.

Baca Juga: Penurunan Beban Bunga Belum Mampu Dongkrak Kinerja Bank Swasta di Kuartal III-2026 Kredit korporasi BCA tumbuh 20,9%

Salah satu bank yang mencatatkan ekspansi kredit adalah PT Bank Central Asia Tbk (BBCA). Hingga September 2026, BCA membukukan penyaluran kredit sebesar Rp 1.063,1 triliun, tumbuh 12,6% YoY.

Wakil Presiden Direktur BCA John Kosasih mengatakan, peningkatan permintaan kredit antara lain didorong oleh persiapan pelaku usaha dalam menghadapi periode dengan aktivitas bisnis yang lebih tinggi.

Menurut John, perusahaan biasanya mulai melakukan pengadaan bahan baku dan mempersiapkan produksi sekitar empat hingga lima bulan sebelum memasuki periode permintaan tinggi, seperti Natal dan Tahun Baru, Tahun Baru Imlek, serta Lebaran.

“Business season ini menjelang Natal, Tahun Baru, Chinese New Year dan juga Lebaran. Biasanya empat sampai lima bulan sebelumnya, ini tentu saja membutuhkan procurement dan production early untuk mengantisipasi,” ujar John dalam pemaparan kinerja BCA, Selasa (27/10/2026).

Selain faktor musiman, BCA mencatat tingkat penggunaan fasilitas kredit nasabah berada di kisaran 65%–66%. Kebutuhan modal kerja untuk mengantisipasi peningkatan aktivitas usaha turut mendorong pemanfaatan fasilitas kredit tersebut.

Berdasarkan segmen bisnis, kredit korporasi BCA mencapai Rp 528,1 triliun, tumbuh 20,9% YoY. Pertumbuhan tersebut antara lain ditopang sektor perkebunan, pertanian, infrastruktur, transportasi, energi, pembangkit listrik, serta teknologi dan infrastruktur digital.

Kredit segmen usaha kecil dan menengah (UKM) juga meningkat 15,4% YoY menjadi Rp 149,2 triliun. Sejumlah sektor yang menopang pertumbuhan ini meliputi perdagangan grosir, ritel dan distribusi, jasa bisnis, transportasi dan logistik, serta industri makanan dan minuman.

Baca Juga: Transaksi wondr by BNI Tembus Rp 1.774 Triliun, Naik 25% hingga September 2026

Adapun kredit komersial BCA tumbuh 7,8% YoY menjadi Rp 154,1 triliun. Sementara itu, pembiayaan syariah meningkat 14,3% YoY menjadi Rp 14 triliun.

Di sisi lain, kredit konsumer BCA mengalami kontraksi 2,2% YoY menjadi Rp 218,7 triliun. Penurunan tersebut terutama dipengaruhi kredit kendaraan bermotor yang merosot 20,6% YoY menjadi Rp 48,7 triliun.

Meski demikian, kredit pemilikan rumah (KPR) masih tumbuh 3,6% YoY menjadi Rp 143,7 triliun. Personal loan juga mencatatkan pertumbuhan 11,5% menjadi Rp 26,3 triliun.

Seiring perkembangan permintaan pembiayaan, BCA memperkirakan pertumbuhan kredit sepanjang 2026 mencapai 10%–12%. Proyeksi tersebut lebih tinggi dibandingkan target sebelumnya dalam Rencana Bisnis Bank (RBB) sebesar 8%–10%. Kredit korporasi Bank Mandiri tembus Rp 700 triliun

Pertumbuhan kredit dengan dukungan segmen korporasi juga terlihat pada PT Bank Mandiri (Persero) Tbk (BMRI). Secara bank only, penyaluran kredit Bank Mandiri mencapai Rp 1.605,8 triliun per September 2026, tumbuh 16% YoY.

Kredit korporasi menjadi salah satu penopang utama dengan nilai penyaluran mencapai Rp 700 triliun, meningkat 22% YoY. Selain memperkuat pembiayaan kepada perusahaan, Bank Mandiri mengembangkan pendekatan berbasis ekosistem dengan menghubungkan kebutuhan pembiayaan perusahaan dengan pelaku usaha dan nasabah individu dalam rantai bisnisnya.

Direktur Operations Bank Mandiri Timothy Utama menjelaskan, keterhubungan layanan transaksi dan pembiayaan membantu memenuhi kebutuhan nasabah di dalam ekosistem tersebut.

Hingga September 2026, kredit ekosistem turunan yang mencakup segmen UKM, mikro, dan konsumer mencapai Rp 174 triliun, tumbuh 9% YoY.

Pada segmen mikro, kredit nonsubsidi Bank Mandiri tercatat sebesar Rp 31,2 triliun, meningkat 12,9% YoY. Pembiayaan tersebut telah menjangkau 37 provinsi di Indonesia.

Ekspansi kredit Bank Mandiri juga tercermin di sejumlah wilayah. Secara bank only, kredit di Bali dan Nusa Tenggara tumbuh 10,3% YoY per September 2026. Sementara itu, kredit di Jawa dan Daerah Istimewa Yogyakarta meningkat 5,97%, sedangkan Sumatera tumbuh 5,09%.

“Kami menghubungkan layanan bagi perusahaan, pelaku UMKM, dan nasabah individu agar kebutuhan transaksi serta pembiayaan mereka dapat terlayani dengan lebih baik,” ujar Timothy.

Baca Juga: Cermati Strategi Pencadangan Perbankan di Kuartal III-2026 Kredit BNI tumbuh 22,2% hingga September 2026

PT Bank Negara Indonesia Tbk (BBNI) juga mencatatkan pertumbuhan kredit yang kuat. Hingga September 2026, kredit konsolidasian BNI mencapai Rp 992,5 triliun, meningkat 22,2% YoY.

Pertumbuhan tersebut terutama ditopang segmen wholesale banking yang meningkat 30,3% YoY menjadi Rp 673,8 triliun.

Di dalam segmen tersebut, kredit enterprise mencatatkan pertumbuhan tertinggi sebesar 105,6% YoY menjadi Rp 136,3 triliun. Sementara itu, kredit korporasi tumbuh 19,3% YoY menjadi Rp 537,5 triliun.

Pada segmen business banking, kredit komersial meningkat 8,3% YoY menjadi Rp 57,6 triliun. Kredit segmen small juga tumbuh 6,1% menjadi Rp 78,9 triliun.

Kredit konsumer BNI turut mencatatkan pertumbuhan sebesar 9% YoY menjadi Rp 163,8 triliun. Kinerja tersebut ditopang KPR yang meningkat 9,9% menjadi Rp 76,7 triliun serta personal loan yang tumbuh 8,8% menjadi Rp 66,8 triliun.

Direktur Finance & Strategy BNI Hussein Paolo Kartadjoemena mengatakan, perseroan melakukan ekspansi secara selektif pada seluruh segmen dengan menerapkan standar analisis kelayakan kredit yang disiplin.

Strategi tersebut mendorong pangsa pasar kredit BNI meningkat 113 basis poin secara tahunan menjadi 10,6%.

Memasuki kuartal IV-2026, BNI akan melanjutkan penguatan pendanaan berbasis transaksi, pertumbuhan kredit secara selektif, pengelolaan risiko, serta transformasi digital dan jaringan. Perseroan menargetkan pertumbuhan kredit sepanjang 2026 berada di kisaran 10%–13% YoY. Kredit korporasi CIMB Niaga tumbuh 15,1%

Sementara itu, PT Bank CIMB Niaga Tbk (BNGA) membukukan kredit sebesar Rp 242,58 triliun per September 2026, tumbuh 6,7% YoY.

Kredit korporasi menjadi penopang utama pertumbuhan dengan penyaluran mencapai Rp 102,79 triliun, meningkat 15,1% YoY.

Baca Juga: Saham Big Banks Kompak Melemah (29/10), Asing Ramai-ramai Jual Saham, BBCA Terparah

Kredit komersial CIMB Niaga juga tumbuh 6,4% YoY menjadi Rp 36,63 triliun. Adapun kredit segmen usaha menengah dan kecil (SME) meningkat 1,9% menjadi Rp 27,01 triliun.

Sebaliknya, kredit konsumer turun 1,3% YoY menjadi Rp 76,15 triliun. Pembiayaan Unit Usaha Syariah juga mengalami kontraksi 13,2% YoY menjadi Rp 49,44 triliun.

Presiden Direktur CIMB Niaga Lani Darmawan mengatakan, target pertumbuhan kredit perseroan pada 2026 dalam RBB masih berada di kisaran satu digit.

Menurut dia, pertumbuhan kredit konsumer ritel yang belum kuat turut memengaruhi kinerja penyaluran kredit CIMB Niaga. Hal ini mengingat segmen tersebut memiliki porsi besar dalam portofolio kredit perseroan.

Untuk tahun ini, CIMB Niaga memperkirakan kredit nonritel, terutama segmen korporasi, akan mencatatkan pertumbuhan paling tinggi.

Selain mendorong penyaluran kredit, perseroan juga berfokus pada penguatan kualitas aset, pertumbuhan kredit secara selektif, optimalisasi komposisi pendanaan, peningkatan pendapatan berbasis komisi, serta efisiensi biaya.

#305. PANDI Gandeng Privy Mengembangkan Teknologi Verifikasi Identitas Digital

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Ahmad Febrian | Editor: Ahmad Febrian

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Kebutuhan verifikasi identitas dan keamanan data membuka ruang pengembangan teknologi kepercayaan digital di Indonesia.

Pengelola Nama Domain Internet Indonesia (PANDI) menjajaki kerja sama dengan PT Privy Identitas Digital (Privy) untuk mengembangkan teknologi identitas digital. Langkah ini ekaligus menggandeng mitra nasional dan internasional guna memperkuat infrastruktur internet.

Kerjasama PANDI dan Privy mencakup riset dan pengembangan verifiable credential (VC) serta tanda tangan elektronik (TTE). Kedua pihak juga akan menjajaki proyek percontohan untuk meningkatkan efisiensi verifikasi identitas dan mengurangi kebutuhan penyimpanan salinan dokumen pribadi yang berisiko bocor atau disalahgunakan.

Sebagai Penyelenggara Sertifikasi Elektronik (PSrE), Privy diharapkan mendukung pengembangan verifikasi identitas dan autentikasi dokumen elektronik sesuai ketentuan hukum.

Ketua Dewan Pengurus PANDI, Isnawan mengatakan, pengembangan identitas digital diperlukan untuk meningkatkan keamanan aktivitas internet sekaligus melindungi data pengguna, termasuk dalam ekosistem domain .id.

Sementara CEO & Founder Privy, Marshall Pribadi menilai, mekanisme verifikasi identitas dan keaslian dokumen semakin penting di tengah meningkatnya risiko penipuan digital. Infrastruktur kepercayaan digital dibutuhkan agar pengguna tidak hanya mengandalkan kewaspadaan saat bertransaksi atau berkomunikasi secara daring.

Baca Juga: TKDN Kendaraan Listrik Naik Jadi 60% pada 2027, Industri Baterai Didorong

Di luar identitas digital, PANDI menggandeng  National Internet Exchange of India (NIXI) untuk pertukaran pengetahuan dalam pengelolaan registri domain, pengembangan IPv6, serta peningkatan keamanan internet melalui RPKI, DMARC, dan DNSSEC.

"Kolaborasi ini juga mencakup peningkatan kapasitas sumber daya manusia dan tata kelola internet di kawasan Asia-Pasifik," kata Isnawan, Rabu (28/10).

PANDI juga menggandeng Asosiasi Penyelenggara Jasa Internet Indonesia (APJII) memperkuat infrastruktur domain name system (DNS) nasional.

Kerjasama ini mencakup pengembangan DNS Resolver Indonesia, DNS Authoritative .id, penempatan Root Server serta peningkatan keamanan siber melalui pemanfaatan jaringan Indonesia Internet Exchange (IIX) di Jakarta, Balikpapan, dan Bandung.

Ketiga kerja sama tersebut mencakup pengembangan teknologi identitas digital, penguatan infrastruktur DNS, dan perluasan kolaborasi internasional. Bagi PANDI, langkah ini menjadi bagian dari pengembangan ekosistem domain .id dan peningkatan keandalan infrastruktur internet Indonesia.

#29. Burnham 'ready to support' Poundland staff as retailer faces collapse

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Andy Burnham has said the government is "ready to support" Poundland staff as the retailer teeters on the brink of collapse.

The prime minister called for answers over why an offer to buy the business from its current owners, which would have protected jobs, was rejected.

"This is a deeply worrying time for Poundland staff and their families," Burnham told the BBC.

The discount chain is teetering on the edge after its US-based owners Gordon Brothers cut off a vital funding supply and rejected a takeover offer from Irish firm Martin Property Group (MPG).

Burnham said: "We want to see jobs protected. If there was a potential owner to avoid this that hasn't been accepted, then the Business Secretary and I want to know why that is the case.

"We stand ready to support the workforce however we can."

The retailer's bosses have filed a notice of intention to appoint administrators, giving the business 10 days to find a rescue deal.

The move comes after managing director Barry Williams told its 12,000 staff the chain's US owner had cut off a vital supply of funding despite Poundland's strong sales.

It is understood Gordon Brothers wants to break it up, with rivals Poundstretcher, B&M and Lidl interested in potentially buying up its stores.

Business Secretary Jonathan Reynolds earlier said he was "very much concerned" by the uncertainty clouding Poundland's future.

Gordon Brothers bought Poundland, which had been struggling, from the Polish retail group Pepco last year for a nominal Â£1. A little over a year later, it put the business up for sale.

Despite an offer on the table for the business, Poundland's managers and owners have not been able to agree on a buyer, which has left it on the brink of collapse.

It could still be purchased out of administration in a so-called pre-pack deal, but the most likely option is for it to be broken up and for Poundland's stores to be sold off.

Poundland was founded in 1990 with a store in Burton-upon-Trent, and has around 20 million customers per year.

It has across its 600 stores in the UK and Ireland, where it trades as Dealz.

It sells low-cost everyday products, general merchandise and fashion on local high streets.

The curious case of why Poundland is struggling during a cost-of-living crisis

Poundland in rescue talks as owners seek buyer

A Gordon Brothers insider said breaking the firm up would allow creditors to recover more money, and that this process would ultimately offer the best outcome for staff. The US investment firm's position is that current employees may be able to get employment with new store owners.

If this type of insolvency happens, it would let interested parties firm up offers.

These include Poundstretcher owner, Fortress, which according to one insider is interested in taking on between 200 and 250 stores.

Another large retailer is understood to be interested in buying up more than 100 other sites.

More than half a dozen retailers, include Iceland, Lidl, and B&M, have expressed interest in Poundland's store estate.

Bally Auluk, a national officer at the Usdaw shopworkers union, said this week had been "incredibly distressing" for Poundland staff.

He claimed staff were having to "watch from the sidelines as a multi-billion pound company jeopardises their livelihoods as it tries to extract Â£30m from a business it bought for Â£1".

He said Williams had "reluctantly" kicked off the administration process, to give the business time to find a rescue deal, adding that Usdaw was urging Gordon Brothers to reconsider.

Staff chanted "Save our Jobs" outside Poundland's customer support centre in Walsall on Thursday afternoon.

The uncertainty surrounding Poundland's future has left some staff fearful about the future of their jobs.

Staff at Poundland's customer support centre in Walsall, some dressed as the Poundland "Pound Hound" mascot, held a protest on Thursday.

One employee said they were trying to "save our jobs".

There is also a split between the owners and the management running Poundland, who described the situation as "unexpected, unwanted and wholly avoidable".

"As you all know, our sales are strong and our recovery is taking root. This business simply does not deserve to go into any kind of administration," the chain's managing director Williams said in a message to staff this week.

He revealed that Gordon Brothers had limited Poundland's access to funds which help it run its day-to-day business.

"I'm disappointed to have to tell you that Gordon Brothers have given instructions that restricts our access to funds that we would, in normal circumstances expect to access fully under our existing loan agreement," he added.

In the memo, seen by the BBC, Williams said the offer from Martin Property Group was a "fully funded, ready to go bid that would have kept the business intact and able to continue the remarkable progress we've made".

Three independent directors who were appointed earlier this month to help oversee a sale resigned with immediate effect after disagreeing with Gordon Brothers' decision not to accept MPG's solvent offer.

#152. Gusto Empowers Accounting Firms with AI-Driven Guidance Solutions

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Gusto has rolled out a new product designed to enhance the operational efficiency of accounting firms while strengthening their advisory capabilities. The latest offering, dubbed Gusto Pro, aims to empower accountants with tools that leverage artificial intelligence for greater insights and streamlined processes. For small business owners, this development could represent a significant shift in how they access and utilize accounting services, ultimately impacting their bottom line and operational strategies.

Gusto Pro is built with a deep focus on guidance, a crucial aspect for small business owners who rely heavily on their accountants not just for bookkeeping but also for strategic financial advice. According to Bridgette Haymaker, Gusto’s representative, the platform is tailored to help accountants “build what’s next,” providing them with sophisticated tools to support small businesses in navigating their financial landscapes.

One of the standout features of Gusto Pro is its AI-driven analytics, which can deliver real-time insights that accountants can share with their clients. These insights help small business owners identify cost-saving opportunities and optimize cash flow management—both critical areas for businesses aiming to enhance profitability. With the growing need for financial data transparency, small businesses need reliable tools to make educated decisions. Gusto Pro makes this possible by converting complex data into digestible information that can drive strategic planning.

Additionally, Gusto Pro simplifies compliance and payroll processes, which are often challenging for small business owners. By automating these tedious tasks, the platform allows accountants to spend more time on strategic initiatives rather than administrative duties. As a result, businesses can experience quicker turnaround times on their financial reporting and advisory services. The goal here is not only to improve efficiency but also to enhance the quality of service that small businesses receive.

Gusto Pro’s AI capabilities also promise to minimize human error, a common issue that can become costly for small businesses. Gusto understands that maintaining accurate financial records is paramount, particularly in an era where even minor discrepancies can lead to significant repercussions. By embedding AI in the accounting process, Gusto Pro gives small businesses the peace of mind that their financial data is less prone to mistakes.

However, while the benefits are compelling, small business owners may want to consider potential challenges before integrating new technology like Gusto Pro into their practices. Transitioning to any new system often presents learning curves that can disrupt existing workflows. Owners should be prepared for some initial resistance from staff who may need time to adapt to the new platform. Furthermore, they should evaluate their current infrastructure to ensure compatibility with Gusto Pro’s features.

Cost is another factor that small business owners will want to weigh. Investing in advanced technology can create budgetary strains, particularly for startups or those operating on tight margins. Gusto Pro aims to be scalable, but business owners will need to assess whether the financial commitment aligns with their long-term goals.

Moreover, small businesses must also stay updated on regulatory changes that could affect how they use technology in their accounting processes. Gusto assures users that they will continue to evolve in response to these changes, but owners still bear the responsibility of ensuring their practices remain compliant with local, state, and federal regulations.

For those accounting firms that successfully adopt Gusto Pro, the rewards may extend beyond increased efficiency and improved client service. The platform’s tools can enhance the firm’s ability to act as a trusted advisor, making them invaluable to small business clients facing a rapidly changing economic landscape. As accounts become more advisory-focused, small business owners could experience a greater level of customized service tailored to their unique needs.

Overall, Gusto Pro represents a significant advancement in accounting technology that could reshape how small businesses engage with financial professionals. By blending AI with actionable insights, the platform positions itself as a critical resource for accountants looking to provide enhanced value to their clients. As small businesses continue to navigate the complexities of modern finance, Gusto Pro could emerge as a cornerstone of their operational strategy.

For a deeper dive into Gusto Pro’s capabilities, you can read the original post here.

#151. More companies like Microsoft are using buyouts. Should you take one?

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Microsoft is trying to cull its workforce by dangling an offer that might be hard for some employees to refuse. This week, the tech giant announced voluntary buyouts for 60,000 people, which is more than a quarter of its workforce. 

Leadership has framed this as a way for employees to have more agency over their future at the company. “The Voluntary Exit Offer reflects an important part of how we’re evolving our approach to change: creating greater flexibility and, where possible, more ways for employees to shape their next chapter,” chief people officer Amy Coleman wrote in a blog post. For employees who were already entertaining the idea of an outside opportunity, she said this could present “a new choice to make that transition now with support.”

It’s not the first time Microsoft has offered buyouts to trim its head count. Earlier this year, the company used another euphemism to nudge thousands of employees to leave. The “Voluntary Retirement Program” was intended for older, more senior employees who may have been planning to retire soon; according to The Wall Street Journal, Microsoft extended offers to about 8,600 employees and more than 30% accepted.

Voluntary buyouts have become an attractive option for companies looking to cut jobs and reduce costs without resorting to explicit layoffs, which draw headlines and heightened scrutiny. Mass layoffs can also significantly impact morale, something major employers like Microsoft are all too familiar with. Just this year, Microsoft faced its share of criticism for eliminating 3,200 jobs in its Xbox division, targeting some veteran staffers who had spent decades there; the company also made thousands of cuts across other departments. 

There are all kinds of euphemistic terms for this strategy, which has become a more popular approach to workforce reductions in corporate America. Just this week, Skydance—the new company formed from the merger between Paramount and Warner Bros. Discovery—proposed buyouts to a number of employees (in addition to adopting a five-day return-to-office mandate that had already been implemented at Paramount). Companies like Google and Verizon have also deployed voluntary buyouts to soften the blow when they need to become leaner. 

As the public backlash over AI intensifies, companies may want to avoid the negative attention they attract when they make sweeping changes to their workforce. But they also run the risk of losing top talent when they make buyout offers—and it isn’t always clear how many people will choose to leave. (According to The Wall Street Journal, Microsoft anticipates that a much smaller share of people eligible for buyouts will accept this time, compared with the 30% who took the offer earlier this year.)

For certain employees, a voluntary exit can, in fact, present an opportunity. But it’s not always clear when it makes sense to take a buyout. 

As Fast Company has reported, companies sometimes target highly paid, experienced employees in the hopes of replacing them with cheaper labor. When presented with a buyout offer, experts say it’s important to think carefully about what it could mean for your career and where you see yourself next.

“The best-case scenario for a voluntary layoff is either stepping straight into retirement or having a new job already lined up,” writes Fast Company contributor Emily Guy Birken. “Then your severance package becomes an unexpected pot of gold in the path you were already walking. Unfortunately, most of us aren’t that lucky.” 

If you weren’t planning to leave prior to the buyout offer, it can be helpful to assess your future career prospects. Since companies usually give employees some time to make a decision about a buyout, you could put out feelers to get a sense of what the job market is like. A generous severance offer may be alluring, but it also means you’ll have to find a new job. 

If the severance offer is not compelling enough to consider, Birken points out that you’re in a position to try and negotiate a better package. “Learning that your employer is offering voluntary layoffs is stressful, but it doesn’t mean the company has all the cards,” she writes. “The organization wants something—a reduction in force—which means you have leverage.” 

The most important consideration, however, may be whether you think you’ll eventually lose your job either way, since voluntary exit programs can also just be a sign that layoffs are on the horizon. Sometimes a buyout offer is primarily a way to manage optics and give employees a false sense of control—only for a company to turn around and fire them a few months down the road.

#155. This chart shows how much Google's head count surged in the AI boom

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

While AI may be driving some companies to slash their head count, Google's is soaring.

When Google reported its Q3 earnings on Wednesday, it said it had 204,757 employees for the quarter ending September 2026. That means Google added roughly 10,000 people to the payroll since the end of March.

The uptick in head count runs counter to another trend across the tech industry, where some of Google's peers are slashing their workforces. Meta has been on a cost-cutting odyssey for several years, while Microsoft recently offered voluntary buyouts to 60,000 employees.

Some of Google's head count growth can be attributed to mergers and acquisitions. On March 11, Google said it had completed its acquisition of cybersecurity firm Wiz, which had roughly 2,000 employees.

In January 2023, Google announced it would slash roughly 12,000 roles — around 6% of the workforce at the time — and has since made several smaller rounds of layoffs, Business Insider has reported.

While the head count has grown somewhat steadily since Q2 2024, it has accelerated since the end of 2025. Google also reported Wednesday that its quarterly revenue grew 25% from this period last year.

#222. Rare Stanley Druckenmiller post sends crypto-linked stock surging

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

The post appeared seemingly out of nowhere. Stanley Druckenmiller, the legendary hedge fund manager who brought the Bank of England to its knees in the 1990s, and whose annual returns are the envy of Wall Street, posted a simple message on an otherwise sparse X feed announcing his involvement with a new type of decentralized exchange. The post caused bedlam on social media and moved shares of a leading crypto treasury company and its token.

TradeXYZ, the primary developer of perpetual futures, or "perps" markets, on exchange Hyperliquid, announced a partnership Thursday with Intercontinental Exchange (ICE) to bring its popular perpetual futures onshore into the U.S.

Shortly after the two said they are seeking regulatory approvals, Druckenmiller said on X that he is "excited to be an investor in trade(xyz)," and that he is "looking forward to to using their innovative product to enhance my investment process." Representatives for Druckenmiller confirmed the statement to the Financial Times.

Options traders pounced on the closest publicly-traded play to the news: Hyperliquid Strategies (PURR), the company that invests in the HYPE crypto token that powers the Hyperliquid exchange.

More than 111,000 options traded on Hyperliquid Strategies Thursday, more than six times the 30-day average, as underlying shares surged 19% to the highest in three weeks. The flows leaned bullish, with almost 86,000 calls and under 27,000 puts trading. Almost four times as many calls were likely initiated by buyers than puts, and of the top 10 trades, only one involved put-buying, Cboe LiveVol dat show.

"We are thrilled to see ICE partnering with Trade.XYZ to bring the markets and liquidity of Hyperliquid to American investors," David Schamis, CEO of Hyperliquid Strategies, said in a text. "ICE is a cornerstone of traditional finance and the ideal partner."

Schamis added that the deal shows demand for perp-futures trading and is a step forward toward regulated trading of the asset class.

In August, President Donald Trump said in a surprise comment to reporters that regulators were working to bring the exchange onshore.

The most popular trades by volume in PURR were selling the 13-strike put expiring Dec. 18, and buying the 13-strike call expiring Nov. 6. Taken together, the two resemble a "risk reversal" strategy, a bullish trade looking for a short-term continuation of the rally, paired with long-term confidence the stock won't be below $13 by year-end.

#312. Mengejar Efisiensi Logistik Lewat Integrasi Tol

📅 Oct 29, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Reporter: Dina Mirayanti Hutauruk | Editor: Dina Hutauruk

KONTAN.CO.ID - JAKARTA. Integrasi jalan tol di wilayah Jabodetabek perlu didukung percepatan regulasi dan kebijakan tarif yang sesuai dengan fungsi setiap ruas, khususnya untuk mendukung kelancaran angkutan logistik. Integrasi itu diharapkan dapat mendistribusikan lalu lintas secara lebih merata serta meningkatkan efisiensi konektivitas antara kawasan industri dan pelabuhan.

Hanya saja, keberhasilannya dinilai akan bergantung pada keterjangkauan tarif, kecukupan biaya operasional pengemudi, kesiapan layanan, dan kejelasan informasi agar penggunaan jalan tol memberikan manfaat nyata bagi pelaku logistik dan perekonomian nasional.

Anggota Badan Pengatur Jalan Tol (BPJT) Kementerian Pekerjaan Umum, Sony Sulaksono Wibowo, mengatakan pemerintah tengah mematangkan regulasi integrasi jalan tol. Harmonisasi rancangan peraturan menteri ditargetkan rampung dalam waktu dekat sebelum ditandatangani Menteri Pekerjaan Umum.

Ia bilang, regulasi ini menjadi landasan pelaksanaan integrasi untuk meratakan distribusi kendaraan dan mengoptimalkan pemanfaatan ruas tol yang saling terhubung. “Harapannya, harmonisasi segera rampung dan peraturan menteri dapat segera ditandatangani agar integrasi jalan tol bisa segera dilaksanakan,” ujar Sony dalam forum diskusi bertajuk “ Rencana Integrasi Jalan Tol Wilayah Jabodetabek” dikutip Kamis (29/10/2026).

Baca Juga: JSMR Perkuat Layanan Jalan Tol, Integrasikan AI hingga Aplikasi Travoy

Salah satu koridor yang disiapkan untuk integrasi adalah Tol Cibitung–Cilincing, Jakarta–Cikampek, dan Tol Akses Tanjung Priok (ATP). Penyelarasan tarif di ketiga ruas tersebut diharapkan memperlancar arus barang dari kawasan industri menuju Pelabuhan Tanjung Priok. Integrasi ini juga diarahkan untuk mengoptimalkan konektivitas jaringan tol sekaligus mendukung efisiensi distribusi logistik.

Dalam kesempatan yang sama, Anggota Badan Pengatur Jalan Tol (BPJT) Kementerian Pekerjaan Umum, Sony Sulaksono Wibowo, mengatakan pemerintah tengah mematangkan regulasi integrasi jalan tol.  Harmonisasi rancangan peraturan menteri ditargetkan rampung dalam waktu dekat sebelum ditandatangani Menteri Pekerjaan Umum.

Integrasi ini bertujuan meratakan distribusi kendaraan dan mengoptimalkan pemanfaatan jaringan tol. Salah satu koridor yang disiapkan mencakup Tol Cibitung–Cilincing, Jakarta–Cikampek, dan Tol Akses Tanjung Priok (ATP). Penyelarasan tarif di koridor tersebut diharapkan memperlancar arus barang dari kawasan industri menuju Pelabuhan Tanjung Priok.

Namun, efektivitas integrasi bergantung pada skema tarif yang sesuai dengan fungsi ruas dan kemampuan pengguna. Direktur Eksekutif Pusat Kajian Infrastruktur Strategis (PUKIS), M. M. Gibran Sesunan, menilai tarif tol perlu mempertimbangkan peruntukan ruas, karakteristik lalu lintas, serta manfaat ekonomi dan sosial. “Tarif yang lebih terjangkau diperlukan agar angkutan barang terdorong menggunakan jalur yang dibangun untuk mendukung logistik,” ujarnya.

Baca Juga: BPJT Catat Ratusan Ribu Kendaraan ODOL Terdeteksi di Jalan Tol

Perwakilan DPW Asosiasi Logistik dan Forwarder Indonesia (ALFI) Jakarta, Ahmad Sugiono, menambahkan bahwa penghematan waktu tempuh belum tentu membuat pengemudi beralih ke tol jika biaya perjalanan meningkat. Keputusan memilih rute juga dipengaruhi kemacetan akses, fleksibilitas perjalanan, frekuensi angkutan, dan peluang memperoleh muatan balik.

Senada, Senior Consultant Supply Chain Indonesia, Sugi Purnoto, menyoroti besarnya porsi tarif tol terhadap uang jalan pengemudi. Dalam simulasinya, biaya tol pada jalur eksisting menyerap sekitar 7%–14% uang jalan. Sementara itu, pada skenario penggunaan ruas tol khusus logistik, porsinya diperkirakan mencapai 28%–40%. Kenaikan tersebut berpotensi menekan alokasi biaya operasional lainnya.

Lebih lanjuta, Sony menjelaskan, Rencana Umum Jaringan Jalan Tol Nasional 2020–2040 telah mengarahkan pembangunan tol sebagai satu jaringan. Namun, perbedaan tahapan pembangunan, keterlambatan ruas pendukung, dan disparitas tarif membuat sebagian ruas padat, sedangkan jalur alternatif belum optimal dimanfaatkan.

Baca Juga: Akses Banten Selatan Makin Terbuka, Tol Rangkasbitung-Cileles Masuk ULFO

Karena itu, integrasi perlu disertai penyelarasan tarif, kesiapan layanan, dan informasi yang jelas. Dukungan responden survei FKBI dapat menjadi masukan bagi penyelesaian regulasi dan kajian integrasi agar konektivitas tol tidak hanya memperlancar lalu lintas, tetapi juga meningkatkan efisiensi biaya logistik.

FGD “Rencana Integrasi Jalan Tol Wilayah Jabodetabek” dislenggarakan oleh Forum Konsumen Berdaya Indonesia (FKBI).  Survei daring FKBI  menunjukkan bahwa 74,1% responden mendukung integrasi tarif jalan tol. Temuan tersebut memperlihatkan penerimaan yang cukup kuat di kalangan responden survei dari kelompok logistik dan komuter rutin pengguna akses tol.
